"use strict";
/* =====================================================================
   HEAT — digital adaptation for personal play.
   Core round structure per the published rules; original visuals.
   ===================================================================== */


  


  
/* (TRACKS is declared above; every board registers itself via defineTrack.) */
let TRACK = TRACKS.pukekohe;   // selected track (chosen on the track-select screen)
let S = TRACK.spaces;

/* =====================================================================
   TRACK FEATURES — gravel Spots and tunnel Spaces (Heavy Rain expansion
   boards, exported straight from the HEAT track builder / checker).

   gravel : { inner:[…space indices…], outer:[…] }
            A Space has two Spots. Gravel can cover the Spot nearest the
            Race Line (inner = spot 0), the outside one (outer = spot 1),
            or both. Rule: at the end of your turn (step 9), if your car
            sits on a gravel Spot you pay 1 Heat if you have any in the
            Engine — with an empty engine the effect is simply ignored.

   tunnels: [ {from, to} … ]  inclusive runs of Spaces, wrap-aware
            (from > to means the run crosses the start line). Rule: while
            a car is on a tunnel Space its player cannot discard from
            hand — this beats any effect that would otherwise let them
            (step 8, Reduce Stress upgrades, etc). Cooldown (the card
            goes to the Engine) and Scrap (cards come off the top of the
            draw deck) are NOT discards, so both stay legal in a tunnel.
   ===================================================================== */
/* tolerate the two shapes the builder has used: {inner,outer} or a bare list */
function gravelLists(t){
  const g = (t||TRACK).gravel;
  if(!g) return {inner:[], outer:[]};
  if(Array.isArray(g)) return {inner:g, outer:[]};
  return {inner:Array.isArray(g.inner)?g.inner:[], outer:Array.isArray(g.outer)?g.outer:[]};
}
function trackHasGravel(t){ const g=gravelLists(t); return g.inner.length>0 || g.outer.length>0; }
function trackHasTunnels(t){ const r=(t||TRACK).tunnels; return Array.isArray(r) && r.length>0; }
/* is board space i inside any tunnel run? (runs may wrap past space 0) */
function inTunnelSpace(i, t){
  for(const r of (t||TRACK).tunnels||[]){
    if(r===null || r===undefined) continue;
    if(runSpaces(r, t).indexOf(i) >= 0) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
/* live-position checks for a car (only meaningful before it crosses the flag) */
function inTunnelNow(p){ return !p.finished && !finishedLine(p) && inTunnelSpace(phys(p.total)); }
function onGravel(p){
  if(p.finished || finishedLine(p)) return false;
  const g=gravelLists(), i=phys(p.total);
  return (p.spot===0 ? g.inner : g.outer).includes(i);
}
/* step 9: the gravel toll — shared by the human and the simulated drivers */
function applyGravel(p){
  if(!trackHasGravel(TRACK) || !onGravel(p)) return;
  const who = p.isBot ? p.name : (G.hotseat ? p.name : "You");
  if(p.engine>0){
    payHeat(p,1);
    log(`${who} end${p.isBot||G.hotseat?"s":""} the turn on gravel — 1 Heat rattles out of the Engine.`,"warn");
    if(!p.isBot) toast("Gravel! −1 Heat","hot");
  }else{
    log(`${who} end${p.isBot||G.hotseat?"s":""} the turn on gravel, but the Engine is empty — no Heat to pay, effect ignored.`);
  }
}

   RIVAL_CARS: every single race runs a full 10-car grid (you + 9 rivals);
   BOT_POOL below holds exactly 9 drivers, so the pack is always the same.
   Rival AI is always the simulated "Full drivers" model — the Legends module
   code below is kept intact (used by the Legend pace profile and championship). */
const RIVAL_CARS = 9;
const RIVAL_AI   = "sim";
/* laps for the Grand Prix option come from the circuit's own metadata */
/* Grand Prix distance = the circuit's own lap count. A point-to-point board
   is a single run, so there is only ever 1 "lap" to offer. */
const gpLaps = () => layoutOf(TRACK)==="open" ? 1 : Math.max(1, (TRACK && TRACK.defaultLaps) || 2);

  laps:2, difficulty:0, aiMode:"sim", players:[], round:0,
  legendDeck:[], legendCard:null, legendRevealed:false,
  queue:[], active:null, stage:"setup",
  finishOrder:[], adrenalineIds:[],
  garage:"none", upgLevel:"reg",     // garage module settings

  hotseat:false, activeHuman:null, hotseatCfg:null   // pass & play: local drivers on one device
};

/* Simulated-driver profiles (used when aiMode==="sim").
   Rivals run a full deck/hand/engine and drive a whole round like a human;
   the difficulty index picks a skill profile. Fields shape *how well* they drive:
     aggression   – general willingness to push high cards / big gears
     heatFloor    – engine-heat reserve they try to protect (lower = tighter/braver)
     boostChance  – how often they Boost when it's sensible
     riskTolerance– how far over a corner limit they'll knowingly run (0–2)
     upshiftBias  – tendency to sit in higher gears
     cornerMargin – braking caution approaching corners (1 = brakes earlier) */
const SIM_PROFILES = [
  /* skill  – turns on the judgement code in simShift / simChooseCards
     rescue – how early the driver bails into a cooling gear (2 timid, 1 balanced, 0 last resort) */
  { key:0, name:"Davidson", aggression:0.15, heatFloor:5, boostChance:0.05, riskTolerance:0, upshiftBias:0.05, cornerMargin:1, skill:1, rescue:2 },
  { key:1, name:"Rookie",   aggression:0.45, heatFloor:2, boostChance:0.30, riskTolerance:0, upshiftBias:0.40, cornerMargin:0, skill:1, rescue:2 },
  { key:2, name:"Pro",      aggression:0.58, heatFloor:1, boostChance:0.45, riskTolerance:1, upshiftBias:0.55, cornerMargin:0, skill:1, rescue:1 },
  { key:3, name:"Legend",   aggression:0.72, heatFloor:0, boostChance:0.60, riskTolerance:1, upshiftBias:0.55, cornerMargin:0, skill:1, rescue:1 } 
];
const simProfile = () => SIM_PROFILES[Math.max(0, Math.min(SIM_PROFILES.length-1, G.difficulty))];
/* Per-driver profile. Every rival normally uses the chosen difficulty profile,
   but an "ace" (Le Cock) drives a profile that is never weaker than the field on
   any dimension — pushes harder, hits Boost more, upshifts sooner and protects a bit
   less Heat — so he's reliably one of the two fastest bots at any difficulty.
   Heat management stays sane (heatFloor kept ≥1, riskTolerance not forced to the
   spin-happy max) so the edge doesn't turn into self-inflicted spin-outs. */
function profileForBot(p){
  const base = simProfile();
  if(!(p && p.ace)) return base;
  /* Nudged toward the optimum, NOT toward recklessness: the old rule forced
     aggression .97 / upshiftBias .95, which measured as the SLOWEST car on the
     grid. Same gearing discipline and corner judgement as the tier he rides on. */
  return Object.assign({}, base, {
    aggression : Math.min(0.80, base.aggression  + 0.12),
    heatFloor  : Math.max(0,    base.heatFloor   - 1),
    boostChance: Math.min(0.75, base.boostChance + 0.15),
    rescue     : Math.min(base.rescue === undefined ? 1 : base.rescue, 1)
  });
}
const botDelay   = () => 220;
/* choose all combinations of k items from arr (small hands only) */
function kcombos(arr,k){
  const out=[];
  (function rec(start,acc){
    if(acc.length===k){ out.push(acc.slice()); return; }
    for(let i=start;i<arr.length;i++){ acc.push(arr[i]); rec(i+1,acc); acc.pop(); }
  })(0,[]);
  return out;
}

/* ============================================================
   GARAGE MODULE — the 60 Upgrade cards (the Garage module, retooled;
   transcribed from the
   "Heat - Garage Module, Regular and Advanced Upgrade Cards 2025"
   sheet). Cards 1–17 are the Regular tier, 18–60 the Advanced tier
   (49–60 are the Blower Scoop / Ram Prow designs added later —
   they introduce the Super Cool and Draft symbols).
   Fields:
     v        fixed speed value
     choice   pick-one speed values (e.g. Brakes)
     plus     number of ＋ flips added to the speed
     heat     mandatory: pay # Heat when revealed (can't pay → card is
              discarded and replaced by a flipped Basic card)
     scrap    mandatory: flip # cards from draw deck to discard
     limit    mandatory: your corner speed limits are adjusted by # this turn
     cool     optional: +# Cooldown available in React
     stress   optional: discard up to # Stress cards from hand
     slip     optional: +# spaces if you slipstream this turn
     salvage  optional: shuffle up to # discard cards back into the deck
     refresh  optional: put this card on top of the deck in step 9
     direct   optional: may be played from hand during React
     accel    optional: +1 speed per ＋ symbol used this turn
     supercool optional (React): pull up to # Heat cards straight out of
              the DISCARD pile and return them to the Engine
     draft    optional (React): move up to # spaces forward. Every space
              moved into/through must be completely free of cars, and the
              space in front of the landing space must hold at least one
              car. Adds no speed, but crossed corner lines still get
              checked. It is NOT slipstream — it may cross the finish
              line. Multiple Draft symbols pool their values.
     xslip    optional (step 6): Extra Slipstream — after a slipstream that
              leaves the car in a NEW slipstream position, the car may
              slipstream again (full move; all round slipstream bonuses
              re-apply, evaluated at the new position). One extra
              slipstream per symbol. Never across the Finish Line, and
              every corner line crossed still triggers Check Corner.
              (Heavy Rain expansion symbol.)
     xheat    this is an Extra Heat card (rides in the deck, +1 heat pool)
   If any icon on your printed cards differs, fix it here — one line per card. */

const upgradeById = id => UPGRADE_CARDS.find(u=>u.id===id);
/* expected speed of an upgrade (for bot valuation & previews) */
function upgExpected(def){
  let e = def.v||0;
  if(def.choice) e = Math.max(...def.choice);
  if(def.plus) e += 2.5*def.plus;
  return e;
}
/* min/max possible speed of an upgrade */
function upgSpan(def){
  let lo = def.v||0, hi = def.v||0;
  if(def.choice){ lo = Math.min(...def.choice); hi = Math.max(...def.choice); }
  if(def.plus){ lo += def.plus*1; hi += def.plus*4; }
  return [lo,hi];
}
/* bot draft valuation: expected speed + symbol worth − costs */
function upgScore(def, prof){
  if(def.xheat) return 0.5;                 // extra heat: mostly a dead pick for bots
  let s = upgExpected(def);
  s += (def.cool||0)*0.8 + (def.stress||0)*0.6 + (def.slip||0)*0.35
     + (def.salvage||0)*0.5 + (def.refresh?0.9:0) + (def.direct?1.1:0)
     + (def.accel?0.6:0) + (def.limit||0)*1.1
     + (def.supercool||0)*0.9 + (def.draft||0)*0.4    // Air Intake / Front Wing symbols
     + (def.xslip||0)*0.4;                             // Sliding Skirts — Extra Slipstream
  s -= (def.heat||0)*(prof ? (1.4-prof.aggression) : 0.9);
  s -= (def.scrap||0)*0.15;
  return s;
}
/* short symbol chips for card rendering / draft market */
function upgChips(def){
  const c=[];
  if(def.heat)   c.push(`<span class="chip m">🔥${def.heat}</span>`);
  if(def.scrap)  c.push(`<span class="chip m">🗑${def.scrap}</span>`);
  if(def.limit)  c.push(`<span class="chip m">⌀${def.limit>0?"+":""}${def.limit}</span>`);
  if(def.cool)   c.push(`<span class="chip c">❄${def.cool}</span>`);
  if(def.stress) c.push(`<span class="chip s">＋↓${def.stress}</span>`);
  if(def.slip)   c.push(`<span class="chip g">≫+${def.slip}</span>`);
  if(def.xslip)  c.push(`<span class="chip g">≫≫${def.xslip>1?def.xslip:""}</span>`);
  if(def.supercool)c.push(`<span class="chip c">🧲${def.supercool}</span>`);
  if(def.draft)  c.push(`<span class="chip g">⇥${def.draft}</span>`);
  if(def.salvage)c.push(`<span class="chip">👁${def.salvage}</span>`);
  if(def.refresh)c.push(`<span class="chip">⟳</span>`);
  if(def.direct) c.push(`<span class="chip">☞</span>`);
  if(def.accel)  c.push(`<span class="chip">⚡＋</span>`);
  return c.join("");
}
function upgValueLabel(def){
  if(def.xheat) return "🔥";
  const parts=[];
  if(def.choice) parts.push(def.choice.join("|"));
  else if(def.v!==undefined) parts.push(String(def.v));
  if(def.plus) parts.push("＋".repeat(def.plus));
  return parts.join("") || "＋";
}

/* ============================================================
   CHAMPIONSHIP SYSTEM — Sponsorship cards, Event cards, Press Corner
   (Advanced Play & Championship System booklet, pp. 8–10.)

   SPONSORSHIP CARDS — one-time-use upgrades. Drawn straight into the HAND
   (event grant / Press Corner), never shuffled into the deck — except cards
   carried over from a previous race, which the rulebook shuffles into the
   draw deck at setup. Played like any upgrade; if one is in your Play Area
   at end of turn it is REMOVED FROM THE GAME instead of discarded. You may
   also discard one from hand (then it lives in your discard pile like any
   card). Unused cards carry over between races; worthless when the season
   ends. The real deck is 7 copies each of 8 sponsors; the two Heavy Rain
   sponsors (E.Mercury, Eole Wings) are included too since this build already
   supports their Super Cool / Draft symbols. Turn SPONSOR_HEAVY_RAIN off for
   the strict base-game deck. */
const SPONSOR_HEAVY_RAIN = true;
const SPONSOR_CARDS = [

  {id:110, name:"Beewee Racecraft", v:4, salvage:2, sponser:true},
  {id:110, name:"Noctie Motorsport", v:7, limit:-1, sponser:true},
];
const sponsorById = id => SPONSOR_CARDS.find(s=>s.id===id);
function makeSponsorCard(def){ return Object.assign(makeCard("upg"), {def}); }
function buildSponsorDeck(){
  const pool=[];
  for(const s of SPONSOR_CARDS){
    if(s.hr && !SPONSOR_HEAVY_RAIN) continue;
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++) pool.push(s.id);
  }
  return shuffle(pool);
}

   text:"Everyone shuffles 1 extra Stress card into their deck — and any spin-out is disqualifying: you leave the race and score no points.",
   sponsor:0, press:[3], setupStress:1, spinDQ:true},
];

/* hand size for the current race (Event cards can raise/lower it) */
function handSize(){ return (G.event && G.event.hand) || 7; }
/* is this corner-line total a Press Corner line? (any lap) */
function isPressLine(ct){
  if(!G.pressCorners || !G.pressCorners.length) return false;
  const at = phys(ct);                       // line's physical space
  return G.pressCorners.some(i=>TRACK.corners[i] && phys(TRACK.corners[i].at)===at);
}
/* deal n Sponsorship cards straight into a driver's hand (champ only).
   Deck empty → reshuffle the cards PLAYED since the race start (rulebook);
   still empty → nothing. Legend pace-bots never hold cards. */
function gainSponsor(p, n, why){
  const ch=G.champ; if(!ch || !ch.sponsorDeck) return 0;
  if(p.isBot && !p.sim) return 0;
  let got=0;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    if(!ch.sponsorDeck.length && ch.sponsorUsed.length){
      ch.sponsorDeck=shuffle(ch.sponsorUsed.splice(0));
      log("Sponsorship deck reshuffled from this race's used cards.");
    }
    const id=ch.sponsorDeck.pop(); if(id===undefined) break;
    p.hand.push(makeSponsorCard(sponsorById(id))); got++;
  }
  if(got){
    const who = p.isBot||G.hotseat ? p.name : "You";
    log(`🤝 ${who} gain${p.isBot?"s":""} ${got} Sponsorship card${got>1?"s":""}${why?` — ${why}`:""}.`, p.isBot?"":"me");
    if(!p.isBot){ toast(`🤝 Sponsorship card${got>1?"s":""}!`); renderGauges(); }
  }
  return got;
}
/* end-of-turn sponsorship awards: Press Corner passes logged during the turn,
   plus the per-turn Event bonuses. Nothing pays out on a spin-out turn. */
function resolveTurnSponsors(p){
  if(!G.champ) return;
  const ev=G.event||{};
  if(p.spunOut){ p.pressPending && p.pressPending.clear(); return; }
  let n=0, whys=[];
  if(p.pressPending && p.pressPending.size){
    n += p.pressPending.size * (ev.press2x?2:1);
    whys.push("Press Corner"); p.pressPending.clear();
  }
  if(ev.speed15 && p.speed>=15){ n++; whys.push(`speed ${p.speed}`); }
  if(ev.overtake3 && p._aheadBefore){
    const passed=[...p._aheadBefore].filter(id=>{
      const q=G.players.find(x=>x.id===id);
      return q && !q.finished && (p.total>q.total || (p.total===q.total && p.spot<q.spot));
    }).length;
    if(passed>=3) { n++; whys.push(`${passed} overtakes`); }
  }
  if(ev.lap1sponsor && !p._lap1Paid && p.total>=S){
    p._lap1Paid=true;
    if((G._lap1Count=(G._lap1Count||0)+1)<=3){ n++; whys.push("lap-1 sprint"); }
  }
  if(n) gainSponsor(p, n, whys.join(" · "));
}

/* ---------------- helpers */
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
/* html-escape for anything dropped into innerHTML (screens are built as strings) */
const esc = s => String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[<>&"]/g, ch=>({"<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","&":"&amp;",'"':"&quot;"}[ch]));
const shuffle = a => { for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[a[i],a[j]]=[a[j],a[i]];} return a; };
/* =====================================================================
   TRACK LAYOUT — how the strip of Spaces is wired up. Set per circuit with
   `layout:` (default "loop" when the field is absent, so every existing
   board keeps behaving exactly as before).

     "loop"    a normal circuit. The last Space joins back to Space 0 and the
               start/finish chequer sits on that join. Race = laps × spaces.

     "open"    point-to-point. Space 0 is the START, the last Space is the
               FINISH, and the two ends NEVER join: nothing wraps, tunnels
               can't run past the end, and no car crosses the flag and
               carries on. Always a single run (laps forced to 1).
               Race distance = spaces. Drag strips, sprints, hillclimbs.

     "leadin"  a one-off run-up joined to a lap. Spaces 0 … lapStart-1 are
               driven ONCE off the start line; the cars then join the circuit
               at Space lapStart and go round lapStart … spaces-1 for `laps`
               laps — the last Space joins back to lapStart (NOT to Space 0),
               and the start/finish chequer sits on THAT join.
               Race distance = lapStart + laps × (spaces − lapStart).

   Everything downstream works in `total` (spaces travelled since the start
   line, negative on the grid); phys() is the one place that maps a total to
   a physical Space, so getting it right here fixes movement, blocking,
   corner checks, sectors, tunnels and the car sprites in one go.
   ===================================================================== */
function layoutOf(t){
  const L = ((t||TRACK).layout || "loop");
  return (L==="open" || L==="leadin") ? L : "loop";
}
/* first Space of the repeating lap — 0 for anything that isn't a lead-in.
   A nonsense lapStart (0, negative, past the end) degrades to a plain loop. */
function lapStartOf(t){
  t = t || TRACK;
  if(layoutOf(t) !== "leadin") return 0;
  const ls = Math.round(+t.lapStart || 0);
  return (ls > 0 && ls < t.spaces) ? ls : 0;
}
function lapLenOf(t){ t = t || TRACK; return t.spaces - lapStartOf(t); }
/* live shorthands for the circuit currently loaded (kept in step by syncLayout) */
let LAYOUT = "loop", LAP_START = 0, LAP_LEN = 0;
function syncLayout(){
  LAYOUT    = layoutOf(TRACK);
  LAP_START = lapStartOf(TRACK);
  if(LAYOUT === "leadin" && LAP_START === 0) LAYOUT = "loop";   // bad data → plain circuit
  LAP_LEN   = S - LAP_START;
}
/* the total at which lap `k` has been completed — used by anything that asks
   "who led at the end of lap 1?" */
function lapEndTotal(k){
  if(LAYOUT==="open")   return raceDist();
  if(LAYOUT==="leadin") return LAP_START + Math.min(k, raceLaps())*LAP_LEN;
  return Math.min(k, raceLaps())*S;
}
/* laps actually raced (a point-to-point board is always a single run) */
const raceLaps = () => LAYOUT==="open" ? 1 : Math.max(1, (typeof G!=="undefined" && G.laps) || 1);
/* the total at which a car has taken the chequered flag */
function raceDist(){
  if(LAYOUT==="open")   return S;                            // start line → past the last Space
  if(LAYOUT==="leadin") return LAP_START + raceLaps()*LAP_LEN;
  return raceLaps()*S;
}
/* which lap a total sits on (1-based); an open board is always lap 1 */
function lapOfTotal(total){
  if(LAYOUT==="open") return 1;
  const t = Math.max(total, 0);
  if(LAYOUT==="leadin")
    return t < LAP_START ? 1 : Math.min(raceLaps(), Math.floor((t-LAP_START)/LAP_LEN)+1);
  return Math.min(raceLaps(), Math.floor(t/S)+1);
}
/* total → physical Space. Negative totals are the starting grid: on a loop
   they wrap round to the tail of the board (which is where the printed grid
   boxes are), on open / lead-in boards there is no road back there, so they
   stay negative and are drawn as virtual Spaces off the front of the strip. */
const phys = t => {
  if(LAYOUT==="open") return t;                              // never wraps, either end
  if(t < 0) return LAYOUT==="loop" ? ((t % S) + S) % S : t;
  if(LAYOUT==="leadin") return t < LAP_START ? t : LAP_START + ((t-LAP_START) % LAP_LEN);
  return t % S;
};
/* physical neighbours, layout aware. nextSpace returns -1 for "there is no
   next Space" (the end of an open board) so drawing walks can just stop. */
function nextSpace(i){
  if(i < 0) return i+1;                                      // grid run-up onto the board
  if(i < S-1) return i+1;
  if(LAYOUT==="open")   return -1;
  if(LAYOUT==="leadin") return LAP_START;
  return 0;
}
function prevSpace(i){
  if(LAYOUT==="leadin" && i===LAP_START) return S-1;         // the lap's join, not the lead-in
  if(i > 0) return i-1;
  return LAYOUT==="loop" ? ((i-1) % S + S) % S : i-1;         // open/lead-in: off the front
}
/* the traced board point for a Space index, with virtual points extrapolated
   off either end so the grid boxes, the start line and any run-off past the
   flag have somewhere to be drawn on open / lead-in boards. */
function tracePt(i, t){
  t = t || TRACK;
  const P = t.spacePts, n = t.spaces, L = layoutOf(t), ls = lapStartOf(t), ll = n - ls;
  if(i >= 0 && i < n) return P[i];
  if(L === "loop") return P[((i % n) + n) % n];
  if(i >= n) return L==="leadin" ? P[ls + ((i-n) % ll)]
                                 : [P[n-1][0] + (P[n-1][0]-P[n-2][0])*(i-(n-1)),
                                    P[n-1][1] + (P[n-1][1]-P[n-2][1])*(i-(n-1))];
  return [P[0][0] + (P[1][0]-P[0][0])*i, P[0][1] + (P[1][1]-P[0][1])*i];
}
/* the run of Spaces a tunnel entry covers, walked in track order so it stops
   dead at the end of an open board and rejoins at lapStart on a lead-in */
function runSpaces(r, t){
  t = t || TRACK;
  const n = t.spaces, L = layoutOf(t), ls = lapStartOf(t);
  const norm = v => ((v % n) + n) % n;
  const from = norm(typeof r === "number" ? r : r.from);
  const to   = norm(typeof r === "number" ? r : (r.to===undefined ? r.from : r.to));
  const out = [];
  let i = from;
  for(let k=0; k<n; k++){
    out.push(i);
    if(i === to) break;
    if(i >= n-1){
      if(L === "open") break;                                // no wrap: the run just ends
      i = (L === "leadin") ? ls : 0;
    } else i++;
  }
  return out;
}
syncLayout();
function log(msg, cls){
  const el = document.createElement("div"); if(cls) el.className = cls;
  el.textContent = msg;
  $("#logbody").prepend(el);
  $("#logtoggle").textContent = "▸ " + msg;
}
let toastTimer=null;
function toast(msg, hot){
  const t=$("#toast"); t.textContent=msg; t.className = hot ? "show hot" : "show";
  clearTimeout(toastTimer); toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove("show"), 2100);
}
const wait = ms => new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
window.G = G; // exposed for debugging/tests
/* stale-tap guard: every interactive stage arms a token; handlers from a
   previous render (detached panels, double-taps) become no-ops */
let uiToken=0;
const arm = () => ++uiToken;
const live = tk => tk===uiToken;

/* ---------------- cards */
function makeCard(t,v){ return {t, v:(v===undefined?null:v), id:Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}; }
function cardLabel(c){
  switch(c.t){
    case "speed": return {cv:c.v, ct:"Speed", cls:"speed"};
    case "up0":   return {cv:0,  ct:"Starting Upgrade", cls:"up"};
    case "up5":   return {cv:5,  ct:"Starting Upgrade", cls:"up"};
    case "upheat":return {cv:"🔥", ct:"Extra Heat", cls:"heatc"};
    case "heat":  return {cv:"🔥", ct:"Heat", cls:"heatc"};
    case "stress":return {cv:"＋", ct:"Stress", cls:"stressc"};
    case "upg":   return {cv:upgValueLabel(c.def), ct:c.def.name, cls:"garage"+(c.def.curse?" curse":"")+(c.def.sponsor?" sponsor":""), def:c.def};
  }
}
/* build a playable card from a garage upgrade definition. Extra Heat upgrades
   are literal Heat cards that start in the draw deck (the "upheat" type). */
function makeUpgCard(def){
  if(def.xheat) return Object.assign(makeCard("upheat"), {xheat:true});
  return Object.assign(makeCard("upg"), {def});
}
const isHeat   = c => c.t==="heat" || c.t==="upheat";
const isStress = c => c.t==="stress";
const isUpg    = c => c.t==="upg";
const isSpeed14= c => c.t==="speed";           // "original 1–4" cards only
const isCurse    = c => c.t==="upg" && c.def && c.def.curse;
const discardable = c => !isHeat(c) && !isStress(c) && !isCurse(c);

/* ---------------- heat conservation
   The map defines the total pool of Heat per car (Pukekohe: 6). Weather /
   road-condition modifiers would adjust heatCap(), never the invariant:
   engine + Heat cards in deck/discard/hand/play must NEVER exceed the cap.
   The starting "upgrade Heat" card counts as one of those 6 — it starts in
   the deck, so the Engine starts with cap−1. cooling it back down (hand→Engine)
   is a transfer, not creation. */
let WEATHER_HEAT_MOD = 0;   // weather car-setup: pool size change (+1 / −1 heat in/out of the game)
let WEATHER_STRESS_MOD = 0; // weather car-setup: stress cards added to / removed from the deck
function heatCap(){ return TRACK.heat + WEATHER_HEAT_MOD; }
/* per-driver pool cap: Extra Heat garage upgrades ride in the deck and grow
   the pool by 1 each (they ARE additional heat cards, by design) */
function heatCapFor(p){ return Math.max(1, heatCap() + (p ? (classOf(p).tank||0) + (p.xheat||0) : 0)); }
function totalHeat(p){
  const n = a => a.filter(isHeat).length;
  return p.engine + n(p.deck) + n(p.discard) + n(p.hand) + n(p.play);
}
/* defensive check after every heat mutation: clamp + shout if the pool grew */
function auditHeat(p, ctx){
  if(p.isBot && !p.sim) return;          // Legend bots have no heat pool; sim drivers do
  const t = totalHeat(p), cap = heatCapFor(p);
  if(t > cap){
    const over = t - cap;
    p.engine = Math.max(0, p.engine - over);
    if(!p.isBot) log(`Heat audit (${ctx}): pool hit ${t}/${cap} — Engine clamped by ${over}.`, "warn");
  }
}

/* ---------------- vehicle classes (wasteland garages)
   Each class trades on three axes that must roughly cancel out:
     deck total   — sum of the 12 speed cards (baseline 30)
     Engine         — Heat-pool delta vs the track's printed pool (baseline 6)
     cornering    — permanent corner-limit adjustment, Cooldown rates,
                    slipstream base distance and spin-out severity.
   deck[]   = how many 1s / 2s / 3s / 4s ride in the deck (always 12 cards)
   cool{}   = Cooldown available per gear in React
   starters = the 3 class cards that replace the classic 0 / 5 / Heat trio
              (each keeps one Heat card riding in the deck, like the core game) */
const VEHICLE_CLASSES = [
  { key:"ghost", icon:"🏎️", name:"Formula", art:"assets/avatars/decks/ghost.png",
    tag:"Base Game. The balanced baseline.",
    deck:[3,3,3,3], tank:0, stress:0, limitAdj:0, slipBase:2, spinStress:0,
    cool:{1:3, 2:1},
    starters:[ {name:"Coast", v:0}, {name:"Full Send", v:5}, {xheat:true} ],
    blurb:"Even deck (3× 1–4) · 6-Heat Engine · standard corner limits · Cooldown 3/1 in gears 1–2. Drives exactly like the classic game." },
  { key:"v8", icon:"🛻", name:"V8 Supercar", art:"assets/avatars/decks/warlord.png",
    tag:"Fan Made - Monstrous on straights",
    deck:[2,2,4,4], tank:+2, stress:0, limitAdj:-1, slipBase:2, spinStress:0,
    cool:{1:2},
    starters:[ {name:"Ram Air", v:5, scrap:1}, {name:"Grease Monkey", v:0, cool:1}, {xheat:true} ],
    blurb:"Fast deck (2×1 2×2 4×3 4×4, total 34) · big 8-Heat Engine · corner limits are 1 LOWER for you · weak Cooldown (2/0). Push the Heat or slow down — you can't do neither." },
  { key:"bike", icon:"🏍️", name:"MotoGP", art:"assets/avatars/decks/viper.png",
    tag:"Fan Made - Corners like a dream.",
    deck:[4,3,3,2], tank:-2, stress:0, limitAdj:+1, slipBase:3, spinStress:1,
    cool:{1:3, 2:1},
    starters:[ {name:"Lean In", v:0, limit:1}, {name:"Wheelie", v:4, heat:1}, {xheat:true} ],
    blurb:"Slow deck (4×1 3×2 3×3 2×4, total 27) · tiny 4 heat · corner limits are 1 HIGHER for you · slipstream 3 spaces · Spinouts deal +1 extra Stress." },
  { key:"buggy", icon:"🚜", name:"Rallycross", art:"assets/avatars/decks/buggy.png",
    tag:"Fan Made - The underdog entry. Never fast, never stranded.",
    deck:[3,3,3,3], tank:-1, stress:+1, limitAdj:0, slipBase:2, spinStress:0,
    cool:{1:3, 2:1, 3:1, 4:1},
    starters:[ {name:"Quick Fix", v:0, salvage:2}, {name:"Bolt-On Turbo", v:5, heat:1}, {xheat:true} ],
    blurb:"Even deck · 5 Heat · one extra Stress card in the deck · may cool 1 in ANY gear · starter cards recycle the deck. The consistency pick." },
];
const classByKey = k => VEHICLE_CLASSES.find(c=>c.key===k) || VEHICLE_CLASSES[0];
const classOf    = p => classByKey(p && p.cls);
const randomClassKey = () => VEHICLE_CLASSES[Math.floor(Math.random()*VEHICLE_CLASSES.length)].key;
const classChip  = p => { const c=classOf(p); return `${c.icon} ${c.name}`; };

/* ---------------- players */
/* a fresh, shuffled starting deck.
   Core game:      12 class speed cards + the class's 3 starter cards + stress.
   Garage / champ: 12 class speed cards + the driver's drafted Upgrade cards
                   + stress (starter cards go back in the box, per the rules). */
function buildDeck(upgrades, clsKey){
  const cls = classByKey(clsKey);
  const deck = [];
  cls.deck.forEach((n,i)=>{ for(let k=0;k<n;k++) deck.push(makeCard("speed", i+1)); });
  if(upgrades) upgrades.forEach(id => deck.push(makeUpgCard(upgradeById(id))));
  else cls.starters.forEach(def => deck.push(makeUpgCard(def)));
  const nStress = Math.max(0, TRACK.stress + WEATHER_STRESS_MOD + (cls.stress||0));
  for(let i=0;i<nStress;i++) deck.push(makeCard("stress"));
  return shuffle(deck);
}
/* per-round upgrade scratch fields, reset every round */
function resetRoundScratch(p){
  p.speed=0; p.crossings=[]; p.boostUsed=false; p.cooldownLeft=0;
  p.adrenaline=false; p.adrSpeedUsed=false; p.cluttered=false; p.spunOut=false;
  if(p.pressPending) p.pressPending.clear();
  p.limitAdj=(classOf(p).limitAdj||0); p.plusUsed=0; p.slipBonus=0; p.accelUsed=false;   // class cornering baked in — upgrade symbols stack on top
  p.draftUsed=0; p._xslipLeft=0;
  p.refreshIds=new Set(); p.usedSym=new Set();
}
/* ---- hotseat helpers: every human-turn function asks "whose turn is it?"
   instead of assuming the single human at G.players[0]. Solo play resolves
   to the same driver it always did. */
function humans(){ return G.players.filter(p=>!p.isBot); }
function curHuman(){
  if(G.activeHuman && !G.activeHuman.isBot && !G.activeHuman._gone) return G.activeHuman;
  return humans()[0] || G.players[0];
}
/* The header sub-line under "HEAT · <track>". Before the lights go out it's the
   circuit's subtitle; once racing it carries what used to sit on the board as the
   Lap flag — lap, round, and (in pass & play) whose hands are on the wheel. */
function updateRoundLabel(){
  const rl=$("#roundlbl"); if(!rl) return;
  if(!G.players || !G.players.length){ rl.textContent = TRACK.subtitle; return; }
  const you=curHuman();
  const rd=`Round ${Math.max(G.round,1)}`;
  if(!you){ rl.textContent = rd; return; }
  const lap=lapOfTotal(you.total);
  // an open (point-to-point) board has no laps to count — just the run
  const where = LAYOUT==="open" ? rd
    : (LAYOUT==="leadin" && you.total < LAP_START) ? `Run-up · ${rd}`
    : `Lap ${lap}/${raceLaps()} · ${rd}`;
  rl.textContent = (G.hotseat ? you.name+" · " : "") + (you.finished ? `🏁 Finished · ${rd}` : where);
}
/* small name chip appended to phase titles in pass & play, so there's never
   any doubt whose hand is on screen */
function phaseWho(you){
  if(!G.hotseat) return "";
  return ` <span style="color:${you.color};text-shadow:0 0 6px rgba(0,0,0,.6)">· ${you.name}</span>`;
}
function makeHuman(upgrades, who, clsKey){
  const cls = clsKey || (who && who.cls) || G.playerCls || "ghost";
  const deck = buildDeck(upgrades, cls);
  const p = {
    id: who ? who.id : "you",
    name: who ? who.name : "No. 17 — You",
    color: who ? who.color : "#8a5cf6",
    carNum: who && who.carNum ? who.carNum : 17,
    isBot:false, cls,
    upgrades: upgrades ? upgrades.slice() : null,
    xheat: upgrades ? upgrades.filter(id=>upgradeById(id).xheat).length : 0,
    total:0, spot:0, gear:1, engine:0,
    deck, discard:[], hand:[], play:[],
    finished:false, finishRound:null
  };
  // core game: Engine holds classCap−1 (the class Extra Heat card rides in the deck).
  // Garage/champ: no starter cards, so the Engine starts full; Extra Heat
  // upgrades ride in the deck on top.
  p.engine = upgrades ? heatCapFor(p) : heatCapFor(p)-1;
  resetRoundScratch(p);
  return p;
}
function makeBot(i, upgrades, clsKey){
  const b = BOT_POOL[i];
  const base = { id:"bot"+i, name:b.name, color:b.color, isBot:true,
    ace:/le\s*cock/i.test(b.name),      // Le Cock is the class of the field
    total:0, spot:0, finished:false, finishRound:null };
  base.cls = clsKey || b.cls || randomClassKey();   // every rival rolls a wasteland garage
  if(G.aiMode==="sim"){
    // full "human-like" driver: its own deck, hand, Engine and gear
    base.sim = true;
    base.upgrades = upgrades ? upgrades.slice() : null;
    base.xheat = upgrades ? upgrades.filter(id=>upgradeById(id).xheat).length : 0;
    base.deck = buildDeck(upgrades, base.cls); base.discard=[]; base.hand=[]; base.play=[];
    base.gear = 1; base.engine = upgrades ? heatCapFor(base) : heatCapFor(base)-1;
    resetRoundScratch(base);
  }
  return base;
}

/* ---------------- deck ops */
function draw(p){
  if(p.deck.length===0){
    if(p.discard.length===0) return null;
    p.deck = shuffle(p.discard); p.discard = [];
    log("You reshuffle your discard into a new draw deck.");
  }
  return p.deck.pop();
}
function drawToHand(p,n){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ const c=draw(p); if(!c) break; p.hand.push(c); }
}
/* flip from deck until an original 1–4 Speed card appears (stress / Boost) */
function flipForSpeed(p){
  const junk=[];
  let tries = p.deck.length + p.discard.length + 2; // one full cycle max
  while(tries-- > 0){
    const c = draw(p);
    if(!c) break;
    if(isSpeed14(c)) return {v:c.v, card:c, junk};
    junk.push(c); p.discard.push(c);
  }
  return {v:0, junk}; // no speed cards reachable — treat as 0
}

/* ---------------- occupancy & movement */
function carsAt(space, exclude){
  return G.players.filter(p => !p.finished && p!==exclude && phys(p.total)===space);
}
function spaceFull(space, exclude){ return carsAt(space, exclude).length >= 2; }
function takeSpot(p){
  const others = carsAt(phys(p.total), p);
  p.spot = others.some(o=>o.spot===0) ? 1 : 0;
}
/* move exactly dist; if landing space full, fall back to nearest free space behind.
   Records corner-line crossings on the car for the corner check step. */
function moveCar(p, dist, opts={}){
  if(dist<=0) return 0;
  const from = p.total;
  let target = from + dist;
  while(target>from && spaceFull(phys(target), p)) target--;
  if(target===from) return 0;
  p.total = target; takeSpot(p);
  if(opts.trackCrossings!==false && p.crossings){   // human + simulated drivers track corner lines; Legend bots don't
    for(const c of cornerTotals()){
      if(from < c && target >= c && c <= raceDist()) p.crossings.push(c);
    }
  }
  renderCars();
  return target-from;
}
/* ---------------- Draft symbol (Front Wing upgrades)
   Move up to `pool` spaces in React. Every space moved into/through must be
   COMPLETELY free (both spots), and the space in front of where the car stops
   must hold at least one car. Adds no speed; crossed corner lines still get
   checked. Not slipstream — it may cross the finish line. Returns the list of
   legal draft distances from the car's current position. */
function draftTargets(p, pool){
  const out=[];
  for(let d=1; d<=pool; d++){
    let free=true;
    for(let t=p.total+1; t<=p.total+d; t++){
      if(carsAt(phys(t),p).length>0){ free=false; break; }
    }
    if(!free) break;                                   // a car in the path blocks all longer drafts too
    if(carsAt(phys(p.total+d+1),p).length>0) out.push(d); // must stop right behind a car
  }
  return out;
}
/* perform a legal draft move: teleport-style (path already verified free),
   record corner-line crossings for the Check Corner step, spend the pool */
function doDraft(p, d){
  const from=p.total;
  p.total=from+d; takeSpot(p);
  if(p.crossings){
    for(const c of cornerTotals()){
      if(from < c && p.total >= c && c <= raceDist()) p.crossings.push(c);
    }
  }
  p.draftUsed=(p.draftUsed||0)+d;
  renderCars();
  return d;
}
/* every Corner Line the race will cross, as a total. On a lead-in board the
   corners that sit in the run-up (at < lapStart) are crossed ONCE; the ones on
   the lap repeat every lap. An open board crosses each corner exactly once. */
function cornerTotals(){
  const out=[];
  if(LAYOUT==="open"){
    for(const c of TRACK.corners) out.push(c.at);
  } else if(LAYOUT==="leadin"){
    const laps=raceLaps();
    for(const c of TRACK.corners){
      if(c.at < LAP_START) out.push(c.at);                       // run-up: driven once
      else for(let lap=0; lap<laps; lap++) out.push(LAP_START + lap*LAP_LEN + (c.at-LAP_START));
    }
  } else {
    for(let lap=0; lap<raceLaps(); lap++) for(const c of TRACK.corners) out.push(lap*S + c.at);
  }
  return out.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
}
function cornerIdxOf(cornerTotal){
  const at = phys(cornerTotal);
  return TRACK.corners.findIndex(c=>c.at===at);
}

/* ============================================================
   CHICANES  (Tunnel Vision / Rocky Roads feature)
   A chicane is 2 (or more) consecutive Corner Lines with the SAME speed
   limit and a space or two between them. On the board they're marked with
   blue kerbs on both sides. Rules that matter here:
     · Only ONE Road Condition token is drawn for the whole chicane.
     · A corner-type token modifies EVERY Corner Line in the chicane.
     · A sector-type token modifies the sector AFTER the chicane (i.e. the
       stretch running on from its last Corner Line, not the short bit of
       road between the two lines).
     · Everything else is unchanged: each Corner Line is still checked
       separately at Check Corner, in the order you crossed it.
   Data: a corner carries `chicane:<n>` — corners sharing the same n are one
   chicane. No `chicane` (or 0) = an ordinary standalone corner.
   ============================================================ */
const chicaneIdOf = idx => {
  const c = TRACK.corners[idx];
  return (c && c.chicane) ? c.chicane : 0;
};
/* every corner index, bundled into the units that share a road-condition
   token: an ordinary corner is a group of one, a chicane is a group of all
   its Corner Lines (in track order). */
function tokenGroups(){
  const groups=[], byId=new Map();
  TRACK.corners.forEach((c,i)=>{
    const id = c.chicane || 0;
    if(!id){ groups.push([i]); return; }
    if(byId.has(id)){ byId.get(id).push(i); return; }
    const grp=[i]; byId.set(id, grp); groups.push(grp);
  });
  return groups;
}
/* the group (chicane or lone corner) that corner #idx belongs to */
function groupOfCorner(idx){
  const id = chicaneIdOf(idx);
  if(!id) return [idx];
  const out=[];
  TRACK.corners.forEach((c,i)=>{ if((c.chicane||0)===id) out.push(i); });
  return out;
}
const isChicaneCorner = idx => chicaneIdOf(idx) > 0;
/* how many chicanes a track has — works on any track object, not just the live one */
function chicaneCount(t){
  const ids=new Set();
  ((t&&t.corners)||[]).forEach(c=>{ if(c.chicane) ids.add(c.chicane); });
  return ids.size;
}
/* how a group reads in the log / tooltips */
function groupLabel(grp){
  return grp.length>1 ? `Chicane (corners ${grp.map(i=>i+1).join("+")})` : `Corner ${grp[0]+1}`;
}

/* the road-condition token sitting on corner #idx (null when none / dry) */
function cornerTokOf(idx){
  return (G.weather && G.weather.cornerTok) ? (G.weather.cornerTok[idx] || null) : null;
}
/* EFFECTIVE speed limit of corner #idx: the printed value with its Adjust Speed
   Limit road-condition token already folded in. This is the number the board
   shows on the corner badge — there is no separate token to read off the board. */
function limitOfCorner(idx){
  let lim = TRACK.corners[idx].limit;
  const tok = cornerTokOf(idx);
  if(tok==="lim+1") lim += 1; else if(tok==="lim-1") lim -= 1;
  return Math.max(0, lim);
}
function limitAt(cornerTotal){
  return limitOfCorner(cornerIdxOf(cornerTotal));
}
function cornerHasOverheat(cornerTotal){
  return cornerTokOf(cornerIdxOf(cornerTotal))==="overheat";
}
function nextCornerTotal(total){
  for(const c of cornerTotals()) if(c > total) return c;
  return Infinity; // past all corners → run to the flag
}
const finishedLine = p => p.total >= raceDist();

/* ---------------- corner-heat forecasting
   Speed for the check = all card values + Boost + adrenaline. Distance moved
   equals speed pre-blocking, so projecting "move d from here" doubles as
   "check corners at speed d" for the pre-Boost estimate. */
function crossingsForMove(from, dist){
  const out=[];
  for(const c of cornerTotals()) if(from < c && from+dist >= c && c <= raceDist()) out.push(c);
  return out;
}
/* deterministic wind shift on a corner whose approach is a weather sector
   (Strong Wind): tailwind +1 / headwind −1 effective speed — mirrors the real
   Check Corner so the spin-out forecast & confirm gate stay accurate in wind. */
function windAdjAt(ct){
  const wDef = (G.weather && G.weather.def) ? G.weather.def.sector : {};
  if(!(wDef.wind && approachIsWeatherSector(ct))) return 0;
  const cls = windClassAt(sectorBefore(ct));
  return cls==='tail' ? 1 : cls==='head' ? -1 : 0;
}
/* Weather effects that PULL AN EXTRA CARD as you cross a corner. Both are
   random draws off the top of your deck, so — unlike steady wind (windAdjAt),
   which shifts the corner speed by a known ±1 — they CANNOT honestly be folded
   into the Heat bill as a fixed number. (An earlier build baked in a flat +1
   for gusts; that was wrong, because a gust can add anywhere from 0 to 4.)
   Instead the planner surfaces them as explicit warnings so you leave Heat
   headroom or brake earlier. The conditions below mirror runCornerCheck exactly.

     · GUST  — flips the top card; a Speed card (1–4) is added to your corner
               speed, anything else adds 0. Fires whenever the corner borders a
               weather sector, regardless of your speed.
     · AQUA  — only bites above speed 10: flips the top card and a Stress card
               spins you out. Fires on a corner whose approach is a weather sector. */
function gustAtCorner(ct){
  const wDef = (G.weather && G.weather.def) ? G.weather.def.sector : {};
  return !!(wDef.gust && cornerBordersWeatherSector(cornerIdxOf(ct)));
}
function aquaAtCorner(ct){
  const wDef = (G.weather && G.weather.def) ? G.weather.def.sector : {};
  return !!(wDef.aqua && approachIsWeatherSector(ct));
}
/* short HTML warning bits for the corner line(s) a move crosses (or the next
   corner, while planning). `speed`, when known, sharpens the aquaplaning note
   (it only triggers above speed 10); pass it null/undefined to show aqua as a
   standing caution before a speed is chosen. Returns [] when nothing applies. */
function cornerWeatherWarnings(crossTotals, speed){
  const bits=[];
  const uniq=[...new Set(crossTotals)].filter(ct=>ct!=null && ct!==Infinity);
  if(uniq.some(gustAtCorner))
    bits.push(`<b style="color:#8fd0ff">💨 Gust</b> — crossing flips the top card of your deck; a Speed card adds <b>+1 to +4</b> to your corner speed (0 if it's a Heat / Stress / Upgrade). Keep a little Heat spare or brake earlier.`);
  if(uniq.some(aquaAtCorner) && (speed==null || speed>10))
    bits.push(`<b style="color:#7fd0ff">🌧 Aquaplane</b> — cross this corner above <b>speed 10</b> and a flipped Stress card <b style="color:#ff5a3c">spins you out</b>. Hold it to 10 or under here.`);
  return bits;
}
function cornerHeatCost(speed, crossTotals, limAdj=0){
  let n=0;
  for(const ct of [...new Set(crossTotals)]){
    const eff = speed + windAdjAt(ct);                 // steady wind shifts corner speed by a known ±1; gusts are random → warned, not billed
    const over = Math.max(0, eff - (limitAt(ct) + limAdj)); // limAdj: Tires/Wings "adjust speed limit" symbols
    if(over>0) n += over + (cornerHasOverheat(ct)?1:0); // Overheat token adds 1 to the bill when you're over
  }
  return n;
}
/* always-on "how far to the next corner" strip, shown while shifting / picking cards */
function cornerDistanceHTML(you){
  const nc = nextCornerTotal(you.total);
  if(nc===Infinity)
    return `<div class="forecast">🏁 <b>No corner before the finish</b> — nothing to brake for, send it.</div>`;
  const dc = nc - you.total;
  const la = you.limitAdj||0;   // driver's persistent corner-limit shift (class cornering + career mods)
  const lim = limitAt(nc) + la;
  const oh = cornerHasOverheat(nc) ? ' · <b style="color:var(--heat)">🔥 overheat</b>' : "";
  const wWarn = cornerWeatherWarnings([nc]);   // gust / aquaplane at the next corner (speed not chosen yet)
  const gustHere = wWarn.length ? ` · ${wWarn.join(" · ")}` : "";
  const nc2 = nextCornerTotal(nc);
  let second = "";
  if(nc2!==Infinity && (nc2 - you.total) <= dc + 9)
    second = ` · then another in <b>${nc2-you.total}</b> (limit ${limitAt(nc2)+la})`;
  // a chicane is two lines at the same limit in quick succession — worth saying so,
  // because one speed is checked against both of them
  const chic = isChicaneCorner(cornerIdxOf(nc))
    ? ` <b style="color:#4fb3ff">Chicane</b> — both lines are checked at this speed.` : "";
  return `<div class="forecast">🏁 <b>Next corner line: ${dc} space${dc===1?"":"s"} ahead</b> — speed limit <b style="color:var(--purple-glow)">${lim}</b>${oh}${gustHere}${second}.${chic} Reach it above the limit and you'll pay Heat.</div>`;
}
/* "what the weather is doing to you right here" — a short strip shown while you
   plan, but only when your car is currently sitting in a weather sector (that
   stretch of the track). Explains the effect that applies to your play this turn. */
function weatherHereHTML(you){
  if(!G.weather || G.weather.key==='none') return "";
  const sec = curSector(you);
  if(!isWeatherSector(sec)) return "";
  const wDef = G.weather.def.sector || {};
  const bits=[];
  if(wDef.noCool)   bits.push(`<b>no cooling</b> allowed on this stretch`);
  if(wDef.cool)     bits.push(`<b style="color:var(--cool)">+${wDef.cool} Cooldown</b> available here`);
  if(wDef.slip)     bits.push(`<b style="color:#9fe08a">slipstream +${wDef.slip}</b> from here`);
  if(wDef.noSlip)   bits.push(`can't slipstream <b>out</b> of this sector`);
  if(wDef.lowSun)   bits.push(`blinded — you must play a <b style="color:var(--stress)">Stress ＋</b>`);
  if(wDef.aqua)     bits.push(`<b style="color:var(--heat)">aquaplaning</b> — over speed 10 into the next corner risks a spin-out`);
  if(wDef.wind){
    const cls = windClassAt(sec);
    if(cls==='tail')      bits.push(`<b>tailwind</b> — +1 corner speed here (weakens the slipstream)`);
    else if(cls==='head') bits.push(`<b>headwind</b> — −1 corner speed here (adds +2 to the slipstream)`);
    else                  bits.push(`crosswind — no corner-speed change`);
  }
  if(wDef.gust)     bits.push(`<b>gusts</b> — a card flips at the next corner check; a Speed card adds <b>+1 to +4</b> to your corner speed`);
  if(!bits.length) return "";
  return `<div class="forecast"><b>${G.weather.def.icon} ${G.weather.def.name} here:</b> ${bits.join(" · ")}.</div>`;
}
/* strip shown in Reveal/React: what the Check Corner step will bill you */
function heatForecastHTML(you, speed, crossings, label){
  const pre = label || "Corner check";
  const uniq = [...new Set(crossings)].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  if(uniq.length===0){
    const nc = nextCornerTotal(you.total);
    const nxt = nc===Infinity ? "no corners left before the flag"
      : `next corner line in ${nc-you.total} space${nc-you.total===1?"":"s"}, limit ${limitAt(nc)+(you.limitAdj||0)}`;
    return `<div class="forecast">${pre}: no corner line crossed yet — ${nxt}.</div>`;
  }
  const cost = cornerHeatCost(speed, uniq, you.limitAdj||0);
  const lims = uniq.map(ct=>limitAt(ct)+(you.limitAdj||0)).join(" & ");
  const wx = cornerWeatherWarnings(uniq, speed);
  const wxTxt = wx.length ? ` · ${wx.join(" · ")}` : "";
  if(cost===0)
    return `<div class="forecast">${pre}: limit-${lims} corner${uniq.length>1?"s":""} crossed at speed ${speed} — clean, <b>no Heat owed</b>.${wxTxt}</div>`;
  if(cost<=you.engine)
    return `<div class="forecast warn">${pre}: speed ${speed} vs limit ${lims} — <b>${cost} Heat</b> will leave the Engine (${you.engine} in Engine).${wxTxt}</div>`;
  return `<div class="forecast spin">${pre}: ${cost} Heat owed but only ${you.engine} in the Engine — ⚠ SPIN OUT!${wxTxt}</div>`;
}

/* "Are you sure?" spin-out gate — resolves true if the player commits anyway.
   Only shown when an action (Boost / slipstream) would actually spin the car out.
   Hardened so it can't get wedged: only one prompt is ever live, every open wires
   fresh handlers, tapping the dark backdrop counts as "back off", and any pending
   prompt is safely resolved (false) before a new one opens. */
let _confirmPending=false;
function confirmSpin(bodyHTML, {tag="Corner check", yes="Send it anyway"}={}){
  const m=$("#confirmModal");
  // if a prompt is somehow already open, cancel it cleanly first
  if(_confirmPending && m._resolve){ m._resolve(false); }
  return new Promise(resolve=>{
    _confirmPending=true;
    $("#cfTag").textContent=tag;
    $("#cfBody").innerHTML=bodyHTML;
    $("#cfYes").textContent=yes;
    m.style.display="flex";
    let done=false;
    const finish=v=>{
      if(done) return; done=true; _confirmPending=false; m._resolve=null;
      m.style.display="none";
      $("#cfYes").onclick=null; $("#cfNo").onclick=null; m.onclick=null;
      resolve(v);
    };
    m._resolve=()=>finish(false);       // used if a newer prompt supersedes this one
    $("#cfYes").onclick=e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); finish(true); };
    $("#cfNo").onclick =e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); finish(false); };
    // tap on the backdrop (outside the sheet) = back off
    m.onclick=e=>{ if(e.target===m) finish(false); };
  });
}

/* ---------------- track selection
   Switches the active track, re-labels the chrome, and rebuilds the board.
   Called from the track-select screen before the garage (setup) screen. */
function selectTrack(key){
  TRACK = TRACKS[key] || TRACKS.pukekohe;
  S = TRACK.spaces;
  syncLayout();     // layout / lapStart shorthands follow the loaded circuit
  // re-label all the Pukekohe-specific chrome for the chosen circuit
  const h1=$("#topbar h1"); if(h1) h1.innerHTML = `HEAT <span style="color:var(--purple-glow)">·</span> ${TRACK.name}`;
  const rl=$("#roundlbl"); if(rl) rl.textContent = TRACK.subtitle;
  const eb=$("#stage .eyebrow"); if(eb) eb.textContent = TRACK.eyebrow;
  const wc=$("#wchip"); if(wc){ wc.textContent=""; wc.hidden=true; }   // weather chip fills in once it's rolled
  buildTrack();     // rebuild geometry + art board for the new track
  renderCars({snap:true});     // clear any stale car layer
}

/* ---------------- deck (car) artwork preloader
   The car-select carousel used to paint the emoji fallback first and swap in
   the PNG whenever it happened to land, which read as a flash of the wrong art.
   Now every deck PNG is fetched once, up front (during the intro loader), and
   the carousel only draws an <img> for a class whose art is known-good. If a
   PNG genuinely isn't there, its status is "fail" and the emoji stands in — the
   original behaviour, just without the flicker. */
const DECK_ART = {};                 // class key -> "ok" | "fail"
let _deckArtPromise = null;
function preloadDeckArt(){
  if(_deckArtPromise) return _deckArtPromise;
  _deckArtPromise = Promise.all(VEHICLE_CLASSES.map(c => new Promise(res => {
    if(!c.art){ DECK_ART[c.key]="fail"; return res(); }
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload  = ()=>{ DECK_ART[c.key]="ok";   res(); };   // decoded + in cache
    im.onerror = ()=>{ DECK_ART[c.key]="fail"; res(); };   // no PNG → emoji fallback
    im.src = c.art;
  })));
  return _deckArtPromise;
}
const deckArtSettled = () => VEHICLE_CLASSES.every(c => DECK_ART[c.key]);
/* resolve after ms even if an image is still hanging, so the UI can't stall */
function withTimeout(p, ms){ return Promise.race([p, new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, ms))]); }
preloadDeckArt();                    // kick it off as soon as the script runs

/* ---------------- pre-race loading screen
   Sits between the setup summary and lights-out, and between championship
   races. `done` fires as the bar completes, so anything the race start throws
   up (the garage draft, for instance) is already painted underneath when the
   screen lifts. */
function showRaceLoading(title, sub, done){
  const el = $("#raceload");
  if(!el){ done(); return; }
  const lamps = [...el.querySelectorAll(".lamp")];
  const fill = $("#rlFill"), status = $("#rlStatus");
  $("#rlTitle").textContent = title || TRACK.name;
  $("#rlSub").textContent   = sub || "";
  lamps.forEach(l=>l.classList.remove("on"));
  fill.style.width = "0%";
  el.classList.remove("done");
  el.style.display = "flex";
  const LINES = ["Wheeling it out of the garage…","Heating the engine…",
                 "Checking tyre pressures…","Rolling onto the grid…","Formation lap…"];
  const t0 = performance.now(), DUR = 1900;
  let fired = false;
  const frame = (now)=>{
    const p = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / DUR);
    fill.style.width = (p*100).toFixed(1) + "%";
    lamps.forEach((l,i)=> l.classList.toggle("on", p >= (i+1)/5 - 0.001));
    status.textContent = LINES[Math.min(LINES.length-1, Math.floor(p*LINES.length))];
    if(p < 1){ requestAnimationFrame(frame); return; }
    status.textContent = "Lights out!";
    setTimeout(()=>{
      lamps.forEach(l=>l.classList.remove("on"));      // gantry blacks out = go
      if(!fired){ fired = true; done(); }              // build the race behind the screen
      el.classList.add("done");
      setTimeout(()=>{ el.style.display="none"; el.classList.remove("done"); }, 520);
    }, 420);
  };
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
