/* pretty name for a track key, even one that has no board built yet */
function trackName(key){
  const t=TRACKS[key];
  if(t) return t.name.replace(/\s*—.*/,"");
  return key.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+key.slice(1);
}
function cupMissing(cup){ return (cup.tracks||[]).filter(k=>!TRACKS[k]); }

/* Cup picker — sits between the title screen and the championship options. */
function showCupSelect(){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  const card=(cup)=>{
    const missing=cupMissing(cup);
    const rounds = cup.tracks
      ? cup.tracks.map((k,i)=>`<div>R${i+1} · <b class="${TRACKS[k]?"":"missing"}">${trackName(k)}</b>${TRACKS[k]?"":" — not built yet"}</div>`).join("")
      : `<div>Race 1–4 · your pick</div>`;
    return `<button class="trackcard cupcard" data-cup="${cup.key}">
      <div class="tc-art">${cup.icon}</div>
      <div class="tc-body">
        <div class="tc-name">${cup.name}</div>
        <div class="tc-meta">${CUP_RACES} races · ${cup.blurb}</div>
        <div class="tc-rounds">${rounds}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="tc-go">${cup.tracks && !missing.length ? "Season ▸" : "Build ▸"}</div>
    </button>`;
  };
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet rules">
    <h1>HEAT <span>· choose your cup</span></h1>
    <div class="tag">Every cup is four races. Points after the finale crown the champion.</div>   
    
    <div class="racerules">
   < ${CUPS.map(card).join("")}</div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:14px"><button class="act secondary" id="cupback">◂ Back</button></div>
  </div>`;
  $("#cupback").onclick=()=>{ el.style.display="none"; showModeSelect(); };
  el.querySelectorAll(".cupcard").forEach(b=>{
    b.onclick=()=>{
      const cup=CUPS.find(c=>c.key===b.dataset.cup);
      if(cup.tracks && !cupMissing(cup).length){
        G.cup={ key:cup.key, name:cup.name, order:cup.tracks.slice() };
        showChampSetup();          // cup (4 tracks) → rules → car → summary
      } else {
        showCupBuilder(cup);   // Custom Cup, or a cup with a circuit still to come
      }
    };
  });
}

/* Custom cup builder — tap four circuits; the tap order is the race order.
   Also used by any fixed cup that names a track this build doesn't have yet:
   its existing circuits come in pre-locked and only the gap is chosen. */
function showCupBuilder(cup){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  const missing = cupMissing(cup);
  // one slot per race: a fixed cup keeps its published order and only the
  // un-built rounds come up empty; the Custom Cup starts with four empties.
  const slots = cup.tracks
    ? cup.tracks.map(k=>TRACKS[k] ? k : null)
    : Array.from({length:CUP_RACES}, ()=>null);
  const locked = slots.filter(Boolean);
  const draw=()=>{
    const rail = slots.map((k,i)=>
      `<div class="${k?"set":""}">R${i+1}${k?" · "+trackName(k):""}</div>`).join("");
    const cards = Object.values(TRACKS).map(t=>{
      const i = slots.indexOf(t.key);
      const isLocked = locked.includes(t.key);
      return `<button class="trackcard pick ${i>=0?"on":""} ${isLocked?"locked":""}" data-key="${t.key}">
        <div class="pickbadge">${i>=0?"R"+(i+1):"+"}</div>
        <div class="tc-art">${trackThumb(t)}</div>
        <div class="tc-body">
          <div class="tc-name">${t.name}</div>
          <div class="tc-meta">${t.spaces} spaces · ${t.corners.length} corners${isLocked?" · locked in":""}</div>
        </div>
      </button>`;
    }).join("");
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
      <h1>HEAT <span>· ${cup.name.toLowerCase()}</span></h1>
      <div class="tag">${missing.length
        ? `${missing.map(trackName).join(" & ")} isn't built yet — pick a stand-in to complete the cup.`
        : "Tap four circuits. The order you tap them is the order you race them."}</div>
      <div class="cuprail">${rail}</div>
      <div class="tracklist" style="margin-top:12px">${cards}</div>
      <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:16px">
        <button class="act" id="cupgo" ${slots.every(Boolean)?"":"disabled style=\"opacity:.45\""}>Season ▸</button>
        <button class="act secondary" id="cupbld-back">◂ Back</button>
      </div>
      <div class="phase-hint" style="margin-top:12px">Tap a chosen circuit again to drop it. A circuit can only appear once in a cup.</div>
    </div>`;
    el.querySelectorAll(".trackcard.pick").forEach(b=>{
      b.onclick=()=>{
        const k=b.dataset.key;
        if(locked.includes(k)) return;                        // fixed rounds can't be dropped
        const at=slots.indexOf(k);
        if(at>=0) slots[at]=null;                             // tap again = drop it
        else {
          const free=slots.indexOf(null);
          if(free<0) return toast("Four circuits already picked — drop one first");
          slots[free]=k;                                      // fills the earliest empty race
        }
        draw();
      };
    });
    $("#cupbld-back").onclick=()=>showCupSelect();
    $("#cupgo").onclick=()=>{
      if(!slots.every(Boolean)) return;
      G.cup={ key:cup.key, name:cup.name, order:slots.slice() };
      showChampSetup();          // cup (4 tracks) → rules → car → summary
    };
  };
  draw();
}

/* ---------------- championship setup */
function showChampSetup(){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  const cup = G.cup || { key:"og", name:"Base Game", order:CUPS[0].tracks.slice() };
  G.cup = cup;
  // last-used picks come back with you if you step back from the car screen
  const ch0 = G.champ || {};
  const D = {
    bots:    ch0.botCount  != null ? ch0.botCount  : 5,
    laps:    G.laps        != null ? G.laps        : 1,
    ai:      G.aiMode      || "sim",
    diff:    G.difficulty  != null ? G.difficulty  : 1,
    upglvl:  G.upgLevel    || "reg",
    weather: ch0.weatherOn === false ? "off" : "on"
  };
  const sel = (a,b) => a===b ? ' class="sel"' : '';
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet rules">
    <h1>HEAT <span>· championship rules</span></h1>
    <div class="tag">${cup.name} — ${cup.order.map(trackName).join(" → ")}. Most points after the finale is champion.</div>
    <div class="racerules">
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Rival cars — up to 7, for an 8-car grid</div>
      <div class="seg" id="segbots" style="gap:5px">
        ${[1,2,3,4,5,6,7].map(v=>`<button data-v="${v}"${sel(v,D.bots)}>${v}</button>`).join("")}
      </div></div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Laps per race</div>
      <div class="seg" id="seglaps">
        <button data-v="1"${sel(1,D.laps)}>1 · Sprint</button><button data-v="2"${sel(2,D.laps)}>2 · Full</button>
      </div></div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Rival AI</div>
      <div class="seg" id="segai">
        <button data-v="sim"${sel("sim",D.ai)}>Full drivers</button><button data-v="legend"${sel("legend",D.ai)}>Legends</button>
      </div></div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Rival pace / skill</div>
      <div class="seg" id="segdiff">
        <button data-v="0"${sel(0,D.diff)}>Davidson</button><button data-v="1"${sel(1,D.diff)}>Rookie</button><button data-v="2"${sel(2,D.diff)}>Pro</button><button data-v="3"${sel(3,D.diff)}>Legend</button>
      </div></div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Upgrade market (1 Upgrade drafted per race)</div>
      <div class="seg" id="segupglvl">
        <button data-v="reg"${sel("reg",D.upglvl)}>Regular only</button><button data-v="all"${sel("all",D.upglvl)}>Regular + Advanced</button>
      </div></div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Weather</div>
      <div class="seg" id="segweather">
        <button data-v="on"${sel("on",D.weather)}>Random each race</button><button data-v="off"${sel("off",D.weather)}>Dry season</button>
      </div></div>
    <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="champstart">Pick your car ▸</button></div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="act secondary" id="champback">◂ Change cup</button></div>
  </div>`;
  for(const seg of ["segbots","seglaps","segai","segdiff","segupglvl","segweather"]){
    $("#"+seg).addEventListener("click",e=>{
      if(e.target.tagName!=="BUTTON") return;
      [...$("#"+seg).children].forEach(b=>b.classList.remove("sel"));
      e.target.classList.add("sel");
    });
  }
  $("#champback").onclick=()=>{ showCupSelect(); };
  $("#champstart").onclick=()=>{
    G.hotseat=false;
    const botCount=+$("#segbots .sel").dataset.v;
    G.laps=+$("#seglaps .sel").dataset.v;
    G.aiMode=$("#segai .sel").dataset.v;
    G.difficulty=+$("#segdiff .sel").dataset.v;
    G.upgLevel=$("#segupglvl .sel").dataset.v;
    G.garage="draft";                         // championship always drafts (1 card/race)
    const weatherOn=$("#segweather .sel").dataset.v==="on";
    // build the persistent roster (survives across races: upgrades + points)
    const roster=[{name:"No. 17 — You",color:"#8a5cf6",isBot:false,upgrades:[],points:0,lastPos:0,sponsors:[]}];
    for(let i=0;i<botCount;i++){
      const b=BOT_POOL[i];
      roster.push({name:b.name,color:b.color,isBot:true,botIndex:i,upgrades:[],points:0,lastPos:0,sponsors:[]});
    }
    G.champ={
      cupKey:cup.key, cupName:cup.name,
      order:cup.order.slice(),                 // four track keys, in race order
      raceIdx:0, roster, weatherOn, botCount,
      baseLaps:G.laps,                         // Event cards nudge laps per race off this base
      sponsorDeck:buildSponsorDeck(),          // 7 copies of each sponsor, shuffled (rulebook p.8)
      sponsorUsed:[],                          // played-this-race pile; reshuffled in when the deck runs dry
      eventOrder:shuffle(EVENT_CARDS.map(e=>e.key)) // custom season: random Event per race (rulebook p.10)
    };
    showDeckSelect(showChampSummary, showChampSetup);   // car select → season summary
  };
}

/* ---------------- championship summary — the last card before race 1 */
function showChampSummary(){
  const ch=G.champ, cup=G.cup;
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  const c = classByKey(G.playerCls||"ghost");
  const carArt = DECK_ART[c.key]==="ok"
    ? `<img src="${c.art}" alt="${esc(c.name)}">`
    : classCarSVG(c.key);
  const rounds = ch.order.map((k,i)=>{
    const t=TRACKS[k];
    return `<div class="standrow"><div class="p">R${i+1}</div>
      <div>${trackName(k)}</div>
      <div style="margin-left:auto;color:var(--cream-dim);font-size:12px">${t?`${t.spaces} spaces · ${t.corners.length} corners`:""}</div>
    </div>`;
  }).join("");
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet rules">
    <h1>HEAT <span>· ${esc(cup.name.toLowerCase())}</span></h1>
    <div class="tag">Four races, one title. Points after the finale crown the champion.</div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Season calendar</div>${rounds}</div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Your car — it carries its Upgrades all season</div>
      <div class="deckcard" style="padding:12px">
        <div class="dk-art" style="height:150px">${carArt}</div>
        <div class="dk-name">${esc(c.name)}</div>
        <div class="dk-tag">${esc(c.tag)}</div>
        <div class="dk-stats">${deckStatPills(c).map(p=>`<span class="${p.k||""}">${p.t}</span>`).join("")}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Rules</div>
      ${summaryRow("Grid", `${ch.botCount+1} cars — you + ${ch.botCount} rival${ch.botCount>1?"s":""}`)}
      ${summaryRow("Laps per race", `${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""}`)}
      ${summaryRow("Rival AI", G.aiMode==="sim" ? "Full drivers" : "Legends module")}
      ${summaryRow("Pace / skill", DIFF_NAMES[G.difficulty]||"Rookie")}
      ${summaryRow("Garage", `Draft 1 Upgrade per race (${G.upgLevel==="all"?"Regular + Advanced":"Regular"})`)}
      ${summaryRow("Weather", ch.weatherOn ? "🎲 Random each race" : "Dry season")}
      ${summaryRow("Points", CHAMP_POINTS.slice(0,6).join(" / ")+" …")}
    </div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:14px"><button class="act" id="csGo">Start the season ▸</button></div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="act secondary" id="csCar">◂ Change car</button></div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="act secondary" id="csRules">◂ Change rules</button></div>
  </div>`;
  $("#csCar").onclick   = ()=>showDeckSelect(showChampSummary, showChampSetup);
  $("#csRules").onclick = ()=>showChampSetup();
  $("#csGo").onclick    = ()=>{
    el.style.display="none";
    showRaceLoading(trackName(ch.order[0]),
      `${cup.name} · Race 1 of ${ch.order.length}`, ()=>startChampRace());
  };
}

function startChampRace(){
  const ch=G.champ;
  // snapshot each driver's upgrades before this race's draft (for a clean restart)
  ch.roster.forEach(r=>{ r.upgradesBeforeRace=(r.upgrades||[]).slice(); r._draftedThisRace=false; });
  selectTrack(ch.order[ch.raceIdx]);   // sets TRACK + rebuilds board
  // ---- Event card for this race (custom season: pre-shuffled order) ----
  G.event = EVENT_CARDS.find(e=>e.key===ch.eventOrder[ch.raceIdx % ch.eventOrder.length]) || null;
  const ev = G.event || {};
  G.laps = Math.max(1, (ch.baseLaps!=null?ch.baseLaps:G.laps) + (ev.laps||0));
  G._lap1Count = 0;
  // Press Corner standee(s): event letters wrap to this track's corner count
  G.pressCorners = (ev.press||[]).map(i=>i % TRACK.corners.length)
                                 .filter((v,i,a)=>a.indexOf(v)===i);
  // weather for this race
  if(ch.weatherOn){ const keys=Object.keys(WEATHER_TYPES).filter(k=>k!=="none"); G.weatherKey=keys[Math.floor(Math.random()*keys.length)]; }
  else G.weatherKey="none";
  setupWeather(G.weatherKey);
  // Event car-setup rides the same pool modifiers as weather, so the heat
  // audit and deck builder pick it up for every driver automatically.
  WEATHER_HEAT_MOD   += ev.setupHeat   || 0;
  WEATHER_STRESS_MOD += ev.setupStress || 0;
  drawCornerBadges();   // repaint so the Press standees show right away
  // rebuild players from the roster; keep their accumulated upgrades
  G.players=[];
  ch.roster.forEach(r=>{
    r.cls = r.cls || (r.isBot ? randomClassKey() : (G.playerCls||"ghost"));  // garage sticks for the season
    let p;
    if(!r.isBot){ p=makeHuman(r.upgrades.slice(), null, r.cls); }
    else { p=makeBot(r.botIndex, G.aiMode==="sim"? r.upgrades.slice() : undefined, r.cls); }
    p._roster=r; G.players.push(p);
  });
  // grid: race 1 random; later races by standings (most points = pole), tiebreak last race pos
  let grid;
  if(ch.raceIdx===0){ grid=shuffle([...G.players]); }
  else {
    grid=[...G.players].sort((a,b)=>
      (b._roster.points-a._roster.points) || (a._roster.lastPos-b._roster.lastPos) || (Math.random()-0.5));
  }
  const slots=[]; for(let i=0;i<8;i++) slots.push({t:-(1+Math.floor(i/2)), s:i%2});
  grid.forEach((p,i)=>{ p.total=slots[i].t; p.spot=slots[i].s; p.gridPos=i; });
  G.legendDeck=shuffle([...LEGEND_CARDS]);
  G.round=0; G.finishOrder=[]; G.replay=[];
  rollRoadConditions();
  renderCars({snap:true});
  // draft one upgrade (championship rules), then start
  runDraft(()=>{
    for(const p of G.players){
      const ids = p.upgrades || [];
      if(!p.isBot || p.sim) equipDriver(p, ids);
      // Sponsorship cards carried over from earlier races are shuffled into
      // the draw deck with everything else (rulebook p.9, race setup).
      const carried = (p._roster.sponsors||[]).splice(0);
      if(carried.length && (!p.isBot || p.sim)){
        carried.forEach(id=>p.deck.push(makeSponsorCard(sponsorById(id))));
        shuffle(p.deck);
        if(!p.isBot) log(`${carried.length} carried-over Sponsorship card${carried.length>1?"s":""} shuffled into your deck.`,"me");
      }
      p._roster.upgrades = ids.slice();   // persist newly drafted card
      p._roster._draftedThisRace = true;
    }
    finishChampRaceStart();
  }, {champ:true});
}
/* ---------------- pre-race briefing
   Modal shown at lights-out with this race's Event card (Championship) and
   the Weather card, so the driver can actually read them before the first
   gear shift. The race is held until it's dismissed. Card scans come from
   assets/cards/events/ and assets/cards/weather/; if a scan is missing the
   card is drawn as a styled text face instead. */
function showRaceBriefing(done){
  const ev=G.event;
  const wx=(G.weather && G.weather.key!=="none") ? G.weather : null;
  if(!ev && !wx){ done(); return; }
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet" style="max-width:480px; text-align:center">
    <h2>Race <span>briefing</span></h2>
    <div class="tag">${esc(TRACK.name)} · read before lights out</div>
    <div style="display:flex; gap:14px; justify-content:center; align-items:flex-start; flex-wrap:wrap; margin:4px 0">
      ${ev?`<div class="brief-card" id="briefEvent"><div class="bc-face bc-event">
          <div class="bc-kind">📋 Event</div>
          <div class="bc-name">${esc(ev.name)}</div>
          ${ev.flavor?`<div class="bc-flavor">${esc(ev.flavor)}</div>`:""}
          <div class="bc-text">${esc(ev.text)}</div>
          <div class="bc-foot">${ev.sponsor?`🤝 ${ev.sponsor} Sponsorship card${ev.sponsor===1?"":"s"} dealt`:""}${
            ev.press&&ev.press.length?`${ev.sponsor?"<br>":""}📸 Press at corner ${ev.press.map(i=>"ABCDE"[i]||i+1).join(" & ")}`:""}</div>
        </div></div>`:""}
      ${wx?`<div class="brief-card" id="briefWx"><div class="bc-face bc-wx">
          <div class="bc-kind">${wx.def.icon} Weather</div>
          <div class="bc-name">${esc(wx.def.name)}</div>
          <div class="bc-text">${esc(wx.def.blurb)}</div>
        </div></div>`:""}
    </div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:16px; justify-content:center">
      <button class="act" id="briefGo">Start race ▸</button>
    </div></div>`;
  // swap the text faces for scans where they exist
  if(ev) firstArt(eventArtCandidates(ev)).then(u=>{
    const h=$("#briefEvent");
    if(u && h){ h.innerHTML=`<img src="${u}" alt="${esc(ev.name)}">`; h.classList.add("hasart"); }
  });
  if(wx) firstArt(weatherArtCandidates(wx.key)).then(u=>{
    const h=$("#briefWx");
    if(u && h){ h.innerHTML=`<img src="${u}" alt="${esc(wx.def.name)}">`; h.classList.add("hasart"); }
  });
  $("#briefGo").onclick=()=>{ el.style.display="none"; done(); };
}

function finishChampRaceStart(){
  const you=curHuman();
  applyWeatherCarSetup(you);
  for(const p of G.players) if(p.sim) applyWeatherCarSetup(p);
  // Event sponsorships are dealt straight into the starting hand, then the
  // draw tops everyone up to the hand size (which the Event can change).
  const ev=G.event;
  if(ev && ev.sponsor){
    gainSponsor(you, ev.sponsor, "race sponsors");
    if(G.aiMode==="sim") for(const p of G.players) if(p.sim) gainSponsor(p, ev.sponsor);
  }
  drawToHand(you, handSize()-you.hand.length);
  if(G.aiMode==="sim") for(const p of G.players) if(p.sim) drawToHand(p, handSize()-p.hand.length);
  const ch=G.champ;
  log(`═══ ${ch.cupName||"Championship"} · Race ${ch.raceIdx+1} of ${ch.order.length} · ${TRACK.name} ═══`,"me");
  if(ev){
    log(`📋 EVENT — ${ev.name}: ${ev.text}`,"warn");
    if(G.pressCorners.length) log(`📸 Press Corner${G.pressCorners.length>1?"s":""}: ${G.pressCorners.map(i=>`corner ${i+1} (limit ${TRACK.corners[i].limit})`).join(" & ")} — slipstream across the line, or beat the limit by 2+, without spinning out, to earn a Sponsorship card.`);
    toast(`📋 ${ev.name}`);
  }
  logWeatherSetup();
  renderCars(); renderGauges(); updateWeatherNote();
  showRaceBriefing(()=>startRound());   // Event card must be read & closed first
}

/* championship standings screen after a race */
function champRaceOver(order){
  const ch=G.champ;
  const ev=G.event||{};
  // DQ'd drivers rank behind everyone who wasn't black-flagged
  order=[...order].sort((a,b)=> (a.dq?1:0)-(b.dq?1:0));
  order.forEach((p,i)=>{
    let pts = p.dq ? 0 : (CHAMP_POINTS[i]||0);
    if(ev.top3bonus && !p.dq && i<3) pts += 1;             // Double Points Purse
    p._roster.points += pts; p._roster.lastPos=i;
  });
  if(ev.top3bonus) log("📋 Double Points Purse — the podium banks +1 point each.","me");
  // Sponsorship housekeeping (rulebook p.9): unused cards travel with the
  // driver to the next race; cards played this race go back into the deck.
  for(const p of G.players){
    const r=p._roster; if(!r) continue;
    const mine=[];
    for(const pile of [p.hand,p.deck,p.discard,p.play])
      for(const c of (pile||[])) if(isUpg(c)&&c.def.sponsor) mine.push(c.def.id);
    r.sponsors=mine;
    if(!p.isBot && mine.length) log(`You carry ${mine.length} unused Sponsorship card${mine.length>1?"s":""} to the next race.`,"me");
  }
  ch.sponsorDeck.push(...ch.sponsorUsed.splice(0)); shuffle(ch.sponsorDeck);
  const isFinale = ch.raceIdx >= ch.order.length-1;
  const standings=[...ch.roster].sort((a,b)=> b.points-a.points || a.lastPos-b.lastPos);
  const nameOf = r => r.isBot ? r.name : "No. 17 — You";
  const raceRows=order.map((p,i)=>`
    <div class="standrow"><div class="p">P${i+1}</div>
      <div class="dot" style="background:${p.color}"></div>
      <div>${nameOf(p)}</div>
      <div style="margin-left:auto;color:var(--purple-glow);font-weight:800">+${CHAMP_POINTS[i]||0}</div>
    </div>`).join("");
  const tableRows=standings.map((r,i)=>`
    <div class="standrow"><div class="p">${i+1}</div>
      <div class="dot" style="background:${r.color}"></div>
      <div>${nameOf(r)}</div>
      <div style="margin-left:auto;font-weight:800">${r.points} pts</div>
    </div>`).join("");
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex"; el.onclick=null;
  if(isFinale){
    const champ=standings[0];
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
      <h2>${champ.isBot?"Season over":"🏆 CHAMPION!"}</h2>
      <div class="tag">${champ.isBot?`${champ.name} wins the ${ch.cupName||"championship"}`:`You win the ${ch.cupName||"championship"}!`} · after ${ch.order.length} races</div>
      <div class="olbl" style="margin:10px 0 2px">Final standings</div>
      ${tableRows}
      <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:18px">
        ${replayBtnHTML()}
        <button class="act" id="champNew">New season</button>
        <button class="act secondary" id="champMenu">Main menu</button>
      </div></div>`;
    wireReplayBtn();
    $("#champNew").onclick=()=>{ el.style.display="none"; showCupSelect(); };
    $("#champMenu").onclick=()=>{ el.style.display="none"; showModeSelect(); };
  } else {
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
      <h2>Race ${ch.raceIdx+1} of ${ch.order.length}</h2>
      <div class="tag">${ch.cupName||"Championship"} · ${TRACK.name}</div>
      ${raceRows}
      <div class="olbl" style="margin:12px 0 2px">Championship standings</div>
      ${tableRows}
      ${(()=>{ const ne=EVENT_CARDS.find(e=>e.key===ch.eventOrder[(ch.raceIdx+1)%ch.eventOrder.length]);
        return ne?`<div class="olbl" style="margin:12px 0 2px">Next race event</div>
        <div class="phase-hint" style="margin:0">📋 <b>${esc(ne.name)}</b> — ${esc(ne.text)} <span style="color:#6f6584">(${ne.sponsor} sponsorship card${ne.sponsor===1?"":"s"} dealt${ne.press&&ne.press.length?", press at corner "+ne.press.map(i=>"ABCDE"[i]||i+1).join(" & "):""})</span></div>`:""; })()}
      <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:16px">
        ${replayBtnHTML()}
        <button class="act" id="champNext">Next race — ${trackName(ch.order[ch.raceIdx+1])} ▸</button>
      </div></div>`;
    wireReplayBtn();
    $("#champNext").onclick=()=>{
      ch.raceIdx++;
      if(window.HeatAudio && window.HeatAudio.race) window.HeatAudio.race();
      el.style.display="none";
      showRaceLoading(trackName(ch.order[ch.raceIdx]),
        `${ch.cupName||"Championship"} · Race ${ch.raceIdx+1} of ${ch.order.length}`,
        ()=>startChampRace());
    };
  }
}
/* kept for any older call sites — trackName() handles every registered circuit */
function TRACKS_NAME(key){ return trackName(key); }

/* ---------------- deck select (the garage carousel)
   Sits between "which circuit / which cup" and the setup sheet. One deck on
   screen at a time; swipe the card or tap the arrows (or the dots, or the
   keyboard) to rotate through the garages. Locking one in just sets
   G.playerCls — every later screen (setup, championship) already reads it.

   Avatar artwork per deck lives at VEHICLE_CLASSES[].art; if the PNG is
   missing the class emoji shows through instead, so nothing breaks. */
function deckStatPills(c){
  const tot  = c.deck.reduce((s,n,i)=>s+n*(i+1),0);   // 30 = baseline
  const heatPool = 6 + (c.tank||0);                   // 6 Heat = baseline pool
  const la   = c.limitAdj||0;
  const sign = v => (v>0?"+":"")+v;
  const pills = [
    {t:`Deck ${c.deck.map((n,i)=>n+"\u00d7"+(i+1)).join(" ")}`},
    {t:`Speed total ${tot}`,  k: tot>30 ? "up" : tot<30 ? "dn" : ""},
    {t:`${heatPool}-Heat`,    k: (c.tank||0)>0 ? "up" : (c.tank||0)<0 ? "dn" : ""},
    {t: la ? `Corner limits ${sign(la)}` : "Standard corner limits", k: la>0?"up" : la<0?"dn" : ""},
    {t:`Slipstream ${c.slipBase}`, k: c.slipBase>2 ? "up" : ""},
    {t:`Cool ${Object.entries(c.cool||{}).map(([g,v])=>"G"+g+"\u00b7"+v).join(" ")||"\u2014"}`}
  ];
  if(c.stress)     pills.push({t:`${sign(c.stress)} stress card`,      k: c.stress>0 ? "dn":"up"});
  if(c.spinStress) pills.push({t:`Spin out +${c.spinStress} stress`,    k:"dn"});
  return pills;
}
/* ---------------- CAR SELECT
   The deck carousel, restyled as a console-game car-select screen: dark
   studio stage, number roundel, purple accent, five spec bars derived from
   the class data, and a bottom action bar. Behaviour is unchanged — swipe,
   arrows, dots, arrow keys and Enter all still rotate / lock in.       */

/* map a class value onto a 1–5 bar */
function csBar(v, lo, hi){
  if(hi===lo) return 3;
  const t=(v-lo)/(hi-lo);
  return Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(t*4)+1));
}
/* the five axes shown as bars. Every one is read straight off the class
   definition, so a new garage in VEHICLE_CLASSES needs no work here. */
function deckStatBars(c){
  const tot  = c.deck.reduce((s,n,i)=>s+n*(i+1),0);      // 30 = baseline
  const heat = 6 + (c.tank||0);
  const cool = Object.values(c.cool||{}).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
  const la   = c.limitAdj||0;
  const slip = c.slipBase||2;
  return [
    {label:"Top speed",  n:csBar(tot,26,35),  note:`${tot} deck total`},
    {label:"Engine",     n:csBar(heat,4,9),   note:`${heat} Heat`},
    {label:"Cornering",  n:csBar(la,-1,1),    note: la ? `limits ${la>0?"+":""}${la}` : "standard limits"},
    {label:"Cooling",    n:csBar(cool,1,7),   note:`${cool} Cooldown`},
    {label:"Slipstream", n:csBar(slip,1,4),   note:`${slip} spaces`}
  ];
}

function showDeckSelect(next, back){
  ensureCarSelectCSS();
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  const N=VEHICLE_CLASSES.length;
  let idx=VEHICLE_CLASSES.findIndex(c=>c.key===(G.playerCls||"ghost"));
  if(idx<0) idx=0;
  let specsOpen=false;
  const where = (G.mode==="champ" && G.cup)
    ? `${G.cup.name} \u00b7 ${CUP_RACES} races` : TRACK.name;

  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet carsel">
    <div class="cs-top">
      <div class="cs-titles">
        <h1 class="cs-h1">Car select</h1>
        <div class="cs-rule"></div>
        <div class="cs-where">${esc(where)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="cs-num">17</div>
    </div>

    <div class="cs-body">
      <div class="cs-panel" id="deckpanel"></div>
      <div class="cs-stagewrap">
        <div class="cs-stage" id="deckstage"></div>
        <button class="csarrow" id="deckprev" aria-label="Previous deck">\u2039</button>
        <button class="csarrow" id="decknext" aria-label="Next deck">\u203a</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="deckdots" id="deckdots"></div>

    <div class="cs-actions">
      <button id="deckback">\u2039 Back</button>
      <button id="deckspecs">Specs</button>
      <button id="deckgo">\u2713 Select</button>
    </div>

    <div class="phase-hint" style="margin-top:12px">Each garage trades speed against Heat and cornering \u2014 they're meant to cancel out, so pick the one that suits how you like to drive. Rivals draw their own garages.</div>
  </div>`;

  const stage=$("#deckstage"), panel=$("#deckpanel"), dots=$("#deckdots");
  let ready=false;                        // true once every deck PNG has resolved

  const paint=(dir)=>{
    const c=VEHICLE_CLASSES[idx];
    const anim = dir==="r" ? "cs-slide-r" : dir==="l" ? "cs-slide-l" : "";
    // only draw the <img> for art we've already fetched — a class whose PNG is
    // missing keeps the car sprite, everything else goes straight to the picture.
    const ok = DECK_ART[c.key]==="ok";
    stage.innerHTML=`<div class="cs-plate"></div>
      ${ok ? `<img class="${anim}" src="${c.art}" alt="${esc(c.name)}">`
           : `<div class="${anim}" style="width:84%">${classCarSVG(c.key)}</div>`}`;

    panel.className="cs-panel "+anim;
    panel.innerHTML=`
      <h2 class="cs-name">${esc(c.name)}</h2>
      <div class="cs-era">${esc(c.tag)}</div>
      <div class="cs-blurb">${esc(c.blurb)}</div>
      <div class="cs-bars">${deckStatBars(c).map(b=>`
        <div class="cs-bar">
          <div class="lbl"><span>${b.label}</span><em>${esc(b.note)}</em></div>
          <div class="trk"><div class="fil" style="width:${b.n*20}%"></div></div>
        </div>`).join("")}</div>
      <div class="cs-specs${specsOpen?" on":""}">
        <div class="dk-stats">${deckStatPills(c).map(p=>`<span class="${p.k||""}">${p.t}</span>`).join("")}</div>
      </div>`;

    dots.innerHTML=VEHICLE_CLASSES.map((_,i)=>
      `<button data-i="${i}" class="${i===idx?"on":""}" aria-label="Deck ${i+1}"></button>`).join("");
    dots.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
      const i=+b.dataset.i; if(i===idx) return;
      const d=i>idx?"r":"l"; idx=i; paint(d);
    });
  };
  const rotate=(step)=>{ if(!ready) return; idx=(idx+step+N)%N; paint(step>0?"r":"l"); };

  // hold the carousel on a quiet placeholder until the art is in (it usually
  // already is — the preload starts on the loading screen); 4s hard cap so a
  // stalled request can never trap the player here.
  if(deckArtSettled()){ ready=true; paint(); }
  else {
    stage.innerHTML=`<div class="dk-fallback">\u{1F527}</div>`;
    panel.innerHTML=`<h2 class="cs-name">Opening the garage\u2026</h2>
      <div class="cs-era">Warming up the cars</div>`;
    withTimeout(preloadDeckArt(), 4000).then(()=>{
      VEHICLE_CLASSES.forEach(c=>{ if(!DECK_ART[c.key]) DECK_ART[c.key]="fail"; });
      ready=true; paint();
    });
  }

  $("#deckprev").onclick=()=>rotate(-1);
  $("#decknext").onclick=()=>rotate(+1);
  $("#deckspecs").onclick=e=>{
    specsOpen=!specsOpen;
    e.currentTarget.classList.toggle("on", specsOpen);
    const box=panel.querySelector(".cs-specs"); if(box) box.classList.toggle("on", specsOpen);
  };

  // swipe / drag across the stage (mouse and touch, via pointer events)
  let x0=null, y0=null;
  stage.addEventListener("pointerdown", e=>{ x0=e.clientX; y0=e.clientY; });
  stage.addEventListener("pointerup", e=>{
    if(x0===null) return;
    const dx=e.clientX-x0, dy=e.clientY-y0;
    x0=null;
    if(Math.abs(dx)>36 && Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)) rotate(dx<0 ? +1 : -1);   // drag left = next
  });
  stage.addEventListener("pointercancel", ()=>{ x0=null; });

  // arrow keys, while this screen is the one on show
  const onKey=e=>{
    if(e.key==="ArrowLeft")  rotate(-1);
    else if(e.key==="ArrowRight") rotate(+1);
    else if(e.key==="Enter") lockIn();
  };
  window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  const leave=fn=>{ window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey); fn(); };
  const lockIn=()=>leave(()=>{ G.playerCls=VEHICLE_CLASSES[idx].key; next(); });

  $("#deckgo").onclick=lockIn;
  $("#deckback").onclick=()=>leave(back);
}

function trackBrief(t){
  return t.brief || t.tagline || t.eyebrow || t.subtitle || "A hand-built fan board.";
}

/* Track picker — sits between the title screen and the garage/setup sheet.
   The sheet is pinned to the viewport: only the list of circuits scrolls.
   Tapping a circuit opens a brief (outline + a line or two) with Cancel / Next,
   so nothing is locked in until the driver actually confirms it. */
function showTrackSelect(){
  const el = $("#setup");
  el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;   // always open on the first card
  const card = (t)=>{
    const ch = chicaneCount(t);
    return `
    <button class="trackcard" data-key="${t.key}">
      <div class="tc-art">${trackThumb(t)}</div>
      <div class="tc-body">
        <div class="tc-name">${t.name}</div>
        <div class="tc-meta">${t.spaces} spaces · ${t.corners.length} corners${ch?` · ${ch} chicane${ch>1?"s":""}`:""} · ${t.subtitle}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="tc-go">Brief ▸</div>
    </button>`;
  };
  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="sheet tspicker">
      <h1>HEAT <span>· choose your circuit</span></h1>
      <div class="tag">${Object.keys(TRACKS).length} hand-built fan boards. Scroll the list — pick where the lights go out.</div>
      <div class="tracklist">
        ${Object.values(TRACKS).map(card).join("")}
      </div>
      <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:14px"><button class="act secondary" id="tsback">◂ Back</button></div>
    </div>`;
  $("#tsback").onclick = ()=>{ closeTrackModal(); el.style.display="none"; showModeSelect(); };
  el.querySelectorAll(".trackcard").forEach(b=>{
    b.onclick = ()=> openTrackModal(b.dataset.key);
  });
}

/* the circuit brief — outline, stats, a sentence or two, then cancel / next */
function closeTrackModal(){
  const m=$("#trackModal");
  if(m._onkey) window.removeEventListener("keydown", m._onkey);
  m._onkey=null; m.style.display="none"; m.onclick=null;
  $("#tmCancel").onclick=null; $("#tmNext").onclick=null;
}
function openTrackModal(key){
  const t = TRACKS[key]; if(!t) return;
  const m = $("#trackModal");
  const ch = chicaneCount(t);
  const tightest = t.corners.length ? Math.min(...t.corners.map(c=>c.limit)) : null;
  $("#tmEyebrow").textContent = t.eyebrow || t.subtitle || "";
  $("#tmName").textContent    = t.name;
  $("#tmArt").innerHTML       = trackThumb(t);
  $("#tmMeta").textContent    =
    `${t.spaces} spaces · ${t.corners.length} corners${ch?` · ${ch} chicane${ch>1?"s":""}`:""}`
    + (tightest!==null?` · tightest limit ${tightest}`:"")
    + ` · GP ${t.defaultLaps} lap${t.defaultLaps>1?"s":""}`;
  $("#tmBrief").textContent   = trackBrief(t);
  m.style.display="flex";

  const go = ()=>{
    closeTrackModal();
    selectTrack(key);
    // Pass & Play picks a garage per driver on its own sheet; solo racers set
    // the race rules first, then choose a car, then confirm on the summary.
    if(G.hotseat) showHotseatSetup();
    else showSetup();
  };
  $("#tmCancel").onclick = e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); closeTrackModal(); };
  $("#tmNext").onclick   = e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); go(); };
  m.onclick = e=>{ if(e.target===m) closeTrackModal(); };          // backdrop = cancel
  m._onkey = e=>{ if(e.key==="Escape") closeTrackModal(); else if(e.key==="Enter") go(); };
  window.addEventListener("keydown", m._onkey);
}

/* ---------------- Pass & Play setup — 2–4 local drivers on one device */
const HOTSEAT_COLORS=[
  {name:"Purple", hex:"#8a5cf6"}, {name:"Green",  hex:"#009E3B"},
  {name:"Orange", hex:"#FF6B00"}, {name:"Yellow", hex:"#FFED00"},
  {name:"Red",    hex:"#FF0033"}, {name:"Blue",   hex:"#0076C0"},
  {name:"White",   hex:"#FFFFFF"}, {name:"Pink", hex:"#FF70A6"}
];
const HOTSEAT_NUMS=[17,5,22,8];
function showHotseatSetup(){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex";
  // sticky state so re-renders (player-count change) keep typed names & colours
  const st = G._hsDraft || (G._hsDraft = {
    count:2,
    players:[0,1,2,3].map(i=>({name:"", color:HOTSEAT_COLORS[i].hex, cls:"ghost"})),
    laps:1, bots:0, diff:1, garage:"none", weather:"none"
  });
  const esc = s => s.replace(/[<>&"]/g, ch=>({"<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","&":"&amp;",'"':"&quot;"}[ch]));
  const draw=()=>{
    if(st.laps!==1) st.laps = gpLaps();     // GP distance follows whichever circuit is loaded
    const takenBy = i => st.players.slice(0,st.count).findIndex((p,j)=>j!==i && p.color===st.players[i].color);
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet rules">
      <h1>HEAT <span>· pass &amp; play — ${TRACK.name}</span></h1>
      <div class="tag">2–4 drivers on this device. Enter a name and pick a car colour each — the game tells you when to pass it on.</div>
      <div class="racerules">
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Local drivers</div>
        <div class="seg" id="hscount">${[2,3,4].map(v=>`<button data-v="${v}"${v===st.count?' class="sel"':''}>${v}</button>`).join("")}</div></div>
      <div id="hsplayers"></div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Race distance</div>
        <div class="seg" id="hslaps">
          <button data-v="1"${st.laps===1?' class="sel"':''}>1 · Sprint</button>
          <button data-v="${gpLaps()}"${st.laps!==1?' class="sel"':''}>${gpLaps()} · Grand Prix</button>
        </div></div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">AI rivals to fill the grid (optional)</div>
        <div class="seg" id="hsbots" style="gap:5px">${[0,1,2,3,4].map(v=>`<button data-v="${v}"${v===st.bots?' class="sel"':''}>${v}</button>`).join("")}</div></div>
      <div class="optgroup" id="hsdiffgroup" style="${st.bots?'':'display:none'}"><div class="olbl">Rival pace / skill</div>
        <div class="seg" id="hsdiff">
          ${["Davidson","Rookie","Pro","Legend"].map((n,i)=>`<button data-v="${i}"${i===st.diff?' class="sel"':''}>${n}</button>`).join("")}
        </div></div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Garage</div>
        <div class="seg" id="hsgarage">
          <button data-v="none"${st.garage==="none"?' class="sel"':''}>Closed · stock car</button>
          <button data-v="random"${st.garage==="random"?' class="sel"':''}>Random · 3 Upgrades</button>
        </div></div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Weather &amp; road conditions</div>
        <select id="hsweather" style="width:100%;padding:11px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:2px solid #4a3f63;background:#171221;color:var(--cream);font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:15px;letter-spacing:.5px">
          <option value="none"${st.weather==="none"?" selected":""}>Dry — no weather</option>
          <option value="random"${st.weather==="random"?" selected":""}>🎲 Random weather</option>
          ${Object.keys(WEATHER_TYPES).filter(k=>k!=="none").map(k=>`<option value="${k}"${st.weather===k?" selected":""}>${WEATHER_TYPES[k].icon} ${WEATHER_TYPES[k].name}</option>`).join("")}
        </select></div>
      <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="hsstart">Start the race</button></div>
      <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="act secondary" id="hsback">◂ Change circuit</button></div>
      <div class="phase-hint" style="margin-top:14px">Steps 1–2 (gears &amp; cards) go round the table first, then each driver plays their turn out in track order. A hand-over screen hides your cards between drivers.</div>
    </div>`;
    // player rows: name input + colour swatches
    const wrap=el.querySelector("#hsplayers");
    for(let i=0;i<st.count;i++){
      const row=document.createElement("div");
      row.className="optgroup";
      row.innerHTML=`<div class="olbl">Driver ${i+1} — No. ${HOTSEAT_NUMS[i]}</div>
        <input class="hsname" data-i="${i}" maxlength="14" placeholder="Player ${i+1}" value="${esc(st.players[i].name)}"
          style="width:100%;padding:10px;border-radius:8px;border:2px solid #4a3f63;background:#171221;color:var(--cream);font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:15px;letter-spacing:.5px">
        <div class="seg" style="margin-top:7px;gap:6px">${HOTSEAT_COLORS.map(cl=>{
          const mine = st.players[i].color===cl.hex;
          const taken = st.players.slice(0,st.count).some((p,j)=>j!==i && p.color===cl.hex);
          return `<button class="hscol" data-i="${i}" data-hex="${cl.hex}" title="${cl.name}" ${taken?"disabled":""}
            style="min-width:40px;background:${cl.hex};border:3px solid ${mine?"#fff":"rgba(0,0,0,.55)"};height:34px;border-radius:8px;${taken?"opacity:.22":""}${mine?";box-shadow:0 0 10px "+cl.hex:""}"></button>`;
        }).join("")}</div>
        <div class="seg" style="margin-top:7px;gap:5px;flex-wrap:wrap">${VEHICLE_CLASSES.map(cl=>`
          <button class="hscls" data-i="${i}" data-k="${cl.key}"${st.players[i].cls===cl.key?' style="border-color:#fff"':''}>${cl.icon} ${cl.name}</button>`).join("")}</div>
        <div class="phase-hint hsclsblurb" data-i="${i}" style="margin:6px 0 0;min-height:14px">${classByKey(st.players[i].cls).blurb}</div>`;
      wrap.appendChild(row);
    }
    // wire it
    el.querySelector("#hscount").addEventListener("click",e=>{
      if(e.target.tagName!=="BUTTON") return;
      st.count=+e.target.dataset.v; saveNames(); draw();
    });
    el.querySelectorAll(".hscol").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
      saveNames(); st.players[+b.dataset.i].color=b.dataset.hex; draw();
    });
    el.querySelectorAll(".hscls").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
      saveNames(); st.players[+b.dataset.i].cls=b.dataset.k; draw();
    });
    for(const [seg,key] of [["hslaps","laps"],["hsbots","bots"],["hsdiff","diff"]]){
      const s=el.querySelector("#"+seg); if(!s) continue;
      s.addEventListener("click",e=>{
        if(e.target.tagName!=="BUTTON") return;
        [...s.children].forEach(x=>x.classList.remove("sel")); e.target.classList.add("sel");
        st[key]=+e.target.dataset.v;
        if(seg==="hsbots") el.querySelector("#hsdiffgroup").style.display = st.bots ? "block":"none";
      });
    }
    el.querySelector("#hsgarage").addEventListener("click",e=>{
      if(e.target.tagName!=="BUTTON") return;
      [...el.querySelector("#hsgarage").children].forEach(x=>x.classList.remove("sel"));
      e.target.classList.add("sel"); st.garage=e.target.dataset.v;
    });
    el.querySelector("#hsweather").addEventListener("change",e=>{ st.weather=e.target.value; });
    el.querySelector("#hsback").onclick=()=>{ saveNames(); el.style.display="none"; showTrackSelect(); };
    el.querySelector("#hsstart").onclick=()=>{
      saveNames();
      // unique colours are enforced by the disabled swatches, but double-check
      const cols=st.players.slice(0,st.count).map(p=>p.color);
      if(new Set(cols).size!==cols.length){ toast("Each driver needs their own colour","hot"); return; }
      G.hotseat=true; G.mode="single"; G.champ=null;
      G.laps=st.laps; G.aiMode="sim"; G.difficulty=st.diff;
      G.garage=st.garage; G.upgLevel="reg";
      let wk=st.weather;
      if(wk==="random"){ const keys=Object.keys(WEATHER_TYPES).filter(k=>k!=="none"); wk=keys[Math.floor(Math.random()*keys.length)]; }
      G.weatherKey=wk;
      G.hotseatCfg={
        players: st.players.slice(0,st.count).map((p,i)=>({
          name: (p.name.replace(/[<>&"']/g,"").trim()||`Player ${i+1}`).slice(0,14),
          color: p.color, carNum: HOTSEAT_NUMS[i], cls: p.cls||"ghost"
        })),
        botCount: st.bots
      };
      el.style.display="none";
      showRaceLoading(TRACK.name,
        `${st.count} local drivers · ${st.laps} lap${st.laps>1?"s":""}`,
        ()=>startRace(st.bots));
    };
    function saveNames(){ el.querySelectorAll(".hsname").forEach(inp=>{ st.players[+inp.dataset.i].name=inp.value; }); }
  };
  draw();
}

/* Tiny inline SVG silhouette for each track card (traced from its spacePts). */
function trackThumb(t){
  const P=t.spacePts, w=t.imgW, h=t.imgH, n=P.length;
  // the silhouette closes the way the layout does: back to the start on a loop,
  // back to the lap's first Space on a lead-in, not at all point-to-point
  const tl=layoutOf(t), tls=lapStartOf(t);
  const d="M "+P.map(p=>p[0]+" "+p[1]).join(" L ")
        + (tl==="loop" ? " Z" : tl==="leadin" ? ` L ${P[tls][0]} ${P[tls][1]}` : "");
  // tunnels darken their stretch of the ribbon; gravel spots show as sand dots
  let feat="";
  for(const r of t.tunnels||[]){
    if(r===null || r===undefined) continue;
    const idx=runSpaces(r,t);
    if(!idx.length) continue;
    feat+=`<path d="M ${idx.map(j=>P[j][0]+" "+P[j][1]).join(" L ")}" fill="none"
      stroke="#0a1420" stroke-width="46" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity=".85"/>
      <path d="M ${idx.map(j=>P[j][0]+" "+P[j][1]).join(" L ")}" fill="none"
      stroke="#9ad0f0" stroke-width="7" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity=".8"/>`;
  }
  {
    const g=gravelLists(t), dots=new Set([...(g.inner||[]),...(g.outer||[])]);
    for(const i of dots){ if(i>=0&&i<n) feat+=`<circle cx="${P[i][0]}" cy="${P[i][1]}" r="12" fill="#c8a86b" stroke="#5c4a26" stroke-width="3"/>`; }
  }
  // tint the little map by terrain so each theme reads at a glance
  const TINTS={
    oval:     {bg:"#2a3a22", glow:"#8a5cf6"},
    mountain: {bg:"#26301e", glow:"#7cae5a"},
    parkland: {bg:"#333c22", glow:"#a5b45e"},
    farmland: {bg:"#2b3a20", glow:"#8fbf62"},
    desert:   {bg:"#5c4623", glow:"#e8b45a"},
    savannah: {bg:"#55431c", glow:"#dfba54"},
    urban:    {bg:"#33353c", glow:"#9fb6d0"},
    arctic:   {bg:"#2c3d4c", glow:"#8fd8ef"},
    ...( (typeof window!=="undefined" && window.BOARD_TINTS) || {} )
  };
  const tint=TINTS[t.terrain]||TINTS.oval;
  return `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet">
      <rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="30" fill="${tint.bg}"/>
      <path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="#0d0b12" stroke-width="46" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
      <path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="${tint.glow}" stroke-width="30" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity=".55"/>
      <path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="#f0e7d3" stroke-width="7" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
      ${feat}
    </svg>`;
}

/* ---------------- rules screen (single race)
   Step 2 of the single-race flow: circuit → RULES → car → summary → lights out.
   Nothing starts here any more; the picks are parked on G (which also means the
   sheet re-opens with your last choices if you come back from the car screen). */
function showSetup(){
  const el = $("#setup");
  el.onclick=null;                 // drop any lingering menu backdrop handler
  el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  // the grid and the AI model are fixed now: 9 rivals, full simulated drivers
  G.setupBots = RIVAL_CARS;
  G.aiMode    = RIVAL_AI;
  const GP = gpLaps();             // Grand Prix distance = the circuit's own lap count
  // remembered picks (or first-run defaults) so Back never wipes the sheet
  const D = {
    laps:     (G.laps === 1) ? 1 : GP,
    diff:     G.difficulty != null ? G.difficulty : 0,
    garage:   G.garage     || "none",
    upglvl:   G.upgLevel   || "reg",
    weather:  G.weatherPick|| "random"
  };
  const sel = (a,b) => a===b ? ' class="sel"' : '';
  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="sheet rules">
      <h1>HEAT <span>· Race Rules</span></h1>
      <div class="tag">${TRACK.name} — ${TRACK.tagline}</div>
    <div class="racerules">
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">The pack</div>
        <div class="standrow"><div>Grid</div><div style="margin-left:auto;font-weight:800">${RIVAL_CARS+1} cars — you + ${RIVAL_CARS} rivals</div></div>
        <div class="standrow"><div>Rival AI</div><div style="margin-left:auto;font-weight:800">Full drivers</div></div>
        <div class="phase-hint" style="margin:7px 0 0">Every race is a full ${RIVAL_CARS+1}-car pack. Rivals are simulated drivers — each one runs its own deck, gears and Heat and drives a whole round. Pace/skill below sets how hard they push and how tightly they hoard Heat.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Race distance</div>
        <div class="seg" id="seglaps">
          <button data-v="1"${sel(1,D.laps)}>1 · Sprint</button><button data-v="${GP}"${sel(GP,D.laps)}>${GP} · Grand Prix</button>
        </div>
        <div class="phase-hint" style="margin:7px 0 0">Grand Prix runs ${TRACK.name}'s full distance — ${GP} lap${GP>1?"s":""}.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Rival pace / skill</div>
        <div class="seg" id="segdiff">
          <button data-v="0"${sel(0,D.diff)}>Davidson</button><button data-v="1"${sel(1,D.diff)}>Rookie</button><button data-v="2"${sel(2,D.diff)}>Pro</button><button data-v="3"${sel(3,D.diff)}>Legend</button>
        </div></div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Garage — Upgrade your deck</div>
        <div class="seg" id="seggarage">
          <button data-v="none"${sel("none",D.garage)}>Closed · stock car</button><button data-v="draft"${sel("draft",D.garage)}>Draft (3 rounds)</button><button data-v="random"${sel("random",D.garage)}>Random (3)</button>
        </div>
        <div id="garageblurb" class="phase-hint" style="margin:7px 0 0;min-height:14px">Closed: you race with your 3 Starting Upgrade cards. Draft: once the grid is set, every driver picks 3 Upgrade cards from a shared market. Random: 3 random Upgrade cards dealt to each car — the quick-start.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="optgroup" id="upglvlgroup" style="display:${D.garage==="none"?"none":"block"}"><div class="olbl">Upgrade cards</div>
        <div class="seg" id="segupglvl">
          <button data-v="reg"${sel("reg",D.upglvl)}>Regular only</button><button data-v="all"${sel("all",D.upglvl)}>Regular + Advanced</button>
        </div></div>
      <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Weather &amp; road conditions</div>
        <select id="selweather" style="width:100%;padding:11px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:2px solid #4a3f63;background:#171221;color:var(--cream);font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:15px;letter-spacing:.5px">
          <option value="none"${D.weather==="none"?" selected":""}>Dry — no weather</option>
          <option value="random"${D.weather==="random"?" selected":""}>🎲 Random weather</option>
          ${Object.keys(WEATHER_TYPES).filter(k=>k!=="random").map(k=>`<option value="${k}"${D.weather===k?" selected":""}>${WEATHER_TYPES[k].icon} ${WEATHER_TYPES[k].name}</option>`).join("")}
        </select>
        <div id="weatherblurb" class="phase-hint" style="margin:8px 0 0;min-height:16px"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="startbtn">Pick your car ▸</button></div>
      <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="act secondary" id="changetrack">◂ Change circuit</button></div>
      <div class="phase-hint" style="margin-top:14px">Set the race up here, then choose the car you'll drive — you'll get a full summary before the lights go out.</div>
    </div>`;
  $("#changetrack").onclick = ()=>{ el.style.display="none"; showTrackSelect(); };
  for(const seg of ["seglaps","segdiff","seggarage","segupglvl"]){
    $("#"+seg).addEventListener("click", e=>{
      if(e.target.tagName!=="BUTTON") return;
      [...$("#"+seg).children].forEach(b=>b.classList.remove("sel"));
      e.target.classList.add("sel");
      if(seg==="seggarage"){
        const g=e.target.dataset.v;
        $("#upglvlgroup").style.display = g==="none" ? "none" : "block";
        $("#garageblurb").textContent = g==="none"
          ? "Closed: you race with the 3 Starting Upgrade cards your car came with."
          : g==="draft"
          ? "Garage Draft: once the grid is set, every driver picks 3 Upgrade cards from a shared market (snake order by grid)."
          : "Garage Random: 3 random Upgrade cards dealt to each car — the quick-start shortcut.";
      }
    });
  }
  const selW=$("#selweather");
  const showBlurb=()=>{ const k=selW.value;
    $("#weatherblurb").textContent = k==="random" ? "A random weather will be drawn when the lights go out."
      : (WEATHER_TYPES[k] ? WEATHER_TYPES[k].blurb : ""); };
  selW.addEventListener("change", showBlurb); showBlurb();
  $("#startbtn").onclick = ()=>{
    G.hotseat=false;
    G.setupBots  = RIVAL_CARS;                               // always a full pack
    G.aiMode     = RIVAL_AI;                                 // always full simulated drivers
    G.laps       = +$("#seglaps .sel").dataset.v;
    G.difficulty = +$("#segdiff .sel").dataset.v;
    G.garage     = $("#seggarage .sel").dataset.v;           // none | draft | random
    G.upgLevel   = $("#segupglvl .sel").dataset.v;           // reg | all
    G.weatherPick= selW.value;                              // none | random | a key
    let wk = G.weatherPick;
    if(wk==="random"){ const keys=Object.keys(WEATHER_TYPES).filter(k=>k!=="none"); wk=keys[Math.floor(Math.random()*keys.length)]; }
    G.weatherKey = wk;                                      // rolled now, revealed at lights-out
    showDeckSelect(showRaceSummary, showSetup);             // car select → summary

  };
}

/* ---------------- race setup summary (single race)
   Last stop before the loading screen: circuit, car and every rule on one card,
   so nothing is a surprise once the lights go out. */
function summaryRow(k, v){
  return `<div class="standrow"><div style="color:var(--cream-dim);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1.6px;text-transform:uppercase;min-width:112px">${k}</div>
    <div style="margin-left:auto;font-weight:800;text-align:right">${v}</div></div>`;
}
const DIFF_NAMES = ["Davidson","Rookie","Pro","Legend"];
function weatherLabel(){
  if(G.weatherPick==="random") return "🎲 Random draw";
  const w = WEATHER_TYPES[G.weatherKey];
  return w && G.weatherKey!=="random" ? `${w.icon} ${w.name}` : "Dry — no weather";
}
function garageLabel(){
  const tier = G.upgLevel==="all" ? "Regular + Advanced" : "Regular";
  return G.garage==="draft"  ? `Draft · 3 rounds (${tier})`
       : G.garage==="random" ? `Random · 3 Upgrades (${tier})`
       : "Closed · stock car";
}
function showRaceSummary(){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  const c = classByKey(G.playerCls||"ghost");
  const bots = RIVAL_CARS;
  const carArt = DECK_ART[c.key]==="ok"
    ? `<img src="${c.art}" alt="${esc(c.name)}">`
    : classCarSVG(c.key);
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
    <h1>HEAT <span>· on the grid</span></h1>
    <div class="tag">Check it over — then send it.</div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Circuit</div>
      <div class="trackcard" style="cursor:default">
        <div class="tc-art">${trackThumb(TRACK)}</div>
        <div class="tc-body">
          <div class="tc-name">${TRACK.name}</div>
          <div class="tc-meta">${TRACK.spaces} spaces · ${TRACK.corners.length} corners · ${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Your car</div>
      <div class="deckcard" style="padding:12px">
        <div class="dk-art" style="height:150px">${carArt}</div>
        <div class="dk-name">${esc(c.name)}</div>
        <div class="dk-tag">${esc(c.tag)}</div>
        <div class="dk-stats">${deckStatPills(c).map(p=>`<span class="${p.k||""}">${p.t}</span>`).join("")}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="optgroup"><div class="olbl">Rules</div>
      ${summaryRow("Grid", `${bots+1} cars — you + ${bots} rivals`)}
      ${summaryRow("Distance", `${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""} — ${G.laps===1?"Sprint":"Grand Prix"}`)}
      ${summaryRow("Rival AI", "Full drivers")}
      ${summaryRow("Pace / skill", DIFF_NAMES[G.difficulty]||"Rookie")}
      ${summaryRow("Garage", garageLabel())}
      ${summaryRow("Weather", weatherLabel())}
    </div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:14px"><button class="act" id="sumGo">Lights out ▸</button></div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="act secondary" id="sumCar">◂ Change car</button></div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="act secondary" id="sumRules">◂ Change rules</button></div>
  </div>`;
  $("#sumCar").onclick   = ()=>showDeckSelect(showRaceSummary, showSetup);
  $("#sumRules").onclick = ()=>showSetup();
  $("#sumGo").onclick    = ()=>{
    el.style.display="none";
    showRaceLoading(TRACK.name, `${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""} · ${bots+1}-car grid · ${esc(c.name)}`,
      ()=>startRace(bots));
  };
}

/* ---------------- race start */
function upgradePool(){
  return UPGRADE_CARDS.filter(u => G.upgLevel==="all" ? true : !u.adv).map(u=>u.id);
}
function startRace(botCount){
  G.botCount = botCount;   // remembered so "Restart race" can rebuild the same grid
  if(G.mode!=="champ"){ G.event=null; G.pressCorners=[]; }   // Events & Press are Championship-only
  // Weather is prepared BEFORE the car is built, so its car-setup modifiers
  // (extra/removed Heat & Stress) shape the deck the player draws from.
  setupWeather(G.weatherKey || "none");
  // Draft/random garage cars are built AFTER the grid is placed (draft order
  // depends on grid position), so defer deck construction until then.
  const garage = G.garage && G.garage!=="none";
  G.activeHuman=null;
  if(G.hotseat && G.hotseatCfg){
    // pass & play: one human per local driver, in setup order
    G.players = G.hotseatCfg.players.map((hp,i)=>
      makeHuman(garage ? [] : undefined, {id:"p"+i, name:hp.name, color:hp.color, carNum:hp.carNum, cls:hp.cls}));
  } else {
    G.players = [ garage ? makeHuman([]) : makeHuman() ];  // temp empty upgrades; deck rebuilt post-draft
  }
  // bots: in hotseat, skip pool entries whose colour a local driver already took
  let botIdxs=[...Array(botCount).keys()];
  if(G.hotseat){
    const used=new Set(G.players.map(p=>p.color.toLowerCase()));
    botIdxs=[];
    for(let i=0;i<BOT_POOL.length && botIdxs.length<botCount;i++)
      if(!used.has(BOT_POOL[i].color.toLowerCase())) botIdxs.push(i);
  }
  for(const i of botIdxs){
    const b = makeBot(i, garage ? [] : undefined); G.players.push(b);
  }
  // starting grid: staggered spots just past the finish line
  const grid = shuffle([...G.players]);
  // as many rows as the field needs (2 cars per row, pole first) — a 10-car
  // pack simply stacks five rows back behind the flag
  const slots = [];
  for(let i=0;i<grid.length;i++) slots.push({t:-(1+Math.floor(i/2)), s:i%2});
  grid.forEach((p,i)=>{ p.total=slots[i].t; p.spot=slots[i].s; p.gridPos=i; });
  if(LAYOUT==="open") G.laps = 1;      // point-to-point: one run, whatever the setup screen said
  G.legendDeck = shuffle([...LEGEND_CARDS]);
  G.round=0; G.finishOrder=[]; G.replay=[];
  rollRoadConditions();           // draw one token per corner (needs the legend deck for wind direction)
  renderCars({snap:true});
  if(garage){
    // hand each drafting driver its final deck after picks are locked in
    runDraft(()=>finishRaceStart());
  } else {
    finishRaceStart();
  }
}
/* rebuild a driver's deck/engine from a finalized upgrade list (post-draft) */
function equipDriver(p, ids){
  p.upgrades = ids.slice();
  p.xheat = ids.filter(id=>upgradeById(id).xheat).length;
  p.deck = buildDeck(ids, p.cls); p.discard=[]; p.hand=[]; p.play=[];
  p.engine = heatCapFor(p);                   // full Engine; no starter Extra Heat card rides the deck
  resetRoundScratch(p);
}
function finishRaceStart(){
  for(const h of humans()){ applyWeatherCarSetup(h); drawToHand(h,handSize()); }
  for(const p of G.players) if(p.sim) applyWeatherCarSetup(p);
  if(G.aiMode==="sim") for(const p of G.players) if(p.sim) drawToHand(p,handSize());   // rivals hold a hand too
  const gtxt = (G.garage&&G.garage!=="none") ? ` · Garage ${G.garage==="draft"?"draft":"random"}` : "";
  const field = G.hotseat
    ? `${humans().length} local drivers${G.botCount?` + ${G.botCount} AI rival${G.botCount>1?"s":""}`:""}`
    : `${G.botCount} ${G.aiMode==="sim"?simProfile().name+"-level driver":"rival"}${G.botCount>1?"s":""}`;
  log(`Lights out at ${TRACK.name}! ${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""}, ${field}${gtxt}.`,"me");
  for(const h of humans()) log(`${h.name} rolls out in a ${classChip(h)}.`,"me");
  if(G.aiMode==="sim") for(const p of G.players) if(p.isBot) log(`${p.name} — ${classChip(p)}.`);
  logWeatherSetup();
  renderCars(); renderGauges(); updateWeatherNote();
  showRaceBriefing(()=>startRound());   // no Event/weather in play → starts immediately
}

/* ---------------- Garage draft
   Single race: 3 rounds, market = drivers+3, snake order by grid
     (back→front, front→back, back→front). Leftovers discarded each round.
   Random:      each driver simply gets 3 random upgrades, no UI.
   Championship: one round, 1 card each, market = drivers+3, back→front,
     then last-on-grid may swap; leftovers reshuffled. (handled via champDraft flag) */
function draftOrderByGrid(reverse){
  // only human + sim drivers draft; Legend bots are ignored (rules)
  const drafters = G.players.filter(p=>!p.isBot || p.sim);
  drafters.sort((a,b)=> reverse ? b.gridPos-a.gridPos : a.gridPos-b.gridPos);
  return drafters;
}
function runDraft(done, opts={}){
  const champ = !!opts.champ;                 // championship: 1 card each, single round
  const pool = shuffle(upgradePool().map(id=>id));   // ids
  const drafters = G.players.filter(p=>!p.isBot || p.sim);
  drafters.forEach(p=> p._draft = (champ && p.upgrades) ? p.upgrades.slice() : []);
  /* The pool must never run dry mid-draft, or a human whose turn lands on an
     empty market has no card to click and the draft freezes. A solo Grand Prix
     fields a 10-car pack, so 10 drivers drafting 3 rounds need far more than the
     17 Regular-tier ids can supply. takeCard() refills from cards nobody has
     drafted yet, and only if even those are exhausted does it recycle the whole
     tier (allowing a repeat) — so it always returns a card and every market fills. */
  function refillPool(){
    const have = new Set(drafters.flatMap(p=>p._draft));
    let refill = shuffle(upgradePool().filter(id=>!have.has(id)));
    if(!refill.length) refill = shuffle(upgradePool());   // everything drafted → allow repeats
    pool.push(...refill);
  }
  function takeCard(){
    if(!pool.length) refillPool();
    return pool.length ? pool.pop() : null;
  }
  if(G.garage==="random" && !champ){
    // deal 3 random upgrades to each, no UI
    drafters.forEach(p=>{ for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ const id=takeCard(); if(id!=null) p._draft.push(id); } });
    finalizeDraft(drafters, champ, done);
    return;
  }
  const rounds = champ ? 1 : 3;
  let round = 0;
  function playRound(){
    const reverse = champ ? true : (round%2===0);  // r1 & r3 back→front, r2 front→back
    const order = draftOrderByGrid(reverse);
    const marketSize = drafters.length + 3;
    // build a full market — takeCard() keeps the pool topped up, so it can never
    // come up short and strand a drafter on an empty market
    const market = [];
    for(let i=0;i<marketSize;i++){ const id=takeCard(); if(id==null) break; market.push(id); }
    let idx = 0;
    function nextPick(){
      if(idx>=order.length){
        // leftovers: single race discards them; champ reshuffles into the pool
        if(champ) pool.push(...market.filter(m=>m!==null));
        round++;
        if(round<rounds) playRound();
        else finishDraftRounds();
        return;
      }
      const p = order[idx];
      if(p.isBot){
        // bot auto-picks best-scoring available card
        const prof = profileForBot(p);
        let best=-1, bi=-1;
        market.forEach((id,i)=>{ if(id===null) return; const sc=upgScore(upgradeById(id),prof); if(sc>best){best=sc;bi=i;} });
        if(bi>=0){ p._draft.push(market[bi]); market[bi]=null; }
        idx++; setTimeout(nextPick, 260);
        renderDraft(order, idx, market, round, rounds, champ);
      } else {
        // safety net: if there's somehow nothing left to pick, don't strand the
        // player on a dead screen — advance the draft instead of freezing
        if(!market.some(id=>id!==null)){ idx++; nextPick(); return; }
        renderDraft(order, idx, market, round, rounds, champ, (i)=>{
          p._draft.push(market[i]); market[i]=null; idx++; nextPick();
        });
      }
    }
    nextPick();
  }
  function finishDraftRounds(){
    // championship: last-on-grid driver may swap one held card for a leftover
    if(champ){
      const last = draftOrderByGrid(false).slice(-1)[0]; // highest gridPos among drafters
      if(last && !last.isBot && pool.length){
        offerChampSwap(last, pool, ()=> finalizeDraft(drafters, champ, done));
        return;
      }
    }
    finalizeDraft(drafters, champ, done);
  }
  playRound();
}
function finalizeDraft(drafters, champ, done){
  drafters.forEach(p=>{
    if(champ){ p.upgrades = p._draft.slice(); }  // champ deck rebuilt in champStartRace
    else equipDriver(p, p._draft);
    delete p._draft;
  });
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="none"; el.innerHTML="";
  done();
}

/* draft market UI */
function renderDraft(order, idx, market, round, rounds, champ, onPick){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null; el.style.display="flex";
  const p = order[idx];
  const you = curHuman();
  const title = champ ? "Build your car — 1 Upgrade" : `Garage draft · round ${round+1} of ${rounds}`;
  const whose = p ? (p.isBot ? `${p.name} is picking…` : "Your pick") : "";
  const yourCards = (you._draft||[]).map(id=>upgradeById(id));
  el.innerHTML = `<div class="sheet">
    <h1>HEAT <span>· Garage Module</span></h1>
    <div class="tag">${title} · market of ${market.length} — pick order follows the grid</div>
    <div class="olbl" style="margin:6px 0 2px">${whose}</div>
    <div class="draftmkt" id="mkt"></div>
    <div class="olbl" style="margin:10px 0 2px">Your Upgrades (${yourCards.length})</div>
    <div class="draftmkt" id="mine" style="max-height:22vh"></div>
  </div>`;
  const mkt=el.querySelector("#mkt");
  market.forEach((id,i)=>{
    if(id===null){ return; }
    const cd=renderCard(makeUpgCard(upgradeById(id)));
    if(p && !p.isBot && onPick){ cd.onclick=()=>onPick(i); }
    else cd.classList.add("taken");
    mkt.appendChild(cd);
  });
  const mine=el.querySelector("#mine");
  yourCards.forEach(def=>{ const cd=renderCard(makeUpgCard(def)); cd.classList.add("taken"); mine.appendChild(cd); });
}

/* championship: last-place driver's optional swap of a held card for a leftover */
function offerChampSwap(p, pool, done){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex"; el.onclick=null;
  const held = p._draft.map(id=>upgradeById(id));
  const leftovers = pool.map(id=>upgradeById(id));
  let pickHeld=null, pickPool=null;
  const draw=()=>{
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
      <h1>HEAT <span>· last-place swap</span></h1>
      <div class="tag">You're last on the grid — you may trade one of your Upgrade cards for a leftover from the market, or skip.</div>
      <div class="olbl" style="margin:6px 0 2px">Your Upgrades — tap one to give up</div>
      <div class="draftmkt" id="held"></div>
      <div class="olbl" style="margin:10px 0 2px">Leftovers — tap one to take</div>
      <div class="draftmkt" id="pool" style="max-height:24vh"></div>
      <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:10px">
        <button class="act" id="doswap" ${pickHeld==null||pickPool==null?"disabled":""}>Swap</button>
        <button class="act secondary" id="skipswap">Keep my cards</button>
      </div></div>`;
    const h=el.querySelector("#held");
    held.forEach((def,i)=>{ const cd=renderCard(makeUpgCard(def)); if(i===pickHeld)cd.classList.add("sel");
      cd.onclick=()=>{ pickHeld=i; draw(); }; h.appendChild(cd); });
    const pl=el.querySelector("#pool");
    leftovers.forEach((def,i)=>{ const cd=renderCard(makeUpgCard(def)); if(i===pickPool)cd.classList.add("sel");
      cd.onclick=()=>{ pickPool=i; draw(); }; pl.appendChild(cd); });
    el.querySelector("#skipswap").onclick=done;
    el.querySelector("#doswap").onclick=()=>{
      if(pickHeld==null||pickPool==null) return;
      const gave = p._draft[pickHeld]; p._draft[pickHeld]=pool[pickPool];
      pool[pickPool]=gave;  // (returns to pool, reshuffled next race)
      done();
    };
  };
  draw();
}

/* ---------------- restart / new game
   Restart = same setup (rivals, laps, difficulty, weather), but the whole race
   is rebuilt from the grid: fresh decks, fresh 7-card hands, Heat reset to full.
   startRace() already rebuilds every player from scratch, so replaying it with
   the stored config is a clean restart. New game returns to the setup sheet. */
function restartRace(){
  closeMenu();
  if(window.HeatAudio && window.HeatAudio.race) window.HeatAudio.race();
  if(G.mode==="champ" && G.champ){
    // restart the current championship race, keeping standings/points intact.
    // Roll back this race's drafted upgrade so the draft isn't stacked twice.
    const ch=G.champ;
    ch.roster.forEach(r=>{ if(r._draftedThisRace){ r.upgrades = r.upgradesBeforeRace.slice(); } });
    showRaceLoading(TRACK.name, `${ch.cupName||"Championship"} · Race ${ch.raceIdx+1} — restart`,
      ()=>startChampRace());
    return;
  }
  const bots = G.botCount != null ? G.botCount : RIVAL_CARS;
  showRaceLoading(TRACK.name, `${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""} · restart`, ()=>startRace(bots));
}
function newGame(){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.onclick=null;         // drop the menu's backdrop handler
  if(window.HeatAudio && window.HeatAudio.toOpening) window.HeatAudio.toOpening();
  showModeSelect();
}

/* ---------------- audio settings (music + SFX volume / mute)
   Playback itself lives in the host page's window.HeatAudio object — this file
   only ever *calls* it (.race()/.win()/.toOpening()). What we own here is the
   player's preference: persist it and push it into HeatAudio through a small,
   defensive contract so it takes effect whether the host exposes an apply hook,
   individual setters, or just reads plain properties at play time. */
const AUDIO_LS = "heat.audio.v1";
function defAudioPrefs(){ return {musicVol:70, sfxVol:80, musicMuted:false, sfxMuted:false}; }
function loadAudioPrefs(){
  try{ const j=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(AUDIO_LS));
       if(j && typeof j==="object") return Object.assign(defAudioPrefs(), j); }catch(e){}
  return defAudioPrefs();
}
const audioPrefs = loadAudioPrefs();
function saveAudioPrefs(){ try{ localStorage.setItem(AUDIO_LS, JSON.stringify(audioPrefs)); }catch(e){} }
/* effective 0..1 gains — an explicit mute always wins over the slider value */
function musicGain(){ return audioPrefs.musicMuted ? 0 : audioPrefs.musicVol/100; }
function sfxGain(){   return audioPrefs.sfxMuted   ? 0 : audioPrefs.sfxVol/100; }
/* push the current prefs into the host audio engine, however it happens to be
   shaped. Safe to call any time (e.g. after HeatAudio loads late). */
function applyAudioPrefs(){
  saveAudioPrefs();
  const A = window.HeatAudio; if(!A) return;
  const mg = musicGain(), sg = sfxGain();
  // 1) preferred single hook
  if(typeof A.applySettings==="function"){
    try{ A.applySettings({musicVol:mg, sfxVol:sg,
                          musicMuted:audioPrefs.musicMuted, sfxMuted:audioPrefs.sfxMuted}); }catch(e){}
  }
  // 2) common individual setters, called only if the host provides them
  const call=(fn,v)=>{ if(typeof A[fn]==="function"){ try{ A[fn](v); }catch(e){} } };
  call("setMusicVolume", mg);  call("setSfxVolume", sg);
  call("setMusicMuted", audioPrefs.musicMuted);  call("setSfxMuted", audioPrefs.sfxMuted);
  call("setMuted", audioPrefs.musicMuted && audioPrefs.sfxMuted);
  // 3) plain-property fallback the host can read whenever it starts a sound
  A.prefs = {musicVol:mg, sfxVol:sg,
             musicMuted:audioPrefs.musicMuted, sfxMuted:audioPrefs.sfxMuted};
}

/* Settings sheet. `back` is the screen to return to (in-race menu or title). */
function openSettings(back){
  back = back || showModeSelect;
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex"; el.scrollTop=0;
  const row=(id,label,vol,muted)=>`
    <div class="setrow">
      <div class="setrow-top">
        <span class="setlbl">${label}</span>
        <label class="mutebox"><input type="checkbox" id="${id}Mute" ${muted?"checked":""}> Mute</label>
      </div>
      <div class="setrow-bar">
        <input type="range" class="volbar" min="0" max="100" step="1" value="${vol}" id="${id}Vol" ${muted?"disabled":""}>
        <span class="setval" id="${id}Val">${muted?"Muted":vol+"%"}</span>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
    <h2>⚙ Settings</h2>
    <div class="tag">Audio</div>
    ${row("music","🎵 Music", audioPrefs.musicVol, audioPrefs.musicMuted)}
    ${row("sfx","🔊 Sound effects", audioPrefs.sfxVol, audioPrefs.sfxMuted)}
    <div class="phase-hint" style="margin-top:12px">Changes save on this device and apply straight away.</div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:12px">
      <button class="act" id="setBack">Done</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  const wire=(id)=>{
    const vol=$("#"+id+"Vol"), mute=$("#"+id+"Mute"), val=$("#"+id+"Val");
    const paint=()=>{ val.textContent = mute.checked ? "Muted" : vol.value+"%"; vol.disabled=mute.checked; };
    vol.oninput  =()=>{ audioPrefs[id+"Vol"]  = +vol.value;   paint(); applyAudioPrefs(); };
    mute.onchange=()=>{ audioPrefs[id+"Muted"] = mute.checked; paint(); applyAudioPrefs(); };
  };
  wire("music"); wire("sfx");
  const done=()=>back();
  $("#setBack").onclick=done;
  el.onclick=e=>{ if(e.target===el) done(); };
}

/* ---------------- in-race menu (accessible any time from the ☰ button) */
function openMenu(){
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex";
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
    <h2>Menu</h2>
    <div class="tag">${TRACK.name} · Round ${G.round}</div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="flex-direction:column;align-items:stretch">
      <button class="act" id="mResume">Resume race</button>
      <button class="act secondary" id="mSettings">⚙ Settings</button>
      <button class="act secondary" id="mRestart">↻ Restart race — same setup</button>
      <button class="act secondary" id="mNew">New game — change setup</button>
    </div>
    <div class="phase-hint" style="margin-top:12px">Restart keeps the same rivals, laps &amp; weather but deals fresh hands and resets Heat. New game lets you change the settings.</div>
  </div>`;
  $("#mResume").onclick=closeMenu;
  $("#mSettings").onclick=()=>openSettings(openMenu);
  $("#mRestart").onclick=()=>menuConfirm("Restart this race?",
    "Same rivals, laps and weather — but fresh hands, Heat reset to full, and everyone back on the starting grid.","↻ Restart",restartRace);
  $("#mNew").onclick=()=>menuConfirm("Start a new game?",
    "Return to the setup screen to change rivals, laps, difficulty or weather.","New game",newGame);
  el.onclick=e=>{ if(e.target===el) closeMenu(); };   // tap backdrop = resume
}
function menuConfirm(title, body, yes, onYes){
  const el=$("#setup");
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
    <h2>${title}</h2>
    <div class="tag">Confirm</div>
    <div class="phase-hint" style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:13px;min-height:0">${body}</div>
    <div class="btnrow">
      <button class="act hot" id="mYes">${yes}</button>
      <button class="act secondary" id="mBack">Cancel</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  $("#mYes").onclick=onYes;
  $("#mBack").onclick=openMenu;
  el.onclick=e=>{ if(e.target===el) openMenu(); };
}
function closeMenu(){ const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="none"; el.innerHTML=""; el.onclick=null; }

/* ---------------- round flow */
function startRound(){
  pushReplaySnap();                 // record where every car starts this round
  G.round++;
  G.legendCard=null; G.legendRevealed=false;
  updateRoundLabel();
  const alive = humans().filter(h=>!h.finished);
  alive.forEach(h=>{ resetRoundScratch(h); h.play=[]; });
  renderGauges();
  if(alive.length===0){ // every local driver home: sim remaining bots quickly
    runBotsOnly();
    return;
  }
  // steps 1 & 2 go round the table, one local driver at a time
  G._spQueue = alive.slice();
  nextShiftPlay();
}
function nextShiftPlay(){
  const h = G._spQueue && G._spQueue.shift();
  if(!h){ G.activeHuman=null; beginMovementPhase(); return; }
  G.activeHuman = h;
  renderGauges();
  hotseatHandoff(h, "shift gears &amp; play your cards face-down", stageShift);
}
/* privacy screen between local drivers — nothing of the outgoing hand stays visible */
function hotseatHandoff(p, what, go){
  if(!G.hotseat || humans().length<2){ go(); return; }
  clearHighlights(); hideCardTip();
  const panel=$("#panel");
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">Pass the device</div>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin:14px 0 4px">
      <div style="width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:50%;flex:none;background:${p.color};border:3px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 0 14px ${p.color}"></div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:1px">${p.name}</div>
        <div class="phase-hint" style="margin:2px 0 0">No. ${p.carNum||17} · ${what}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="phase-hint" style="margin-top:6px">Other drivers, eyes on the track — the next screen shows ${p.name}'s hand.</div>
    <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="hogo">I'm ${p.name} — show my cards</button></div>`;
  const tk=arm();
  $("#hogo").onclick=()=>{ if(live(tk)) go(); };
}

/* ---- 1. Shift Gears */
function stageShift(){
  const tk=arm();
  const you=curHuman();
  let pick=you.gear;
  const opts=[];
  for(let g=Math.max(1,you.gear-2); g<=Math.min(4,you.gear+2); g++) opts.push(g);
  const playableN = you.hand.filter(c=>!isHeat(c)).length;
  const heatN = you.hand.length - playableN;
  const panel=$("#panel");
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">1 · Shift gears${phaseWho(you)}</div>
    <div class="phase-hint">One step is free. Jumping two gears costs <b style="color:var(--heat)">1 Heat</b>, paid now. Your gear sets how many cards you must play.</div>
    ${cornerDistanceHTML(you)}
    ${weatherHereHTML(you)}
    <div id="geargate"></div>
    ${engineBarHTML(you)}
    <div class="phase-hint" style="margin:4px 0 0">Your hand — <b>${playableN}</b> playable${heatN?`, <b style="color:var(--heat)">${heatN}🔥</b> stuck (Heat can't be played)`:""} <span style="color:var(--cream-dim)">· hold a card for help</span>:</div>
    ${drawPileSlotHTML()}
    <div id="hand"></div>
    <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="confirmgear">Engage gear <span id="gpick">${pick}</span></button></div>`;
  mountDrawPile(you);
  /* read-only hand preview so gear choice is informed, like holding your cards at the table */
  const handEl=$("#hand");
  you.hand.forEach(c=>{
    const el=renderCard(c);
    if(isHeat(c)) el.classList.add("dim");
    handEl.appendChild(el);
  });
  const wetSec = weatherShiftPenaltyHere(you);   // Wet Asphalt weather sector: shifting DOWN costs +1 Heat per gear
  if(wetSec) panel.querySelector(".phase-hint").innerHTML +=
    ` <b style="color:var(--cool)">Wet asphalt:</b> shifting down costs <b style="color:var(--heat)">1 Heat per gear</b> here.`;
  const gate=$("#geargate");
  for(const g of opts){
    const two = Math.abs(g-you.gear)===2;
    const clut = g > playableN;
    const cost = shiftHeatCost(you.gear, g, wetSec);
    const b=document.createElement("button");
    b.className="gearopt"+(cost>0?" heatcost":"")+(g===pick?" sel":"");
    b.innerHTML = `${g}<small>${clut?'<span style="color:var(--heat)">clutter!</span>':g===you.gear?"stay":g>you.gear?"↑":"↓"}${cost>0?` <span style="color:var(--heat)">${cost}🔥</span>`:""}</small>${cost>0?'<span class="flame">🔥</span>':""}`;
    b.disabled = cost>you.engine;
    b.onclick=()=>{ pick=g; [...gate.children].forEach(x=>x.classList.remove("sel")); b.classList.add("sel"); $("#gpick").textContent=g; };
    gate.appendChild(b);
  }
  $("#confirmgear").onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const cost = shiftHeatCost(you.gear, pick, wetSec);
    if(cost>you.engine){ toast("Not enough Heat to shift","hot"); return; }
    if(cost>0){
      payHeat(you,cost);
