      const dn = pick<you.gear;
      log(`Shift ${you.gear}→${pick} costs ${cost} Heat${wetSec&&dn?" (wet asphalt)":""}.`,"me");
      toast(`Shift · ${cost} Heat`,"hot");
    }
    you.gear=pick; renderGauges();
    stagePlay();
  };
}

/* ---- 2. Play Cards */
function stagePlay(){
  const tk=arm();
  const you=curHuman();
  const playable = you.hand.filter(c=>!isHeat(c));
  const cluttered = playable.length < you.gear;
  // Low Sun weather sector: you must play at least one Stress card if you hold one (blinded — no brake points).
  const mustStress = !cluttered && isWeatherSector(curSector(you)) && !!(G.weather && G.weather.def.sector.lowSun) && you.hand.some(isStress);
  const stressSatisfied = () => !mustStress || you.hand.some(c=>sel.has(c.id) && isStress(c));
  const sel=new Set();
  const panel=$("#panel");
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">2 · Play cards${phaseWho(you)}</div>
    <div class="phase-hint">${cluttered
      ? `<b style="color:var(--heat)">Cluttered hand!</b> Too many Heat cards to play gear ${you.gear}. You'll dump what you can, the car won't move, and you drop to 1st gear.`
      : `Gear ${you.gear}: choose exactly <b>${you.gear}</b> card${you.gear>1?"s":""}. Heat cards can't be played — cool them down instead. Stress ＋ draws a random speed. <span style="color:var(--cream-dim)">Hold (or hover) any card to see what it does.</span>`}</div>
    ${cornerDistanceHTML(you)}
    ${mustStress?`<div class="forecast warn" style="margin-bottom:8px">🌅 <b>Low sun</b> — you're blinded here and must include at least one <b>Stress ＋</b> card this round.</div>`:""}
    ${drawPileSlotHTML()}
    <div id="hand"></div>
    <div class="phase-hint" id="landnote" style="min-height:18px;margin-bottom:6px"></div>
    <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="playbtn" disabled>Reveal &amp; drive</button></div>`;
  mountDrawPile(you);
  const handEl=$("#hand");
  /* preview where the selected cards would land the car (before Boost /
     slipstream / blocking). Stress ＋ shows the possible band instead. */
  function updateLanding(){
    const note=$("#landnote"); if(!note) return;
    const chosen=you.hand.filter(c=>sel.has(c.id));
    let known=0, loX=0, hiX=0, heatDue=0, limAdj=0;
    for(const c of chosen){
      if(c.t==="speed") known+=c.v;
      else if(c.t==="up5") known+=5;
      else if(isUpg(c)){
        const [lo,hi]=upgSpan(c.def);
        if(lo===hi) known+=lo; else { loX+=lo; hiX+=hi; }
        heatDue += c.def.heat||0;
        limAdj  += c.def.limit||0;
      }
    }
    const nS=chosen.filter(isStress).length;
    if(chosen.length===0){ clearHighlights(); note.textContent=""; return; }
    /* projected Check Corner bill if this were the final speed (pre-Boost/slipstream).
       The persistent limitAdj (class cornering + career mods) is baked into the
       driver every round; the chosen Upgrade cards' limit symbols stack on top.
       Both must be in the bill or the Heat forecast won't match the real check. */
    const totalLimAdj = limAdj + (you.limitAdj||0);
    const bill = d => cornerHeatCost(d, crossingsForMove(you.total, d), totalLimAdj);
    const costNote = heatDue ? ` · Upgrade cost <b style="color:var(--heat)">${heatDue}🔥</b> on reveal` : "";
    const limNote  = limAdj ? ` · these Upgrades: corner limits <b>${limAdj>0?"+":""}${limAdj}</b>` : "";
    if(nS===0 && loX===0 && hiX===0){
      if(known===0){ clearHighlights(); note.textContent="These cards give 0 speed — no movement."; return; }
      highlightSpaces([{total:you.total+known, kind:"exact"}]);
      const cost=bill(known);
      const engAfter = Math.max(0, you.engine - heatDue);   // upgrade heat costs are paid before the corner check
      let heatTxt="";
      if(cost>0){
        heatTxt = cost<=engAfter
          ? ` · Corner bill: <b style="color:var(--heat)">≈${cost} Heat</b> (engine ${engAfter})`
          : ` · <b style="color:#ff5a3c">⚠ SPIN OUT</b> — ${cost} Heat owed, engine only has ${engAfter} after costs`;
      } else if(crossingsForMove(you.total,known).length){
        heatTxt = ` · crosses a corner <b style="color:#9fe08a">clean</b>`;
      }
      const wx = cornerWeatherWarnings(crossingsForMove(you.total,known), known);
      const wxTxt = wx.length ? `<div style="margin-top:4px">${wx.join(" · ")}</div>` : "";
      note.innerHTML=`Landing: <b style="color:var(--purple-glow)">+${known}</b> — highlighted on the track (before Boost / slipstream; blocking ignored).${heatTxt}${costNote}${limNote}${wxTxt}`;
    }else{
      const min=known+nS+loX, max=known+nS*4+hiX;
      const items=[]; for(let d=min; d<=max; d++) items.push({total:you.total+d, kind:"range"});
      highlightSpaces(items);
      let cMin=Infinity, cMax=0;
      for(let d=min; d<=max; d++){ const c=bill(d); cMin=Math.min(cMin,c); cMax=Math.max(cMax,c); }
      let heatTxt="";
      const engAfter = Math.max(0, you.engine - heatDue);   // upgrade heat costs are paid before the corner check
      if(cMax>0){
        if(cMin>engAfter) heatTxt = ` · <b style="color:#ff5a3c">⚠ SPIN OUT guaranteed</b> (${cMin}+ Heat owed, engine ${engAfter} after costs)`;
        else if(cMax>engAfter) heatTxt = ` · corner bill ≈${cMin}–${cMax} Heat — <b style="color:#ff5a3c">spin-out risk</b> (engine ${engAfter} after costs)`;
        else if(cMax>0) heatTxt = ` · corner bill <b style="color:var(--heat)">≈${cMin}–${cMax} Heat</b> (engine ${engAfter})`;
      }
      const why=[];
      if(nS) why.push(`${nS} stress ＋`);
      if(loX||hiX) why.push(`Upgrade ＋/choice values`);
      const crossUnion=new Set();
      for(let d=min; d<=max; d++) for(const c of crossingsForMove(you.total,d)) crossUnion.add(c);
      const wx = cornerWeatherWarnings([...crossUnion], max);   // max speed → aqua caution shown if it could bite
      const wxTxt = wx.length ? `<div style="margin-top:4px">${wx.join(" · ")}</div>` : "";
      note.innerHTML=`Landing range: <b style="color:var(--stress)">+${min} to +${max}</b> — ${why.join(" · ")}.${heatTxt}${costNote}${limNote}${wxTxt}`;
    }
  }
  you.hand.forEach(c=>{
    const el=renderCard(c);
    if(isHeat(c)) el.classList.add("dim");
    else el.onclick=()=>{
      if(cluttered) return;
      if(sel.has(c.id)) sel.delete(c.id); else if(sel.size<you.gear) sel.add(c.id);
      el.classList.toggle("sel", sel.has(c.id));
      $("#playbtn").disabled = sel.size!==you.gear || !stressSatisfied();
      updateLanding();
    };
    handEl.appendChild(el);
  });
  updateLanding();
  const btn=$("#playbtn");
  if(cluttered){ btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent="Cluttered — coast this round"; btn.classList.add("hot"); }
  btn.onclick=async ()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    if(cluttered){
      you.cluttered=true;
      // rule: play every playable card, cover the difference with Heat; car doesn't move
      const down = you.hand.filter(c=>!isHeat(c));
      let need = you.gear - down.length;
      for(const c of you.hand){ if(need>0 && isHeat(c)){ down.push(c); need--; } }
      you.play = down;
      you.hand = you.hand.filter(c=>!down.includes(c));
      log("Cluttered hand: your car doesn't move this round. Gear drops to 1st.","warn");
      toast("Cluttered hand — no move","hot");
      you.gear=1;
    }else{
      const chosen = you.hand.filter(c=>sel.has(c.id));
      /* RED WEATHER GATE — if this move would cross a gust / aquaplane corner,
         make the driver acknowledge it before committing (hard to miss). It's a
         heads-up, not a block: the random flip still resolves when you drive. */
      let k=0, lo=0, hi=0;
      for(const c of chosen){
        if(c.t==="speed") k+=c.v;
        else if(c.t==="up5") k+=5;
        else if(isUpg(c)){ const [a,b]=upgSpan(c.def); if(a===b) k+=a; else { lo+=a; hi+=b; } }
      }
      const ns=chosen.filter(isStress).length;
      const dMin=k+ns+lo, dMax=k+ns*4+hi;
      const crossU=new Set();
      for(let d=dMin; d<=dMax; d++) for(const c of crossingsForMove(you.total,d)) crossU.add(c);
      const wx = cornerWeatherWarnings([...crossU], dMax);
      if(wx.length){
        const body =
          `<div style="background:#3a0f0f;border:2px solid #ff5a3c;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;margin:-2px 0 10px">`
          + `<div style="color:#ff6a4d;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.5px;text-transform:uppercase;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:6px">⚠ Weather at this corner</div>`
          + `<div style="color:#ffd9cf;line-height:1.5">${wx.join("<br>")}</div></div>`
          + `<div style="color:var(--cream-dim,#b9adcf)">Your cards carry you across it. This is a heads-up, not a block — the flip resolves when you drive.</div>`;
        const ok = await confirmSpin(body, {tag:"Weather", yes:"Cross anyway"});
        if(!ok || !live(tk)) return;   // back off → stay on the card-selection screen
      }
      you.play = chosen;
      you.hand = you.hand.filter(c=>!sel.has(c.id));
    }
    renderGauges();
    nextShiftPlay();   // next local driver's steps 1–2, or on to movement
  };
}

/* ---- turn order & per-car resolution (steps 3–9) */
function beginMovementPhase(){
  clearHighlights();
  const racers = G.players.filter(p=>!p.finished);
  racers.sort((a,b)=> b.total-a.total || a.spot-b.spot);
  G.queue = racers;
  // adrenaline: last car this round; last TWO if 5+ cars started the race
  const adrN = G.players.length >= 5 ? 2 : 1;
  G.adrenalineIds = racers.slice(-adrN).map(p=>p.id);
  nextInQueue();
}
function waitingPanel(msg){
  $("#panel").innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">On track</div>
    <div class="phase-hint">${msg}</div>
    <div class="phase-hint" style="color:#6f6584">Open the race radio below to follow the action.</div>`;
}
async function nextInQueue(){
  if(G.weather && G.weather.aborted){ raceOver(); return; }   // thunderstorm red flag
  const p = G.queue.shift();
  if(!p){ endRound(); return; }
  if(G.champ && G.event && G.event.overtake3)
    p._aheadBefore = new Set(G.players.filter(q=>q!==p && !q.finished &&
      (q.total>p.total || (q.total===p.total && q.spot<p.spot))).map(q=>q.id));
  pointTurn(p);
  if(p.isBot){ waitingPanel(`${p.name} is driving…`); await (p.sim ? simBotTurn(p) : botTurn(p)); nextInQueue(); }
  else {
    G.activeHuman = p;
    renderGauges();
    hotseatHandoff(p, "drive your turn out — reveal, react, slipstream", playerReveal);
  }
}

/* ---------------- shared upgrade resolution (reveal-time, player & bots)
   Handles the mandatory side of a played upgrade: Scrap flips, the Heat cost
   (unpayable → the card is discarded and replaced by a flipped Basic card,
   per the rulebook), ＋ flips, and the adjust-limit registration. Returns
   human-readable notes. Choice values are handled separately (UI / heuristic). */
function resolveUpgradeMandatory(p, c, notes){
  const d=c.def;
  if(d.scrap){
    for(let i=0;i<d.scrap;i++){ const s=draw(p); if(s) p.discard.push(s); }
    notes.push(`${d.name}: scrapped ${d.scrap} card${d.scrap>1?"s":""} off the deck`);
  }
  if(d.heat){
    if(p.engine>=d.heat){ payHeat(p,d.heat); notes.push(`${d.name}: paid ${d.heat} Heat`); }
    else{
      // can't pay → discard the upgrade and replace it with a flipped Basic card
      p.play.splice(p.play.indexOf(c),1); p.discard.push(c);
      const r=flipForSpeed(p);
      if(r.card) p.play.push(Object.assign(r.card,{fromStress:true}));
      notes.push(`${d.name}: couldn't pay ${d.heat} Heat — replaced by a flipped ${r.v}`);
      return false;                     // card is gone; skip its other effects
    }
  }
  if(d.plus){
    let sum=0;
    for(let i=0;i<d.plus;i++){
      const r=flipForSpeed(p); sum+=r.v;
      if(r.card) p.play.push(Object.assign(r.card,{fromStress:true}));
      p.plusUsed=(p.plusUsed||0)+1;
    }
    c.plusResolved=sum;
    notes.push(`${d.name}: ＋ flipped ${sum}`);
  }
  if(d.limit){ p.limitAdj=(p.limitAdj||0)+d.limit; }
  return true;
}
/* total speed of a play area (choice cards must carry .chosen by now) */
function playSpeed(play){
  return play.reduce((s,c)=>{
    if(c.t==="speed") return s+c.v;
    if(c.t==="up5") return s+5;
    if(isUpg(c)){
      // ＋ flips were pushed into the play area as real Speed cards — the
      // shell only contributes its printed / chosen value here.
      return s + (c.def.choice ? (c.chosen ?? Math.min(...c.def.choice)) : (c.def.v||0));
    }
    return s; // up0, stress shells, junk
  },0);
}

/* ---- 3. Reveal & Move (player) */
function playerReveal(){
  const you=curHuman();
  if(you.cluttered){ playerReplenish(); return; }
  // resolve stress (＋) and mandatory upgrade effects now
  const notes=[];
  for(const c of you.play.slice()){
    if(isStress(c)){
      const r=flipForSpeed(you);
      c.resolved=r.v;
      you.plusUsed=(you.plusUsed||0)+1;
      if(r.card) you.play.push(Object.assign(r.card,{fromStress:true}));
      if(r.junk.length) notes.push(`Stress flip burned ${r.junk.length} card${r.junk.length>1?"s":""} off your deck`);
    }else if(isUpg(c)){
      resolveUpgradeMandatory(you, c, notes);
    }
  }
  // multi-value upgrades (Brakes etc.): let the player pick before the car moves
  const choicers = you.play.filter(c=>isUpg(c) && c.def.choice && c.chosen===undefined);
  if(choicers.length){ playerChooseValues(choicers, notes); return; }
  finishReveal(notes);
}

/* value picker for played choice cards (e.g. Brakes 1|2|3|4) */
function playerChooseValues(choicers, notes){
  const tk=arm();
  const you=curHuman();
  const panel=$("#panel");
  const fixed = playSpeed(you.play.filter(c=>!(isUpg(c)&&c.def.choice)));
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">3 · Reveal — choose values${phaseWho(you)}</div>
    <div class="phase-hint">Pick a speed for each multi-value Upgrade, then drive. Other cards give <b>+${fixed}</b>.</div>
    ${cornerDistanceHTML(you)}
    <div id="choicerows"></div>
    <div class="phase-hint" id="landnote" style="min-height:18px;margin-bottom:6px"></div>
    <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="chosen">Lock it in &amp; drive</button></div>`;
  const rows=$("#choicerows");
  choicers.forEach(c=>{
    c.chosen = Math.max(...c.def.choice);      // default: flat out
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.style.cssText="margin:6px 0";
    row.innerHTML=`<div class="olbl">${c.def.name} ${upgValueLabel(c.def)}</div>
      <div class="seg">${c.def.choice.map(v=>`<button data-v="${v}"${v===c.chosen?' class="sel"':''}>${v}</button>`).join("")}</div>`;
    row.querySelector(".seg").addEventListener("click",e=>{
      if(e.target.tagName!=="BUTTON") return;
      [...e.target.parentNode.children].forEach(b=>b.classList.remove("sel"));
      e.target.classList.add("sel"); c.chosen=+e.target.dataset.v; preview();
    });
    rows.appendChild(row);
  });
  function preview(){
    const total = playSpeed(you.play);
    highlightSpaces([{total:you.total+total, kind:"exact"}]);
    const bill = cornerHeatCost(total, crossingsForMove(you.total,total), you.limitAdj||0);
    $("#landnote").innerHTML = `Speed <b style="color:var(--purple-glow)">${total}</b>`+
      (bill>0 ? (bill<=you.engine
        ? ` · corner bill ≈<b style="color:var(--heat)">${bill} Heat</b> (engine ${you.engine})`
        : ` · <b style="color:#ff5a3c">⚠ SPIN OUT</b> — ${bill} owed vs ${you.engine} in Engine`) : "");
  }
  preview();
  $("#chosen").onclick=()=>{ if(live(tk)) finishReveal(notes); };
}

function finishReveal(notes){
  const you=curHuman();
  const cardSpeed = playSpeed(you.play);
  you.speed = cardSpeed;
  const moved = moveCar(you, cardSpeed);
  const blocked = moved<cardSpeed;
  renderGauges();
  if(G.adrenalineIds.includes(you.id)){ you.adrenaline=true; }
  const panel=$("#panel");
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">3 · Reveal &amp; move${phaseWho(you)}</div>
    <div class="phase-hint">Speed <b style="color:var(--purple-glow)">${cardSpeed}</b>${blocked?` — blocked, only moved ${moved}`:""}. ${notes.join(". ")}${you.adrenaline?` <b style="color:var(--purple-glow)">Adrenaline!</b> You're among the last this round: +1 speed and +1 Cooldown available.`:""}</div>
    ${heatForecastHTML(you, you.speed, you.crossings)}
    <div id="played"></div>
    <div class="btnrow"><button class="act" id="toreact">On to React</button></div>`;
  const pl=$("#played");
  you.play.forEach(c=>{ pl.appendChild(renderCard(c)); });
  log(`${G.hotseat?you.name+" reveals":"You reveal"} ${cardSpeed} speed${blocked?` (blocked at ${moved})`:""}.`,"me");
  const tk=arm();
  $("#toreact").onclick = ()=>{ if(live(tk)) playerReact(); };
}

/* ---- 5. React (Cooldown / Boost / adrenaline) */
function playerReact(){
  const tk=arm();
  const you=curHuman();
  // weather at the car's current resting position
  const sec = curSector(you);
  const wSec = isWeatherSector(sec);
  const wDef = G.weather ? G.weather.def.sector : {};
  const noCool  = wSec && !!wDef.noCool;                 // Extreme Heat weather sector
  const coolBonus = (wSec && wDef.cool) ? wDef.cool : 0; // Cold / Light Rain weather sector
  const heatCtrl = sectorTokOf(sec)==="heatctrl";        // Heat-control road-condition sector: Boost is free
  const upgCool = you.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.cool||0),0);   // ❄ symbols on played upgrades
  let baseCool = (classOf(you).cool[you.gear]||0);   // class Cooldown table per gear
  baseCool += coolBonus + upgCool;
  if(you.cooldownLeft===undefined || !you._coolInit){
    you.cooldownLeft = noCool ? 0 : baseCool + (you.adrenaline?1:0);
    you._coolInit=true;
  }
  const heatInHand = you.hand.filter(isHeat).length;
  /* tunnel: Reduce Stress moves a card from HAND to the discard — that's a
     discard, so it's barred inside a tunnel (Cooldown & Scrap stay legal) */
  const tunnelHere = inTunnelNow(you);
  /* optional upgrade symbols available this turn */
  const stressAllow = tunnelHere ? 0 : you.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.stress||0),0) - (you.stressShed||0);
  const stressInHand = you.hand.filter(isStress).length;
  const salvCards  = you.play.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def.salvage&&!you.usedSym.has(c.id));
  const refrCards  = you.play.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def.refresh);
  const directCards= you.hand.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def.direct);
  const canAccel   = you.play.some(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def.accel) && (you.plusUsed||0)>0 && !you.accelUsed;
  /* Super Cool (Air Intake): pull Heat out of the DISCARD back into the Engine */
  const superCards = you.play.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def.supercool&&!you.usedSym.has(c.id));
  const heatInDiscard = you.discard.filter(isHeat).length;
  const capRoom = heatCapFor(you)-you.engine;
  /* Draft (Front Wing): pooled spaces still unspent this round, legal landings */
  const draftPool = you.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.draft||0),0) - (you.draftUsed||0);
  const draftOpts = draftPool>0 ? draftTargets(you, draftPool) : [];
  let wNote="";
  if(noCool) wNote=`<div class="forecast warn" style="margin-bottom:8px">🌡 <b>Extreme heat</b> — no cooling allowed in this sector.</div>`;
  else if(coolBonus) wNote=`<div class="forecast" style="margin-bottom:8px">❄ <b>+${coolBonus} cooling</b> available in this sector.</div>`;
  if(heatCtrl) wNote+=`<div class="forecast" style="margin-bottom:8px">🛠 <b>Heat control</b> — your Boost is free here (no Heat).</div>`;
  if(tunnelHere) wNote+=`<div class="forecast warn" style="margin-bottom:8px">🕳 <b>Tunnel</b> — no discarding from hand in here (Reduce Stress is off, and step 8 will be skipped). Cooldown and Scrap are still fine.</div>`;
  const panel=$("#panel");
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">5 · React${phaseWho(you)}</div>
    <div class="phase-hint">Use symbols in any order. Cooldown returns Heat from hand to the Engine. Boost pays 1 Heat to flip a bonus speed card — it adds to your speed <b>and the corner bill below</b>.</div>
    ${wNote}
    ${heatForecastHTML(you, you.speed, you.crossings, "Owed at corner check")}
    ${engineBarHTML(you)}
    <div class="btnrow">
      <button class="act cool" id="coolbtn" ${you.cooldownLeft<1||heatInHand<1?"disabled":""}>Cooldown ×${you.cooldownLeft} <small>(${heatInHand}🔥 in hand)</small></button>
      <button class="act hot" id="boostbtn" ${you.boostUsed||(you.engine<1&&!heatCtrl)?"disabled":""}>Boost · ${heatCtrl?"free":"1 Heat"}</button>
    </div>
    ${(stressAllow>0&&stressInHand>0)||salvCards.length||directCards.length||canAccel||refrCards.length||superCards.length||draftOpts.length||draftPool>0 ? `
    <div class="olbl" style="margin:8px 0 2px">Upgrade symbols</div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="flex-wrap:wrap">
      ${stressAllow>0&&stressInHand>0?`<button class="act secondary" id="shedstress">＋↓ Discard a Stress <small>(×${Math.min(stressAllow,stressInHand)})</small></button>`:""}
      ${superCards.map(c=>`<button class="act secondary scool" data-cid="${c.id}" ${heatInDiscard<1||capRoom<1?"disabled":""}>🧲 Super Cool ${c.def.supercool} <small>(${heatInDiscard}🔥 in discard)</small></button>`).join("")}
      ${draftOpts.map(d=>`<button class="act secondary drft" data-d="${d}">⇥ Draft +${d} <small>(free move, no speed)</small></button>`).join("")}
      ${draftPool>0&&!draftOpts.length?`<button class="act secondary" disabled>⇥ Draft ×${draftPool} <small>(no car to tuck in behind)</small></button>`:""}
      ${salvCards.map(c=>`<button class="act secondary salv" data-cid="${c.id}">👁 Salvage ${c.def.salvage} <small>(${c.def.name})</small></button>`).join("")}
      ${directCards.map(c=>`<button class="act secondary dirp" data-cid="${c.id}">☞ Direct play ${c.def.name} ${upgValueLabel(c.def)}</button>`).join("")}
      ${canAccel?`<button class="act" id="accelbtn">⚡ Accelerate +${you.plusUsed}</button>`:""}
      ${refrCards.map(c=>`<button class="act secondary refr ${you.refreshIds.has(c.id)?"":"off"}" data-cid="${c.id}" style="${you.refreshIds.has(c.id)?"":"opacity:.65"}">⟳ ${c.def.name} → deck top: <b>${you.refreshIds.has(c.id)?"ON":"off"}</b></button>`).join("")}
    </div>`:""}
    <div class="btnrow">
      ${you.adrenaline && !you.adrSpeedUsed ? `<button class="act" id="adrbtn">Adrenaline +1 space</button>`:""}
      <button class="act secondary" id="donereact">Done reacting</button>
    </div>`;
  $("#coolbtn") && ($("#coolbtn").onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const idx = you.hand.findIndex(isHeat);
    if(idx<0) return; // no Heat card in hand — nothing to cool (never mint heat)
    if(you.engine >= heatCapFor(you)){ toast("Engine already at your heat cap","hot"); return; }
    const c = you.hand.splice(idx,1)[0];   // the card leaves circulation…
    you.engine++;                          // …and becomes 1 Heat: net zero
    you.cooldownLeft--;
    auditHeat(you,"cooling");
    log(`Cooldown: 1 ${c.t==="upheat"?"Extra ":""}Heat back into the Engine.`,"me");
    renderGauges(); playerReact();
  });
  $("#boostbtn") && ($("#boostbtn").onclick=async ()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    // Look ahead: a Boost flips a random 1–4 and may push the car over a corner
    // limit. Check every possible flip so we can warn before committing the Heat.
    const engineAfter = you.engine - (heatCtrl?0:1);
    let anySpin=false, allSpin=true, worst=0;
    for(let v=1; v<=4; v++){
      const nc=crossingsForMove(you.total, v);
      const bill=cornerHeatCost(you.speed+v, [...you.crossings, ...nc], you.limitAdj||0);
      if(bill>worst) worst=bill;
      if(bill>engineAfter) anySpin=true; else allSpin=false;
    }
    if(anySpin){
      const body = allSpin
        ? `A Boost here sends you over the next corner limit no matter what you flip. The check will owe more Heat than the <b>${engineAfter}</b> left in the Engine after the Boost — you'll <b>spin out</b>, drop to 1st gear and pick up Stress.`
        : `A Boost risks the next corner: a high flip could owe up to <b>${worst} Heat</b> against just <b>${engineAfter}</b> left in the Engine after paying for the Boost, which would <b>spin you out</b>.`;
      const ok = await confirmSpin(body, {tag:"Boost", yes:"Boost anyway"});
      if(!ok || !live(tk)) return;
    }
    if(!heatCtrl){ if(you.engine<1) return; payHeat(you,1); }  // Heat control: Boost costs no Heat
    you.boostUsed=true;
    const r=flipForSpeed(you);
    if(r.card) you.discard.push(r.card);
    you.speed += r.v;
    const m=moveCar(you,r.v);
    log(`Boost! Flipped a ${r.v}${m<r.v?` (blocked at ${m})`:""}.`,"me");
    toast(`Boost +${r.v}`,"hot"); renderGauges(); playerReact();
  });
  $("#adrbtn") && ($("#adrbtn").onclick=async ()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    // +1 speed can itself push you over the next corner — warn before committing.
    const nc=crossingsForMove(you.total, 1);
    const bill=cornerHeatCost(you.speed+1, [...you.crossings, ...nc], you.limitAdj||0);
    if(bill>you.engine){
      const body = `Adrenaline's +1 speed pushes you over a limit-${[...new Set([...you.crossings,...nc])].map(limitAt).join(" & ")} corner. The check would owe <b>${bill} Heat</b> against just <b>${you.engine}</b> in the Engine — you'll <b>spin out</b>, drop to 1st gear and pick up Stress.`;
      const ok = await confirmSpin(body, {tag:"Adrenaline", yes:"Push anyway"});
      if(!ok || !live(tk)) return;
    }
    you.adrSpeedUsed=true; you.speed+=1;
    const m=moveCar(you,1);
    log(`Adrenaline: +1 speed${m<1?" (blocked)":""}.`,"me");
    renderGauges(); playerReact();
  });
  $("#shedstress") && ($("#shedstress").onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    if(inTunnelNow(you)){ toast("Tunnel — no discarding","hot"); return; }
    const idx=you.hand.findIndex(isStress);
    if(idx<0) return;
    you.discard.push(you.hand.splice(idx,1)[0]);
    you.stressShed=(you.stressShed||0)+1;
    log("Reduce Stress: a Stress card goes to the discard.","me");
    renderGauges(); playerReact();
  });
  panel.querySelectorAll(".salv").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const card=you.play.find(c=>c.id===b.dataset.cid);
    if(card) openSalvage(card);
  });
  /* Super Cool: up to # Heat cards jump from the discard straight to the Engine */
  panel.querySelectorAll(".scool").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const card=you.play.find(c=>c.id===b.dataset.cid);
    if(!card) return;
    let n=card.def.supercool, moved=0;
    while(n>0 && you.engine<heatCapFor(you)){
      const i=you.discard.findIndex(isHeat); if(i<0) break;
      you.discard.splice(i,1); you.engine++; n--; moved++;
    }
    if(!moved){ toast("No Heat in the discard (or engine full)","hot"); return; }
    you.usedSym.add(card.id);
    auditHeat(you,"reclaim");
    log(`Super Cool: ${moved} Heat pulled from the discard straight back into the Engine.`,"me");
    renderGauges(); playerReact();
  });
  /* Draft: free spaces up to the car ahead — no speed, but corner lines count */
  panel.querySelectorAll(".drft").forEach(b=>b.onclick=async ()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const d=+b.dataset.d;
    const nc=crossingsForMove(you.total,d);
    const costNow=cornerHeatCost(you.speed,you.crossings,you.limitAdj||0);
    const cost=cornerHeatCost(you.speed,[...you.crossings,...nc],you.limitAdj||0);
    if(nc.length && cost>you.engine && cost>costNow){
      const ok=await confirmSpin(`Drafting +${d} crosses a limit-${nc.map(limitAt).join(" & ")} corner at speed ${you.speed}. The check would owe <b>${cost} Heat</b> against just <b>${you.engine}</b> in the Engine — you'll <b>spin out</b>.`,{tag:"Draft",yes:"Draft anyway"});
      if(!ok||!live(tk)) return;
    }
    doDraft(you,d);
    log(`Draft: +${d} space${d>1?"s":""}, tucking in behind the car ahead (no speed added).`,"me");
    renderGauges(); playerReact();
  });
  panel.querySelectorAll(".dirp").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const card=you.hand.find(c=>c.id===b.dataset.cid);
    if(card) directPlayCard(card);
  });
  $("#accelbtn") && ($("#accelbtn").onclick=async ()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const n=you.plusUsed;
    const nc=crossingsForMove(you.total, n);
    const bill=cornerHeatCost(you.speed+n, [...you.crossings, ...nc], you.limitAdj||0);
    if(bill>you.engine){
      const ok=await confirmSpin(`Accelerating +${n} pushes you over a corner limit — the check would owe <b>${bill} Heat</b> against <b>${you.engine}</b> in the tank: <b>spin out</b>.`,{tag:"Accelerate",yes:"Send it"});
      if(!ok||!live(tk)) return;
    }
    you.accelUsed=true; you.speed+=n;
    const m=moveCar(you,n);
    log(`Accelerate: +${n} speed (one per ＋ used)${m<n?` (blocked at ${m})`:""}.`,"me");
    renderGauges(); playerReact();
  });
  panel.querySelectorAll(".refr").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const id=b.dataset.cid;
    you.refreshIds.has(id) ? you.refreshIds.delete(id) : you.refreshIds.add(id);
    playerReact();
  });
  $("#donereact").onclick=()=>{ if(!live(tk)) return; you._coolInit=false; playerSlipstream(); };
}

/* Salvage picker: choose up to # cards from the discard, shuffle them into the deck */
function openSalvage(card){
  const you=curHuman();
  const n=card.def.salvage;
  const opts=you.discard.slice();
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex";
  const sel=new Set();
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
    <h2>👁 Salvage ${n}</h2>
    <div class="tag">${card.def.name} · pick up to ${n} discard cards to shuffle back into your deck</div>
    <div id="salvhand" style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;max-height:44vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:6px 0"></div>
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:12px">
      <button class="act" id="salvgo" disabled>Shuffle into deck</button>
      <button class="act secondary" id="salvskip">Cancel</button>
    </div></div>`;
  const wrap=el.querySelector("#salvhand");
  if(!opts.length) wrap.innerHTML=`<div class="phase-hint">Your discard pile is empty.</div>`;
  opts.forEach(c=>{
    const cd=renderCard(c);
    cd.onclick=()=>{
      if(sel.has(c.id)) sel.delete(c.id);
      else if(sel.size<n) sel.add(c.id);
      cd.classList.toggle("sel",sel.has(c.id));
      el.querySelector("#salvgo").disabled=sel.size===0;
    };
    wrap.appendChild(cd);
  });
  el.querySelector("#salvskip").onclick=()=>{ el.style.display="none"; el.innerHTML=""; };
  el.querySelector("#salvgo").onclick=()=>{
    const going=you.discard.filter(c=>sel.has(c.id));
    you.discard=you.discard.filter(c=>!sel.has(c.id));
    you.deck.push(...going); shuffle(you.deck);
    you.usedSym.add(card.id);
    log(`Salvage: ${going.length} card${going.length>1?"s":""} shuffled back into your deck.`,"me");
    el.style.display="none"; el.innerHTML="";
    renderGauges(); playerReact();
  };
}

/* Direct Play: play an upgrade from hand during React — it applies as if it
   had been revealed in step 3 (speed moves the car now, symbols become live). */
function directPlayCard(card){
  const you=curHuman();
  you.hand.splice(you.hand.indexOf(card),1);
  you.play.push(card);
  const notes=[];
  const kept = resolveUpgradeMandatory(you, card, notes);
  const applyMove=()=>{
    const newSpeed=playSpeed(you.play);
    const delta=newSpeed-you.speed;
    if(delta>0){ const m=moveCar(you,delta); you.speed=newSpeed;
      log(`Direct play ${card.def?card.def.name:""}: +${delta} speed${m<delta?` (blocked at ${m})`:""}. ${notes.join(". ")}`,"me"); }
    else log(`Direct play resolved. ${notes.join(". ")}`,"me");
    renderGauges(); you._coolInit=false; playerReact();   // re-init Cooldown to include its ❄ symbols
  };
  if(kept && card.def.choice && card.chosen===undefined){
    // quick value picker for a direct-played Brakes-style card
    const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex";
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet"><h2>${card.def.name} ${upgValueLabel(card.def)}</h2>
      <div class="tag">Choose the speed to apply now</div>
      <div class="seg" id="dpseg">${card.def.choice.map(v=>`<button data-v="${v}">${v}</button>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
    el.querySelector("#dpseg").addEventListener("click",e=>{
      if(e.target.tagName!=="BUTTON") return;
      card.chosen=+e.target.dataset.v;
      el.style.display="none"; el.innerHTML="";
      applyMove();
    });
  } else applyMove();
}

/* ---- 6. Slipstream */
function playerSlipstream(chain){
  const you=curHuman();
  /* ≫≫ Extra Slipstream (Heavy Rain): count the symbols on played cards once,
     at the start of the step. Each successful slipstream may chain into one
     more if a symbol is left AND the car ended in a new slipstream position. */
  if(!chain) you._xslipLeft = you.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.xslip||0),0);
  const here=phys(you.total), ahead=phys(you.total+1);
  const eligible = carsAt(here,you).length>0 || carsAt(ahead,you).length>0;
  // sector / weather slipstream modifiers (car must be in the sector to benefit)
  const sec = curSector(you);
  const wSec = isWeatherSector(sec);
  const wDef = G.weather ? G.weather.def.sector : {};
  let bonus = 0, blocked = false, why = [];
  const upSlip = you.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.slip||0),0);   // ≫ symbols on played upgrades
  if(upSlip){ bonus += upSlip; why.push(`upgrade Slipstream Boost +${upSlip}`); }
  if(sectorTokOf(sec)==="slip"){ bonus += 1; why.push("road-condition Slipstream Boost +1"); } // RC Slipstream Boost token
  if(wSec){
    if(wDef.slip){ bonus += wDef.slip; why.push(`${G.weather.def.name.toLowerCase()} +${wDef.slip}`); } // Sunny +2 / Cloudy +1
    if(wDef.noSlip){ blocked = true; why.push("mist — no slipstreaming"); }                            // Mist
    if(wDef.wind){                                                                                     // Strong Wind
      const cls = windClassAt(sec);
      if(cls==="head"){ bonus += 2; why.push("headwind +2"); }
      else if(cls==="tail"){ blocked = true; why.push("tailwind cancels the slipstream"); }
    }
  }
  const slipDist = classOf(you).slipBase + bonus;
  const wouldFinish = (you.total < raceDist()) && (you.total+slipDist >= raceDist());
  if(!eligible || blocked || wouldFinish || finishedLine(you) || (chain && you._xslipLeft<1)){
    if(eligible && blocked) log(`Slipstream unavailable here (${why.join(", ")}).`);
    playerCornerCheck(); return;
  }
  /* what slipping would add to the corner bill (speed itself doesn't change) */
  const newCross = crossingsForMove(you.total, slipDist);
  const costNow  = cornerHeatCost(you.speed, you.crossings, you.limitAdj||0);
  const costSlip = cornerHeatCost(you.speed, [...you.crossings, ...newCross], you.limitAdj||0);
  const delta = costSlip - costNow;
  let slipNote = "";
  if(bonus) slipNote += `<div class="forecast">Slipstream boosted to <b>+${slipDist}</b> spaces (${why.join(", ")}).</div>`;
  if(newCross.length){
    if(costSlip > you.engine && delta > 0)
      slipNote += `<div class="forecast spin">Slipstreaming crosses a limit-${newCross.map(limitAt).join(" & ")} corner at speed ${you.speed} — total bill ${costSlip} Heat vs ${you.engine} in the Engine: ⚠ SPIN OUT!</div>`;
    else if(delta > 0)
      slipNote += `<div class="forecast warn">Slipstreaming crosses a limit-${newCross.map(limitAt).join(" & ")} corner line — adds <b>+${delta} Heat</b> to the check (total ${costSlip}, engine ${you.engine}).</div>`;
    else
      slipNote += `<div class="forecast">Slipstreaming crosses a corner line, but speed ${you.speed} is under the limit — free.</div>`;
  }
  const panel=$("#panel");
  const xNote = (!chain && you._xslipLeft>0)
    ? `<div class="forecast">≫≫ <b>Extra Slipstream ×${you._xslipLeft}</b> in your play area — if this slipstream leaves you in a new slipstream position, you may chain another one.</div>` : "";
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">6 · ${chain?"Extra Slipstream":"Slipstream"}${phaseWho(you)}</div>
    <div class="phase-hint">${chain
      ? `Your slipstream ended in a <b>new slipstream position</b> — an ≫≫ Extra Slipstream symbol lets you take another full ${slipDist}-space slipstream (${you._xslipLeft} symbol${you._xslipLeft>1?"s":""} left). It doesn't add speed, but any corner line you cross still gets checked.`
      : `You're in the slipstream of another car. Take the full ${slipDist} free spaces? It doesn't add speed, but any corner line you cross still gets checked.`}</div>
    ${xNote}${slipNote}
    <div class="btnrow">
      <button class="act" id="slipyes">${chain?"Extra Slipstream":"Slipstream"} +${slipDist}</button>
      <button class="act secondary" id="slipno">Stay put</button>
    </div>`;
  const tk=arm();
  $("#slipyes").onclick=async ()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    // The slipstream is deterministic, so we know exactly whether it spins the car out.
    const willSpin = newCross.length>0 && costSlip > you.engine && delta > 0;
    if(willSpin){
      const body = `Taking the slipstream crosses a limit-${newCross.map(limitAt).join(" & ")} corner at speed ${you.speed}. The check owes <b>${costSlip} Heat</b> but the Engine holds only <b>${you.engine}</b> — you'll <b>spin out</b>, drop to 1st gear and pick up Stress.`;
      const ok = await confirmSpin(body, {tag:"Slipstream", yes:"Slipstream anyway"});
      if(!ok || !live(tk)) return;
    }
    if(chain) you._xslipLeft--;                        // an ≫≫ symbol is spent per extra slipstream
    const nb=you.crossings.length;
    const m=moveCar(you,slipDist);
    if(G.champ) for(const ct of you.crossings.slice(nb))
      if(isPressLine(ct)) (you.pressPending||(you.pressPending=new Set())).add(phys(ct));
    log(m>0?`${G.hotseat?you.name+" slipstreams":"You slipstream"} ${m} space${m>1?"s":""}${chain?" (Extra Slipstream)":""}.`:"Slipstream blocked — no move.","me");
    await carSettle(you);
    /* Extra Slipstream: chain another offer only if the move actually landed
       somewhere new — the chained call re-checks eligibility (new slipstream
       position), sector/weather bonuses and the finish-line ban from there. */
    if(m>0 && you._xslipLeft>0){ playerSlipstream(true); return; }
    playerCornerCheck();
  };
  $("#slipno").onclick=()=>{ if(live(tk)) playerCornerCheck(); };
}

/* ---- 7. Check Corner — shared by the player and simulated drivers */
function runCornerCheck(p){
  const isYou = !p.isBot;
  const lines=[...new Set(p.crossings)].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const wDef = G.weather ? G.weather.def.sector : {};
  for(const ct of lines){
    const idx = cornerIdxOf(ct);

    /* Aquaplaning (Heavy Rain / Thunderstorm): passing a weather-governed corner
       approach above speed 10 flips the top card — a Stress card spins you off. */
    if(wDef.aqua && approachIsWeatherSector(ct) && p.speed>10){
      const c = draw(p);
      if(c){
        const stress = isStress(c);
        p.discard.push(c);
        if(stress){ weatherSpinOut(p, ct, p.isBot?`${p.name} AQUAPLANES`:"AQUAPLANE", true); return; }
        if(isYou) log(`Aquaplaning at speed ${p.speed} — you held it together.`);
      }
    }

    /* effective corner speed = card+Boost speed, adjusted by wind + a wind-gust flip */
    let eff = p.speed;
    if(wDef.wind && approachIsWeatherSector(ct)){
      const cls = windClassAt(sectorBefore(ct));
      if(cls==="tail"){ eff+=1; } else if(cls==="head"){ eff-=1; }
    }
    if(wDef.gust && cornerBordersWeatherSector(idx)){
      const c = draw(p);
      if(c){ if(isSpeed14(c)){ eff += c.v; if(isYou) log(`Wind gust at the corner: +${c.v} to corner speed (now ${eff}).`,"warn"); } p.discard.push(c); }
    }

    const lim = limitAt(ct) + (p.limitAdj||0);   // Tires/Wings adjust-limit symbols (this player, this turn)
    const excess = eff - lim;
    /* Press Corner (Championship): beating this line's limit by 2+ (after
       tokens) earns a Sponsorship card — one per pass, paid out at the end of
       the turn and cancelled if the turn ends in a spin-out. The slipstream
       route into the same set lives in the Slipstream step. */
    if(G.champ && isPressLine(ct)){
      const pv=G.event||{};
      if(pv.pressUnder ? (eff<lim) : (excess>=2))
        (p.pressPending || (p.pressPending=new Set())).add(phys(ct));
    }
    if(excess<=0){ if(isYou) log(`Corner (limit ${lim}) taken clean at speed ${eff}.`); continue; }
    const cost = excess + (cornerHasOverheat(ct)?1:0);
    if(p.engine>=cost){
      payHeat(p,cost);
      if(isYou){
        log(`Corner limit ${lim}, speed ${eff}: Engine takes ${cost} Heat${cornerHasOverheat(ct)?" (overheat +1)":""}.`,"warn");
        toast(`Over the limit · ${cost} Heat`,"hot");
      } else log(`${p.name} runs the limit-${lim} corner at ${eff} — pays ${cost} Heat.`);
    }else{
      const induced = wDef.gust && cornerBordersWeatherSector(idx); // gust-forced over-speed counts toward a red flag
      weatherSpinOut(p, ct, p.isBot?`${p.name} SPINS OUT`:"SPIN OUT", induced);
      return;
    }
  }
}
function playerCornerCheck(){
  const you=curHuman();
  runCornerCheck(you);
  resolveTurnSponsors(you);        // Press Corner / Event sponsorships pay out now
  you.crossings=[];
  renderGauges();
  if(G.weather && G.weather.aborted){ raceOver(); return; }
  playerDiscard();
}

/* shared spin-out: dump Engine Heat, drop back before the corner, take Stress,
   reset to 1st gear. `induced` marks weather-caused spin-outs for the storm red flag. */
function weatherSpinOut(p, cornerTotal, tag, induced){
  /* ---- rival spin-loop breaker -----------------------------------------
     A bot with a dry Engine facing a tight corner (limit 1–2) could spin on
     the same Corner Line forever: each spin dumps its Heat and stuffs its
     hand with Stress, so the next attempt spins too. Real drivers don't do
     that — the SECOND consecutive spin at the same Corner Line is therefore
     downgraded to a "gather": the bot sheds whatever Heat it has, limps to
     the first free Space past the line, takes just 1 Stress and drops to 1st
     gear, and the race moves on. Humans are never touched by this. */
  if(p.isBot){
    if(p._spinLoop && p._spinLoop.ct === cornerTotal) p._spinLoop.n++;
    else p._spinLoop = { ct: cornerTotal, n: 1 };
    if(p._spinLoop.n >= 2){
      const shed = p.engine;
      p.discard.push(...Array(shed).fill().map(()=>makeCard("heat")));
      p.engine = 0;
      let t = cornerTotal; while(spaceFull(phys(t), p)) t++;
      p.total = t; takeSpot(p); renderCars();
      p.hand.push(makeCard("stress"));
      p.gear = 1; p.spunOut = true;
      if(p.pressPending) p.pressPending.clear();
      p._spinLoop = null;
      auditHeat(p, "spin gathered");
      log(`${p.name} nearly loses it at the limit-${limitAt(cornerTotal)} corner AGAIN — gathers it up and limps through. 1 Stress, back to 1st gear.`, "warn");
      registerWeatherSpin(!!induced);
      return;
    }
  }
  const paid=p.engine;
  p.discard.push(...Array(paid).fill().map(()=>makeCard("heat")));
  p.engine=0;
  let t=cornerTotal-1; while(spaceFull(phys(t),p)) t--;
  p.total=t; takeSpot(p); renderCars();
  const extra = (p.gear<=2?1:2) + (classOf(p).spinStress||0);
  for(let i=0;i<extra;i++) p.hand.push(makeCard("stress"));
  p.gear=1; p.spunOut=true;
  if(p.pressPending) p.pressPending.clear();      // no sponsorship on a spin-out turn
  auditHeat(p,"spin out");
  /* Event "No Second Chances": any spin-out is disqualifying */
  if(G.champ && G.event && G.event.spinDQ && !p.dq){
    p.dq=true; p.finished=true; p.finishRound=G.round;
    G.finishOrder.push(p); sortFinishOrder && sortFinishOrder();
    log(`🏴 ${p.isBot?p.name+" is":"You are"} DISQUALIFIED — the stewards show the black flag. No points this race.`,"warn");
    if(!p.isBot) toast("🏴 DISQUALIFIED","hot");
    renderCars(); renderStandings && renderStandings();
  }
  log(`${tag} before the limit-${limitAt(cornerTotal)} corner! ${paid} Heat gone, ${extra} Stress in hand, back to 1st gear.`,"warn");
  if(!p.isBot){
    toast(tag+"!","hot");
    // image modal + crash sting for the human driver's spin-out
    if(window.HeatAudio) window.showSpinOut(`${paid} Heat gone · ${extra} Stress in hand · back to 1st gear.`);
  }
  registerWeatherSpin(!!induced);
}

/* ---- 8. Discard */
function playerDiscard(){
  const you=curHuman();
  if(inTunnelNow(you)){
    // tunnel rule: no discarding from hand while inside — beats everything
    log("Tunnel — no discarding from hand while your car is inside.","warn");
    toast("Tunnel — no discarding","hot");
    playerReplenish(); return;
  }
  const options = you.hand.filter(discardable);
  if(options.length===0){ playerReplenish(); return; }
  const sel=new Set();
  const panel=$("#panel");
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="phase-title">8 · Discard${phaseWho(you)} <span style="color:var(--cream-dim)">(optional)</span></div>
    <div class="phase-hint">Bin cards you don't want next round — you'll draw back to 7. Heat and Stress can't be discarded.</div>
    <div id="hand"></div>
    <div class="btnrow">
      <button class="act secondary" id="skipdisc">Keep hand</button>
      <button class="act" id="dodisc" disabled>Discard selected</button>
    </div>`;
  const handEl=$("#hand");
  you.hand.forEach(c=>{
    const el=renderCard(c);
    if(!discardable(c)) el.classList.add("dim");
    else el.onclick=()=>{ sel.has(c.id)?sel.delete(c.id):sel.add(c.id);
      el.classList.toggle("sel",sel.has(c.id)); $("#dodisc").disabled=sel.size===0; };
    handEl.appendChild(el);
  });
  const tk=arm();
  $("#skipdisc").onclick=()=>{ if(live(tk)) playerReplenish(); };
  $("#dodisc").onclick=()=>{
    if(!live(tk)) return;
    const going=you.hand.filter(c=>sel.has(c.id));
    you.hand=you.hand.filter(c=>!sel.has(c.id));
    you.discard.push(...going);
    log(`${G.hotseat?you.name+" discards":"You discard"} ${going.length} card${going.length>1?"s":""}.`,"me");
    playerReplenish();
  };
}

/* ---- 9. Replenish */
function playerReplenish(){
  const you=curHuman();
  // Sponsorship cards are one-time use: played ones leave the game entirely
  // (they feed the Sponsorship deck's mid-race reshuffle instead).
  const burnt = you.play.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def.sponsor);
  if(burnt.length){
    you.play = you.play.filter(c=>!burnt.includes(c));
    if(G.champ) G.champ.sponsorUsed.push(...burnt.map(c=>c.def.id));
    log(`🤝 ${burnt.map(c=>c.def.name).join(", ")} — one-time use, removed from the game.`,"me");
  }
  // Refresh symbols: chosen cards ride back on top of the draw deck
  const keep = you.play.filter(c=>you.refreshIds && you.refreshIds.has(c.id));
  you.play.forEach(c=>{ if(isUpg(c)){ c.chosen=undefined; c.plusResolved=undefined; } });
  you.discard.push(...you.play.filter(c=>!keep.includes(c))); you.play=[];
  keep.forEach(c=>{ c.chosen=undefined; c.plusResolved=undefined; you.deck.push(c); });
  if(keep.length) log(`Refresh: ${keep.map(c=>c.def.name).join(", ")} back on top of your deck.`,"me");
  you.stressShed=0;
  applyGravel(you);                          // gravel spot: 1 Heat out of the Engine (if any)
  drawToHand(you, handSize()-you.hand.length);
  renderGauges();
  checkFinish(you);
  nextInQueue();
}

/* ---------------- Legend (bot) turns — Legends module logic */
async function botTurn(p){
  if(!G.legendRevealed){
    G.legendRevealed=true;
    if(G.legendDeck.length===0) G.legendDeck=shuffle([...LEGEND_CARDS]);
    const c=G.legendDeck.pop();
    G.legendCard={ts:c.ts+G.difficulty, d:c.d};
    log(`Legend card: top speed ${G.legendCard.ts}, diamond ${G.legendCard.d}.`);
  }
  // Legend bots all share one pace card, so they'd otherwise move identically.
  // The ace (Le Cock) gets a small top-speed + diamond bump every round so he
  // consistently runs a touch quicker than the rest of the field — keeping him
  // among the two fastest bots. The "never two corners in one move" clamp below
  // still applies, so the bonus never breaks the cornering rule.
  let {ts,d}=G.legendCard;
  if(p.ace){ ts+=2; d+=1; }
  const nc=nextCornerTotal(p.total);
  let move, why;
  if(nc===Infinity){ move=ts; why="flat out to the flag"; }
  else if(nc - p.total <= TRACK.legendsLine){
    // clearing the corner
    move = limitAt(nc)+d; why=`clears the corner (${limitAt(nc)}+${d})`;
    const nc2=nextCornerTotal(nc);
    if(p.total+move >= nc2) move = nc2 - 1 - p.total; // never 2 corners in one move
  }else if(p.total+ts < nc){ move=ts; why=`runs top speed ${ts}`; }
  else { move = (nc-1-d) - p.total; why=`brakes to ${d} before the corner`; }
  const startTotal=p.total;
  if(move>0){
    const m=moveCar(p,move,{trackCrossings:false});
    log(`${p.name} ${why} → ${m} space${m===1?"":"s"}${m<move?" (blocked)":""}.`);
    botWeatherCheck(p, startTotal, m);
  }else log(`${p.name} holds position.`);
  checkFinish(p);
  await carSettle(p);              // let the car finish driving before the next turn
  await wait(220);
}

/* ============================================================
   SIMULATED DRIVERS — rivals that play a full human-style round
   (own deck, hand, engine & gears) instead of the Legends deck.
   Skill comes from SIM_PROFILES: gear choice, card choice, Boost,
   Cooldown, slipstream and how much corner risk they'll accept.
   ============================================================ */
async function simBotTurn(p){
  const prof = profileForBot(p);
  // reset per-round scratch
  resetRoundScratch(p); p.play=[];

  simShift(p, prof);                                  // 1. shift gears

  // 2. play cards
  const playableN = p.hand.filter(c=>!isHeat(c)).length;
  if(playableN < p.gear){
    // cluttered: dump what we can, cover with Heat, don't move, drop to 1st
    p.cluttered=true;
    const down = p.hand.filter(c=>!isHeat(c));
    let need = p.gear - down.length;
    for(const c of p.hand){ if(need>0 && isHeat(c)){ down.push(c); need--; } }
    p.play = down; p.hand = p.hand.filter(c=>!down.includes(c));
    p.gear = 1;
    log(`${p.name} has a cluttered hand — coasts this round, drops to 1st.`);
    p.discard.push(...p.play); p.play=[];
    applyGravel(p);                          // still the end of the turn — gravel applies
    drawToHand(p, 7-p.hand.length);
    checkFinish(p); renderCars(); renderGauges();
    await wait(botDelay()); return;
  }
  const chosen = simChooseCards(p, prof);
  p.play = chosen;
  p.hand = p.hand.filter(c=>!chosen.includes(c));

  // 3. reveal & move (resolve stress ＋ and upgrade mandatory effects first)
  const upNotes=[];
  for(const c of p.play.slice()){
    if(isStress(c)){
      const r = flipForSpeed(p); c.resolved=r.v; p.plusUsed=(p.plusUsed||0)+1;
      if(r.card) p.play.push(Object.assign(r.card,{fromStress:true}));
    }else if(isUpg(c)){
      resolveUpgradeMandatory(p, c, upNotes);
    }
  }
  // choice cards: run as fast as the next corner allows
  for(const c of p.play){
    if(isUpg(c) && c.def.choice && c.chosen===undefined){
      const others = playSpeed(p.play.filter(x=>x!==c));
      let best=Math.min(...c.def.choice);
      for(const v of [...c.def.choice].sort((a,b)=>a-b)){
        const spd=others+v;
        const bill=cornerHeatCost(spd, crossingsForMove(p.total,spd), p.limitAdj||0);
        if(bill<=Math.max(0,p.engine-prof.heatFloor) || (bill<=p.engine && prof.riskTolerance>=1)) best=v;
      }
      c.chosen=best;
    }
  }
  const cardSpeed = playSpeed(p.play);
  p.speed = cardSpeed;
  const moved = moveCar(p, cardSpeed);
  log(`${p.name} · gear ${p.gear} · speed ${cardSpeed}${moved<cardSpeed?` (blocked at ${moved})`:""}.`);
  if(G.adrenalineIds.includes(p.id)) p.adrenaline=true;
  renderGauges();
  await carSettle(p);              // watch the move land before Boost/slipstream resolve
  await wait(120);

  simReact(p, prof);        // 5. Cooldown (top up the Engine) → Boost → adrenaline
  simDraft(p);              // 5b. Front Wing Draft: free spaces up to the car ahead
  simSlip(p, prof);         // 6. slipstream if it helps and is safe
  runCornerCheck(p);        // 7. corner check (may spin out)
  resolveTurnSponsors(p);   // Press Corner / Event sponsorships pay out now
  p.crossings=[];
  if(!(G.weather && G.weather.aborted)) simDiscard(p, prof);   // 8. light hand cycling

  // 9. replenish — bots refresh any card worth 3+ back onto the deck
  const burnt = p.play.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def.sponsor);
  if(burnt.length){
    p.play = p.play.filter(c=>!burnt.includes(c));
    if(G.champ) G.champ.sponsorUsed.push(...burnt.map(c=>c.def.id));
    log(`${p.name} cashes in ${burnt.map(c=>c.def.name).join(", ")} — removed from the game.`);
  }
  const keep = p.play.filter(c=>isUpg(c) && c.def.refresh && upgExpected(c.def)>=3);
  p.play.forEach(c=>{ if(isUpg(c)){ c.chosen=undefined; c.plusResolved=undefined; } });
  p.discard.push(...p.play.filter(c=>!keep.includes(c))); p.play=[];
  p.deck.push(...keep);
  applyGravel(p);                            // gravel spot: 1 Heat out of the Engine (if any)
  drawToHand(p, handSize()-p.hand.length);
  checkFinish(p);
  renderCars(); renderGauges();
  await wait(botDelay());
}

/* 1 · gear: match gear to the distance to the next corner, tempered by profile */
function simShift(p, prof){
  const nc = nextCornerTotal(p.total);
  const dc = nc===Infinity ? 99 : nc - p.total;
  const lim = nc===Infinity ? 99 : limitAt(nc);
  let want;
  if(dc>12) want=4;
  else if(dc>8) want=3;
  else if(dc>4) want=(lim>=5?3:2);
  else if(dc>2) want=2;
  else want=1;
  if(prof.upshiftBias>=0.7 && want<4 && dc>3) want++;          // brave drivers carry a taller gear
  if(prof.cornerMargin>=1 && want>1 && dc<=6) want--;          // cautious drivers gear down early
  /* skill: protect the Heat cycle. Gears 1 and 2 are the ONLY source of Cooldown,
     so a driver whose Engine is running dry gears down to refill rather than
     coasting into a corner it cannot pay for. */
  if(prof.skill){
    const heatInHand = p.hand.filter(c=>isHeat(c)).length;
    const rescue = (prof.rescue === undefined) ? 1 : prof.rescue;
    if(rescue >= 2){
      if(p.engine<=2 && heatInHand>0 && dc<=10) want = Math.min(want,1);
      else if(p.engine<=2 && dc<=6)             want = Math.min(want,2);
    } else if(rescue >= 1){
      if(p.engine<=1 && heatInHand>0 && dc<=6)  want = Math.min(want,1);
      else if(p.engine<=1 && dc<=4)             want = Math.min(want,2);
    }
    if(p.engine===0 && dc<=3) want = 1;      // nothing to pay with: crawl the corner
  }
  want = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, want));
  // reachable this shift: ±1 free, ±2 costs 1 Heat
  let g = want, diff = want - p.gear;
  if(Math.abs(diff)>2) g = p.gear + Math.sign(diff)*2;
  // rookies avoid paying Heat for a 2-gear jump unless braking hard into a corner
  if(Math.abs(g-p.gear)===2 && prof.aggression<0.4 && dc>2) g = p.gear + Math.sign(g-p.gear);
  let cost = shiftHeatCost(p.gear, g, weatherShiftPenaltyHere(p));
  if(cost>p.engine){ g = p.gear + Math.sign(g-p.gear); cost = shiftHeatCost(p.gear,g,weatherShiftPenaltyHere(p)); }
  // don't gear into a certain clutter if a lower gear is playable
  const playable = p.hand.filter(c=>!isHeat(c)).length;
  if(g>playable && playable>=1){ g=Math.min(g,playable); cost=shiftHeatCost(p.gear,g,weatherShiftPenaltyHere(p)); }
  if(cost>0 && cost<=p.engine){ payHeat(p,cost); }
  p.gear = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, g));
}

/* 2 · cards: pick exactly `gear` non-Heat cards that best fit the situation */
function simChooseCards(p, prof){
  const playable = p.hand.filter(c=>!isHeat(c));
  const g = p.gear;
  const nc = nextCornerTotal(p.total);
  const lim = nc===Infinity ? 99 : limitAt(nc);
  const val = c => c.t==="speed" ? c.v : c.t==="up5" ? 5 : isStress(c) ? 2.5
    : isUpg(c) ? Math.max(0.5, upgExpected(c.def) - (c.def.heat && p.engine<=(c.def.heat+prof.heatFloor) ? 3 : 0) + (c.def.limit||0)*0.8)
    : 0; // stress ≈ expected flip; upgrades ≈ expected speed, discounted when the heat cost bites
  const combos = kcombos(playable, g);
  let best=null, bestScore=-1e9;
  for(const combo of combos){
    const sum = combo.reduce((s,c)=>s+val(c),0);
    const stressN = combo.filter(isStress).length;
    let score;
    const crosses = nc!==Infinity && (p.total + Math.round(sum) >= nc);
    if(crosses){
      const excess = Math.max(0, Math.round(sum) - lim);
      const heatCost = excess>0 ? excess + (cornerHasOverheat(nc)?1:0) : 0;
      const reserve = p.engine - prof.heatFloor;
      const leftover = p.engine - heatCost;             // engine after paying this corner
      if(heatCost > p.engine)              score = -100 - heatCost - stressN*8;               // would spin: avoid — and if unavoidable, prefer the smallest deterministic overshoot (a Stress flip could land a 4)
      else if(heatCost > Math.max(0,reserve)) score = sum - heatCost*3 - (prof.riskTolerance<1?18:4);
      else                                 score = sum - heatCost*(2-prof.aggression);
      score -= Math.max(0, prof.heatFloor - leftover)*3;// keep Heat banked for the corner after this one
      score -= stressN*(2*(1-prof.aggression)+1);      // random stress into a braking zone is scary
      /* skill: a Stress card is a 1-4 gamble, not its 2.5 average. When the
         Engine is thin the WORST case is what decides whether the car spins. */
      if(prof.skill){
        const wsum  = combo.reduce((s,c)=>s + (isStress(c) ? 4 : val(c)), 0);
        const wExc  = Math.max(0, Math.round(wsum) - lim);
        const wCost = wExc>0 ? wExc + (cornerHasOverheat(nc)?1:0) : 0;
        if(wCost > p.engine) score -= 14*prof.skill*(1+stressN);
        score -= stressN*(1 + 2*prof.skill*Math.max(0, 1 - p.engine/3));
      }
    } else {
      score = sum + stressN*0.6;                        // straight: run hard, happily shed stress
    }
    score += sum*prof.aggression*0.3;                   // overall push
    if(score>bestScore){ bestScore=score; best=combo; }
  }
  return best || playable.slice(0,g);
}

/* 5 · react: Cooldown to refill the Engine before the corner, then maybe Boost / adrenaline */
function simReact(p, prof){
  const sec = curSector(p), wSec = isWeatherSector(sec);
  const wDef = G.weather ? G.weather.def.sector : {};
  const noCool   = wSec && !!wDef.noCool;
  const coolBonus= (wSec && wDef.cool) ? wDef.cool : 0;
  const heatCtrl = sectorTokOf(sec)==="heatctrl";
  const upgCool = p.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.cool||0),0);
  let cool = (classOf(p).cool[p.gear]||0) + coolBonus + upgCool + (p.adrenaline?1:0);
  if(noCool) cool=0;
  // Reduce Stress symbols: bots always shed what they can — unless they're in
  // a tunnel, where discarding from hand is barred (Cooldown below stays legal)
  let shed = inTunnelNow(p) ? 0 : p.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.stress||0),0);
  while(shed>0){
    const i=p.hand.findIndex(isStress); if(i<0) break;
    p.discard.push(p.hand.splice(i,1)[0]); shed--;
  }
  while(cool>0 && p.engine<heatCapFor(p)){                  // top the Engine up (Heat pays corners in step 7)
    const idx = p.hand.findIndex(isHeat); if(idx<0) break;
    p.hand.splice(idx,1); p.engine++; cool--; auditHeat(p,"bot Cooldown");
  }
  // Super Cool symbols (Air Intake): Heat comes back from the DISCARD — always worth it
  let sc = p.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.supercool||0),0), scMoved=0;
  while(sc>0 && p.engine<heatCapFor(p)){
    const i=p.discard.findIndex(isHeat); if(i<0) break;
    p.discard.splice(i,1); p.engine++; sc--; scMoved++; auditHeat(p,"bot reclaim");
  }
  if(scMoved) log(`${p.name} Super Cools ${scMoved} Heat out of the discard.`);
  // Boost — only when it can't spin the car out. A Boost adds a random 1–4 to
  // the round speed, which also raises the Check-Corner bill for EVERY corner
  // line crossed this turn — including ones already crossed back in step 3. The
  // old check only looked at the corner ahead, so a bot could Boost after
  // already clipping a corner, owe more Heat than its Engine held, and spin out
  // (then respawn before the same corner and do it again — stuck forever).
  // Now we test all four possible flips against the Heat left after paying for
  // the Boost, exactly like the human's Boost gate, and refuse if any flip spins.
  if(!p.boostUsed && Math.random()<prof.boostChance){
    const engineAfter = p.engine - (heatCtrl?0:1);        // Heat left to cover corners after the Boost's own cost
    let worstSpin = false;
    for(let v=1; v<=4; v++){
      const nc   = crossingsForMove(p.total, v);           // corners this Boost's extra move would cross
      const bill = cornerHeatCost(p.speed+v, [...p.crossings, ...nc], p.limitAdj||0);
      if(bill > engineAfter){ worstSpin = true; break; }   // some flip can't be paid → skip the Boost
    }
    const canPay = heatCtrl || p.engine>prof.heatFloor+1;   // still keep the profile's Heat reserve
    if(canPay && engineAfter>=0 && !worstSpin){
      if(!heatCtrl) payHeat(p,1);
      p.boostUsed=true;
      const r=flipForSpeed(p); if(r.card) p.discard.push(r.card);
      p.speed+=r.v; const m=moveCar(p,r.v);
      log(`${p.name} fires the Boost: +${r.v}${m<r.v?" (blocked)":""}.`);
    }
  }
  // adrenaline extra space — skip if the extra speed would owe more corner Heat
  // than the Engine holds (counting corners already crossed this turn, not just
  // the one ahead), so the free space can't quietly spin the bot out either.
  if(p.adrenaline && !p.adrSpeedUsed){
    const nc   = crossingsForMove(p.total, 1);
    const bill = cornerHeatCost(p.speed+1, [...p.crossings, ...nc], p.limitAdj||0);
    if(bill <= p.engine){ p.adrSpeedUsed=true; p.speed+=1; moveCar(p,1); }
  }
  renderGauges();
}

/* 5b · Draft (Front Wing): take the longest legal draft that doesn't
   push the corner bill past the Engine. No speed is added, so the check
   runs at the bot's existing round speed. */
function simDraft(p){
  const pool = p.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.draft||0),0) - (p.draftUsed||0);
  if(pool<=0) return;
  const opts = draftTargets(p, pool);
  for(const d of opts.slice().sort((a,b)=>b-a)){       // longest draft first
    const nc  = crossingsForMove(p.total, d);
    const bill= cornerHeatCost(p.speed, [...p.crossings, ...nc], p.limitAdj||0);
    if(bill<=p.engine){
      doDraft(p,d);
      log(`${p.name} drafts +${d} space${d>1?"s":""} up to the car ahead.`);
      break;
    }
  }
}

/* 6 · slipstream when eligible and it won't cause an unaffordable corner.
   ≫≫ Extra Slipstream (Sliding Skirts): each symbol on a played card lets the
   bot chain one more full slipstream if the previous one ended in a new
   slipstream position — bonuses re-evaluated at the new spot each time. */
function simSlip(p, prof){
  let xleft = p.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.xslip||0),0);
  let chain = false;
  while(true){
    const here=phys(p.total), ahead=phys(p.total+1);
    const eligible = carsAt(here,p).length>0 || carsAt(ahead,p).length>0;
    if(!eligible) return;
    if(chain && xleft<1) return;
    const sec=curSector(p), wSec=isWeatherSector(sec), wDef=G.weather?G.weather.def.sector:{};
    let bonus=0, blocked=false;
    bonus += p.play.filter(isUpg).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.def.slip||0),0);
    if(sectorTokOf(sec)==="slip") bonus+=1;
    if(wSec){
      if(wDef.slip) bonus+=wDef.slip;
      if(wDef.noSlip) blocked=true;
      if(wDef.wind){ const cls=windClassAt(sec); if(cls==="head") bonus+=2; else if(cls==="tail") blocked=true; }
    }
    if(blocked) return;
    const dist=classOf(p).slipBase+bonus;
    const wouldFinish=(p.total<raceDist())&&(p.total+dist>=raceDist());
    if(wouldFinish||finishedLine(p)) return;              // can't slip across / after the flag
    const newCross=crossingsForMove(p.total,dist);
    const addCost=cornerHeatCost(p.speed,[...p.crossings,...newCross],p.limitAdj||0) - cornerHeatCost(p.speed,p.crossings,p.limitAdj||0);
    if(addCost>p.engine) return;   // cannot pay the corner bill — never take a certain spin
    if(chain) xleft--;
    const nb=p.crossings.length;
    const m=moveCar(p,dist);
    if(G.champ) for(const ct of p.crossings.slice(nb))
      if(isPressLine(ct)) (p.pressPending||(p.pressPending=new Set())).add(phys(ct));
    if(m>0) log(`${p.name} slipstreams ${m} space${m>1?"s":""}${chain?" (Extra Slipstream)":""}.`);
    if(m<=0 || xleft<1) return;                           // blocked / no symbols → stop chaining
    chain=true;
  }
}

/* 8 · discard: light cycling — bin a dead 0-upgrade, and (if aggressive) a spare 1 */
function simDiscard(p, prof){
  if(inTunnelNow(p)) return;   // tunnel: no discarding from hand
  const opts=p.hand.filter(discardable);
  if(!opts.length) return;
  const toGo=[];
  // a "dead zero" is a 0-value card with no symbols worth holding (Coast; the
  // classic up0). Quick Fix / Lean In / Grease Monkey keep their utility symbols.
  const deadZero = c => c.t==="up0" ||
    (isUpg(c) && !c.def.v && !c.def.choice && !c.def.plus &&
     !c.def.salvage && !c.def.limit && !c.def.cool && !c.def.slip && !c.def.stress &&
     !c.def.refresh && !c.def.direct && !c.def.draft && !c.def.supercool && !c.def.accel && !c.def.xslip);
  for(const c of opts) if(deadZero(c)) toGo.push(c);
  if(prof.aggression>0.6){
    // Never bin low cards while the Engine can't buy the car out of a tight
    // corner ahead — a 1 in hand can be the only clean way through a limit-1/2
    // apex, and shedding it is exactly how Pro/Legend rivals used to strand
    // themselves spinning on the same Corner Line forever.
    const nc = nextCornerTotal(p.total);
    const tightAhead = nc!==Infinity && (limitAt(nc)+(p.limitAdj||0)) <= 2 && p.engine < 2;
    if(!tightAhead){
      const ones=opts.filter(c=>c.t==="speed"&&c.v===1&&!toGo.includes(c));
      if(ones.length>1) toGo.push(ones[0]);
    }
  }
  if(!toGo.length) return;
  p.hand=p.hand.filter(c=>!toGo.includes(c));
  p.discard.push(...toGo);
}

/* player finished earlier: keep simulating bot rounds till race complete */
async function runBotsOnly(){
  waitingPanel(G.hotseat?"All local drivers are home — the remaining rivals race to the flag.":"You're home — the remaining rivals race to the flag.");
  const racers=G.players.filter(p=>!p.finished);
  if(racers.length===0){ raceOver(); return; }
  racers.sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total||a.spot-b.spot);
  G.legendRevealed=false;
  for(const p of racers){ pointTurn(p); await (p.sim ? simBotTurn(p) : botTurn(p)); }
  endRound();
}

/* ---------------- round end / finish */
/* Finishing order (per the rules):
     1. an earlier round always beats a later one;
     2. cars that take the flag in the SAME round are ranked by how far past the
        finish line they travelled that round — furthest ahead finishes first,
        NOT whoever happened to move first in turn order;
     3. side by side on the same space, the spot closest to the racing line wins.
   (A car may not slipstream across, or after, the finish line — so slipstream
   can never buy a place here. That is already enforced in slipOffer/botSlip.)
   The list is re-sorted on every finish so standings, the podium bays and the
   championship points all read from the same, correct order. */
function sortFinishOrder(){
  G.finishOrder.sort((a,b)=>
      ((a.dq?1:0) - (b.dq?1:0))         // black-flagged cars always classify last
   || (a.finishRound - b.finishRound)   // earlier round first
   || (b.total - a.total)               // then furthest past the flag
   || (a.spot - b.spot));               // then closest to the racing line
}
function checkFinish(p){
  if(!p.finished && finishedLine(p)){
    p.finished=true; p.finishRound=G.round;
    G.finishOrder.push(p);
    sortFinishOrder();
    const past = p.total - raceDist();                     // spaces beyond the line
    const shared = G.finishOrder.some(q=>q!==p && q.finishRound===p.finishRound);
    const tag = shared ? ` — ${past} past the line, P${G.finishOrder.indexOf(p)+1}` : "";
    log(`${p.isBot||G.hotseat?p.name:"YOU"} take${p.isBot||G.hotseat?"s":""} the chequered flag!${tag}`, p.isBot?"":"me");
    if(!p.isBot) toast(G.hotseat?`🏁 ${p.name} — chequered flag!`:"🏁 Chequered flag!");
    renderCars();
    renderStandings();
  }
}
function endRound(){
  pushReplaySnap();                 // record where every car ended this round
  renderStandings();
  if(G.mode==="career" && G.career && typeof careerLapCheck==="function") careerLapCheck();
  const stillRacing=G.players.filter(p=>!p.finished);
  if(stillRacing.length===0){ raceOver(); return; }
  if(humans().every(h=>h.finished)){
    // every local driver is home — let the remaining rivals race to the flag
    setTimeout(()=>{ G.round++; updateRoundLabel(); runBotsOnly(); }, 500);
    return;
  }
  startRound();
}
function raceOver(){
  pushReplaySnap();
  if(window.HeatAudio) window.HeatAudio.win();   // stop race loop, play win sting once
  sortFinishOrder();   // (defensive) same-round finishers ranked by distance past the flag
  const order=[...G.finishOrder,
               ...G.players.filter(p=>!p.finished).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total||a.spot-b.spot)];
  if(G.mode==="champ" && G.champ){ champRaceOver(order); return; }
  if(G.mode==="career" && G.career){ careerRaceOver(order); return; }
  const rows=order.map((p,i)=>`
    <div class="standrow"><div class="p">P${i+1}</div>
      <div class="dot" style="background:${p.color}"></div>
      <div>${p.isBot||G.hotseat?p.name:"No. 17 — You"}</div>
      <div style="margin-left:auto;color:var(--cream-dim);font-size:12px">${p.finishRound?("R"+p.finishRound):"DNF"}</div>
    </div>`).join("");
  const winner=order[0];
  const youP = order.indexOf(curHuman())+1;
  const h2 = G.hotseat
    ? (winner.isBot ? "Race over" : `🏆 ${winner.name} wins!`)
    : (youP===1 ? "🏆 Victory!" : "Race over");
  const tag = G.hotseat
    ? `${TRACK.name} · ${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""} · pass &amp; play`
    : `${TRACK.name} · ${G.laps} lap${G.laps>1?"s":""} · you finished P${youP}`;
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex";
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
    <h2>${h2}</h2>
    <div class="tag">${tag}</div>
    ${rows}
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:18px">
      ${replayBtnHTML()}
      <button class="act" id="goAgain">↻ Race again — same setup</button>
      <button class="act secondary" id="goNew">New game — change setup</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  wireReplayBtn();
  $("#goAgain").onclick=restartRace;
  $("#goNew").onclick=newGame;
}

/* ---------------- heat helper */
function payHeat(p,n){
  const pay=Math.min(n,p.engine);
  p.engine-=pay;
  for(let i=0;i<pay;i++) p.discard.push(makeCard("heat"));
  auditHeat(p,"pay heat");
  renderGauges();
  return pay;
}

/* ---------------- rendering: cards, gauges, standings */
/* ---------------- card artwork
   Scans live under assets/cards/:
     upgrades/id1.png … id61.png       Garage upgrade cards (keyed by def.id)
     starter/zero|one|…|five.png       Speed 1–4 + Starting Upgrade 0/5
     starter/startheat.png             Extra Heat (upgrade) card
     starter/heat.png, stress.png      Heat & Stress cards
     sponser/…                         Sponsorship cards
     events/…, weather/…               Event & Weather cards (briefing modal)
   Every card tries a shortlist of likely filenames; the first that loads wins.
   Missing files leave the existing vector face untouched (rule 1: SVG/CSS art
   is the fallback), and load results are cached so a missing file only 404s
   once per session. */
const CARD_ART_DIR = "assets/cards/";
const _artCache = new Map();                      // url -> "ok" | "bad" | Promise
const artSlug = s => String(s).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,"_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g,"");
function cardArtCandidates(c){
  const S=CARD_ART_DIR, num=["zero","one","two","three","four","five"];
  switch(c.t){
    case "speed": return num[c.v] ? [S+"starter/"+num[c.v]+".png"] : [];
    case "up0":   return [S+"starter/zero.png"];
    case "up5":   return [S+"starter/five.png"];
    case "upheat":return [S+"starter/startheat.png"];
    case "heat":  return [S+"starter/heat.png"];
    case "stress":return [S+"starter/stress.png"];
    case "upg":{
      const d=c.def||{}; if(d.id===undefined) return [];
      if(d.sponsor) return [S+"sponser/id"+d.id+".png", S+"sponser/"+d.id+".png",
                            S+"sponser/"+artSlug(d.name)+".png"];
      return [S+"upgrades/id"+d.id+".png", S+"upgrades/"+d.id+".png"];
    }
  }
  return [];
}
function eventArtCandidates(ev){
  const S=CARD_ART_DIR+"events/", i=EVENT_CARDS.indexOf(ev);
  return [S+ev.key+".png", ...(i>=0?[S+"id"+(i+1)+".png", S+(i+1)+".png"]:[]),
          S+artSlug(ev.name)+".png"];
}
function weatherArtCandidates(key){
  const S=CARD_ART_DIR+"weather/", def=WEATHER_TYPES[key]||{};
  return [S+key+".png", S+artSlug(def.name||key)+".png"];
}
function loadArt(url){
  const hit=_artCache.get(url);
  if(hit==="ok")  return Promise.resolve(true);
  if(hit==="bad") return Promise.resolve(false);
  if(hit) return hit;                              // in-flight probe
  const p=new Promise(res=>{
    const im=new Image();
    im.onload =()=>{ _artCache.set(url,"ok");  res(true);  };
    im.onerror=()=>{ _artCache.set(url,"bad"); res(false); };
    im.src=url;
  });
  _artCache.set(url,p);
  return p;
}
async function firstArt(cands){
  for(const u of cands) if(await loadArt(u)) return u;
  return null;
}
function attachCardArt(el, c){
  const cands=cardArtCandidates(c); if(!cands.length) return;
  const apply=url=>{
    const im=document.createElement("img");
    im.className="cart"; im.alt=""; im.draggable=false; im.src=url;
    el.prepend(im); el.classList.add("hasart");
  };
  const known=cands.find(u=>_artCache.get(u)==="ok");
  if(known){ apply(known); return; }                // no flicker on cached hits
  firstArt(cands).then(url=>{ if(url) apply(url); });
}

function renderCard(c){
  const d=cardLabel(c);
  const el=document.createElement("div");
  el.className="card "+d.cls;
  if(c.t==="upg"){
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="cv" style="font-size:${String(d.cv).length>3?18:24}px">${d.cv}</div>
      <div class="ct">${d.ct}</div>
      <div class="chips">${upgChips(c.def)}</div>`;
    attachCardArt(el, c);
    attachCardHelp(el, c);
    return el;
  }
  // Cards stay clean: no "→ drew N" on stress cards, no "from stress" on the drawn speed card.
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="cv">${d.cv}</div><div class="ct">${d.ct}</div>`;
  attachCardArt(el, c);
  attachCardHelp(el, c);
  return el;
}

/* ---------------- draw pile (play-area widget + deck-info sheet)
   Shows the face-down draw deck with a live count. Because a Stress ＋ (and a
   Boost) flips off the top of this deck until an original 1–4 Speed card shows
   up, seeing what's left — and the resulting odds — is exactly the info the
   physical player has by knowing their own deck. */
const DRAW_ART = CARD_ART_DIR + "starter/draw.png";
function deckStats(p){
  const D=p.deck||[], Dis=p.discard||[];
  const sp=[0,0,0,0,0];                       // speed 1–4 counts in the draw deck
  D.forEach(c=>{ if(isSpeed14(c)) sp[c.v]++; });
  const speedTotal=sp[1]+sp[2]+sp[3]+sp[4];
  const heat   = D.filter(isHeat).length;
  const stress = D.filter(isStress).length;
  const upg    = D.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&!(c.def&&c.def.sponsor)).length;
  const spon   = D.filter(c=>isUpg(c)&&c.def&&c.def.sponsor).length;
  const up0    = D.filter(c=>c.t==="up0").length;
  const up5    = D.filter(c=>c.t==="up5").length;
  // Stress/Boost flip: the first 1–4 Speed card reached is a uniformly random
  // one among the deck's Speed cards. If the deck holds none, the discard would
  // reshuffle in — so fall back to the discard's Speed cards for the odds.
  let pool=sp.slice(), poolTotal=speedTotal;
  if(poolTotal===0){ Dis.forEach(c=>{ if(isSpeed14(c)) pool[c.v]++; }); poolTotal=pool[1]+pool[2]+pool[3]+pool[4]; }
  const ev = poolTotal ? (pool[1]+pool[2]*2+pool[3]*3+pool[4]*4)/poolTotal : 0;
  return {total:D.length, discard:Dis.length, sp, speedTotal, heat, stress, upg, spon, up0, up5,
          flip:{pool, poolTotal, ev, fromDiscard:speedTotal===0&&poolTotal>0}};
}
/* a face-down pile element for the play area; tap → showDeckSheet */
function drawPileEl(p){
  p = p || curHuman();
  const el=document.createElement("div");
  el.className="drawpile";
  el.title="Draw deck — tap to see what's left";
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="dp-face">Draw<br>Deck</div><div class="dp-cnt">${p.deck.length}</div>`;
  loadArt(DRAW_ART).then(ok=>{
    if(!ok) return;                            // keep the vector face if the scan is missing
    const im=document.createElement("img");
    im.className="dp-art"; im.alt=""; im.draggable=false; im.src=DRAW_ART;
    el.prepend(im);
  });
  el.onclick=()=>showDeckSheet(p);
  return el;
}
/* a pile + one-line odds hint, dropped into the play area above the hand */
function drawPileSlotHTML(){ return `<div id="drawpileslot" class="pileslot"></div>`; }
function mountDrawPile(p){
  const slot=$("#drawpileslot"); if(!slot) return;
  p = p || curHuman();
  if(!p || !p.deck) return;
  const s=deckStats(p);
  slot.innerHTML="";
  slot.appendChild(drawPileEl(p));
  const hint=document.createElement("div");
  hint.className="pilehint";
  hint.innerHTML = s.flip.poolTotal
    ? `<b>${p.deck.length}</b> in the draw deck${s.discard?` · ${s.discard} in discard`:""}.<br>`+
      `A <b>Stress ＋</b>/Boost flip would average <span class="ev">${s.flip.ev.toFixed(1)}</span>`+
      `${s.flip.fromDiscard?" (from a reshuffle)":""}. Tap the pile for the full odds.`
    : `<b>${p.deck.length}</b> in the draw deck. No Speed cards left to flip — a Stress ＋/Boost would come up <b>0</b>. Tap for details.`;
  slot.appendChild(hint);
}
function showDeckSheet(p){
  p = p || curHuman();
  if(!p || !p.deck) return;                    // no driver yet (e.g. tapped before a race) — nothing to show
  const s=deckStats(p);
  const el=$("#setup"); el.style.display="flex"; el.onclick=null;
  const row=(label,val)=>`<div class="dkrow"><span>${label}</span><b>${val}</b></div>`;
  const odds = s.flip.poolTotal
    ? [1,2,3,4].map(v=>{
        const c=s.flip.pool[v], pct=Math.round(100*c/s.flip.poolTotal);
        return `<div class="oddrow"><span>Speed ${v}</span><div class="oddbar"><i style="width:${pct}%"></i></div><b>${pct}%</b></div>`;
      }).join("")
    : `<div class="phase-hint" style="margin:0">No Speed cards remain to be flipped — a Stress ＋ or Boost would resolve to 0.</div>`;
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="sheet">
    <h2>Draw deck</h2>
    <div class="tag">${s.total} card${s.total===1?"":"s"} face-down${s.discard?` · ${s.discard} in discard`:""}</div>
    <div class="dklist">
      ${s.speedTotal?row("Speed 1–4", s.speedTotal):""}
      ${[1,2,3,4].map(v=>s.sp[v]?row(`&nbsp;&nbsp;&middot; Speed ${v}`, s.sp[v]):"").join("")}
      ${s.up5?row("Speed 5 (Upgrade)", s.up5):""}
      ${s.up0?row("Speed 0 (Upgrade)", s.up0):""}
      ${s.upg?row("Upgrade cards", s.upg):""}
      ${s.spon?row("🤝 Sponsorship", s.spon):""}
      ${s.stress?row("Stress ＋", s.stress):""}
      ${s.heat?row("Heat 🔥 (stuck in deck)", s.heat):""}
      ${s.total===0?`<div class="phase-hint" style="margin:0">The draw deck is empty — it reshuffles from your discard when you next need a card.</div>`:""}
    </div>
    <div class="olbl" style="margin:16px 0 4px">If you play a Stress ＋ or Boost</div>
    <div class="phase-hint" style="margin:0 0 10px">It flips off the top until a Speed 1–4 appears. Given what's left${s.flip.fromDiscard?" (deck empty of Speed, so from a reshuffle of your discard)":""}, the value lands:</div>
    <div class="odds">${odds}</div>
    ${s.flip.poolTotal?`<div class="dkev">Average flip &asymp; <b>${s.flip.ev.toFixed(1)}</b></div>`:""}
    <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:18px"><button class="act" id="dkclose">Close</button></div>
  </div>`;
  const close=()=>{ el.style.display="none"; el.innerHTML=""; el.onclick=null; };
  $("#dkclose").onclick=close;
  el.onclick=e=>{ if(e.target===el) close(); };
}

/* ---------------- in-context card help (hold on touch, hover on desktop)
   Every rendered card explains itself — especially the Garage Upgrade
   cards whose symbols aren't obvious. Long-press peeks at the help without
   selecting the card; hovering with a mouse shows it after a short delay. */
function cardHelp(c){
  switch(c.t){
