    rot.appendChild(el("circle",{cx,cy,r,fill:"#f0e7d3",stroke:"#221d2b","stroke-width":.8}));
    const t=el("text",{x:cx,y:cy+fs*0.35,"text-anchor":"middle","font-size":String(fs),
      "font-weight":"800",fill:"#14101a"});
    t.textContent=numTxt; rot.appendChild(t);
  };

  rot.appendChild(el("ellipse",{cx:1,cy:2,rx:16,ry:9,fill:"#000",opacity:.25}));            // shadow

  switch(classOf(p).key){

  /* ---- Warlord V8 — armoured muscle: ram bar, roof, twin stacks, fat rubber */
  case "v8": {
    for(const [wx,wy,ww,wh] of [[-11,-9,8,5.4],[-11,3.6,8,5.4],[5,-8.6,7,5],[5,3.6,7,5]])
      rot.appendChild(tyre(wx,wy,ww,wh));                                                   // fat tyres
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-11,y:-6,width:22,height:12,fill:"#3a3644"}));             // chassis rails
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M14 -6.6 L14 6.6 L16.6 5 L16.6 -5 Z",
      fill:"#5b5566",stroke:"#14101a","stroke-width":.9}));                                 // ram bar
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:12.4,y:-1.6,width:2.4,height:3.2,fill:"#5b5566"}));        // ram spine
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M13.6 -5.4 L13.6 5.4 L4 6.6 L-9 6.6 L-14.5 5 L-14.5 -5 L-9 -6.6 L4 -6.6 Z",
      fill:p.color, stroke:outline, "stroke-width":ow, "stroke-linejoin":"round"}));        // slab body
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M13.6 -1.6 L13.6 1.6 L-14.5 1.6 L-14.5 -1.6 Z",
      fill:"#f0e7d3",opacity:.85}));                                                        // stripe
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-4.4,y:-5,width:8.4,height:10,rx:1.2,
      fill:"#221d2b",stroke:"#14101a","stroke-width":.7,opacity:.95}));                     // armoured roof
    rot.appendChild(el("circle",{cx:0.4,cy:0,r:2.1,fill:helmet,stroke:"#221d2b","stroke-width":.7}));
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:4.6,y:-4.6,width:2.6,height:2.2,rx:.6,fill:"#2c2a33"}));   // blower stacks
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:4.6,y:2.4,width:2.6,height:2.2,rx:.6,fill:"#2c2a33"}));
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:7.6,y:-2.4,width:4,height:4.8,rx:.8,
      fill:"#2c2a33",stroke:"#14101a","stroke-width":.6}));                                 // supercharger
    roundel(-9.6,0,4,6);                                                                    // number on the boot
    break;
  }

  /* ---- Dust Viper — inline bike: two wheels, a leaning rider, no bodywork */
  case "bike": {
    rot.appendChild(tyre(-14.5,-2.6,6.4,5.2));                                              // rear wheel
    rot.appendChild(tyre(9,-2.4,6,4.8));                                                    // front wheel
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M-9 0 L-2 -0.4 L6 -0.6 L11.5 -1.2",
      fill:"none",stroke:"#3a3644","stroke-width":2.6,"stroke-linecap":"round"}));          // frame spine
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M14.5 0 L9.5 -3.2 L1 -3.6 L-4 -2.6 L-9.5 -3.4 L-9.5 3.4 L-4 2.6 L1 3.6 L9.5 3.2 Z",
      fill:p.color, stroke:outline, "stroke-width":ow, "stroke-linejoin":"round"}));        // Engine + fairing
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M14.5 0 L9 -1 L-9.5 -1 L-9.5 1 L9 1 Z",fill:"#f0e7d3",opacity:.9}));
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:6.4,y:-5.6,width:1.6,height:11.2,rx:.7,fill:"#2c2a33"}));  // handlebars
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M-6.4 -4.4 L-1 -3 L-1 3 L-6.4 4.4 Z",
      fill:"#221d2b",opacity:.92}));                                                        // rider's back
    rot.appendChild(el("circle",{cx:-0.6,cy:0,r:2.3,fill:helmet,stroke:"#221d2b","stroke-width":.7}));
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-12.6,y:-4.6,width:3.4,height:1.6,rx:.6,fill:"#2c2a33"}));  // exhaust
    roundel(-6.8,0,3.1,4.6);                                                                // plate on the tail
    break;
  }

  /* ---- Privateer — open tube frame, knobbly tyres, engine hanging out the back */
  case "buggy": {
    for(const [wx,wy,ww,wh] of [[-12.5,-9.4,7.4,5.6],[-12.5,3.8,7.4,5.6],[6.5,-8.8,6.6,5.2],[6.5,3.6,6.6,5.2]])
      rot.appendChild(tyre(wx,wy,ww,wh));                                                   // knobbly tyres
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-9.6,y:-6.4,width:3,height:12.8,fill:"#3a3644"}));         // rear axle
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:8,y:-5.8,width:2.6,height:11.6,fill:"#3a3644"}));          // front axle
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M15 0 L9 -3.4 L2 -4.6 L-8 -4.6 L-12.5 -3.4 L-12.5 3.4 L-8 4.6 L2 4.6 L9 3.4 Z",
      fill:p.color, stroke:outline, "stroke-width":ow, "stroke-linejoin":"round"}));        // pan
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M15 0 L9 -1.2 L-12.5 -1.2 L-12.5 1.2 L9 1.2 Z",fill:"#f0e7d3",opacity:.9}));
    // roll cage: two hoops and a spine
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-4.6,y:-4.6,width:1.5,height:9.2,rx:.6,
      fill:"none",stroke:"#d9d2bf","stroke-width":1.1,opacity:.9}));
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:2.4,y:-4.2,width:1.5,height:8.4,rx:.6,
      fill:"none",stroke:"#d9d2bf","stroke-width":1.1,opacity:.9}));
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M-4 -4.2 L3 -3.8 M-4 4.2 L3 3.8",
      fill:"none",stroke:"#d9d2bf","stroke-width":1,opacity:.85}));
    rot.appendChild(el("circle",{cx:-0.8,cy:0,r:2.2,fill:helmet,stroke:"#221d2b","stroke-width":.7}));
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-9.4,y:-3.4,width:5,height:6.8,rx:1,
      fill:"#2c2a33",stroke:"#14101a","stroke-width":.7}));                                 // exposed lump
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-13.4,y:-1,width:3.2,height:2,rx:.7,fill:"#5b5566"}));      // straight pipe
    roundel(7,0,3.6,5.4);                                                                   // number on the nose
    break;
  }

  /* ---- Circuit Ghost — the pre-war open-wheeler, exactly as it always was */
  default: {
    for(const [wx,wy,ww,wh] of [[-10,-8.2,7,4.6],[-10,3.6,7,4.6],[6,-7.6,5.6,4.2],[6,3.4,5.6,4.2]])
      rot.appendChild(tyre(wx,wy,ww,wh));                                                   // tyres
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:7,y:-5,width:3,height:10,fill:"#3a3644"}));                // front axle
    rot.appendChild(el("rect",{x:-9.5,y:-5.4,width:4,height:10.8,fill:"#3a3644"}));         // rear axle
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M16.5 0 L11.5 -2.7 L7.5 -3.3 L3.5 -5.2 L-5.5 -5.2 L-10.5 -3.9 L-14 -4.8 L-14 4.8 L-10.5 3.9 L-5.5 5.2 L3.5 5.2 L7.5 3.3 L11.5 2.7 Z",
      fill:p.color, stroke:outline, "stroke-width":ow, "stroke-linejoin":"round"}));        // body
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M16.5 0 L11 -1.4 L-14 -1.4 L-14 1.4 L11 1.4 Z",
      fill:"#f0e7d3",opacity:.92}));                                                        // centre stripe
    rot.appendChild(el("path",{d:"M-1 -2.6 L4.5 -1.6 L4.5 1.6 L-1 2.6 Z",fill:"#221d2b",opacity:.9})); // cockpit
    rot.appendChild(el("circle",{cx:1.2,cy:0,r:2.1,fill:helmet,stroke:"#221d2b","stroke-width":.7}));  // helmet
    rot.appendChild(el("circle",{cx:-4,cy:0,r:1.4,fill:"#221d2b"}));                        // intake
    roundel(9.8,0,4.4,6.2);                                                                 // nose roundel
  }
  }
  g.appendChild(rot);
  return rot;
}
/* the same car, drawn standalone as card art (car select, summary sheets) —
   so the model you pick is literally the model you'll see on the track */
function classCarSVG(clsKey, color, num){
  const NS="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  const svg=document.createElementNS(NS,"svg");
  svg.setAttribute("viewBox","-19 -12 39 24");
  svg.setAttribute("width","100%"); svg.setAttribute("height","100%");
  const g=document.createElementNS(NS,"g");
  carSpriteInto(g, {color:color||"#8a5cf6", isBot:false, carNum:num||"17", cls:clsKey, name:""}, NS);
  svg.appendChild(g);
  return `<div class="dk-model">${svg.outerHTML}</div>`;
}

/* parking bays on the Hall of Fame podium, by finishing position */
function podiumSlot(k){
  const pd=PODIUM||{x:TRACK.imgW-110,y:TRACK.imgH-90};
  if(k===0) return {x:pd.x,    y:pd.y-24};
  if(k===1) return {x:pd.x-42, y:pd.y-14};
  if(k===2) return {x:pd.x+42, y:pd.y-13};
  const r=k-3;
  return {x:pd.x-45+(r%4)*30, y:pd.y+30+Math.floor(r/4)*22};
}
/* ============================================================
   SMOOTH CAR MOTION
   Cars no longer teleport between spaces: each car keeps a *visual*
   position (a fractional space count) that chases its true game
   position along the traced track centreline, space by space, at a
   racing pace. Play 9 speed and you watch the car drive through
   0-1-2-3-4-5-6-7 and pull up on 8. Knock-backs (spin-outs, blocked
   moves) reverse the car with a quick 360° spin. Finished cars cruise
   over the line, then peel off and park on the Hall of Fame podium.
   ============================================================ */
const ANIM={raf:0,last:0};
const REPLAY={active:false,raf:0,last:0,t:0,speed:1,rps:0.85};   // rps = rounds per second at 1×
const REDUCED_MOTION = window.matchMedia && matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
const spotOffOf = s => s===0? -9 : 9;      // racing line = inner printed lane
const CAR_REV=14, CAR_TELEPORT=40;         // reverse speed · snap threshold (restarts/grid resets)

/* pose at a fractional space count: lerp position/normal/tangent between
   the two neighbouring traced points, then push out to the lane offset */
function carPose(totF, off){
  const i=Math.floor(totF), f=totF-i;
  const a=PT(phys(i)), b=PT(phys(i+1));
  const nx=a.nx+(b.nx-a.nx)*f, ny=a.ny+(b.ny-a.ny)*f;
  const tx=a.tx+(b.tx-a.tx)*f, ty=a.ty+(b.ty-a.ty)*f;
  return {x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*f + nx*off, y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*f + ny*off,
          ang:Math.atan2(ty,tx)*180/Math.PI};
}
function applyCarTransform(p,x,y,targ,scale){
  const g=document.getElementById("car-"+p.id); if(!g) return;
  // unwrap the heading so the sprite always steers the short way round
  if(p._ang==null) p._ang=targ;
  else{ let d=(targ-p._ang)%360; if(d>180)d-=360; if(d<-180)d+=360; p._ang+=d; }
  g.setAttribute("transform",`translate(${x.toFixed(2)},${y.toFixed(2)})`);
  const rot=(p._rot&&p._rot.isConnected)?p._rot:g.querySelector(".car-rot");
  if(rot) rot.setAttribute("transform",`rotate(${p._ang.toFixed(1)}) scale(${scale.toFixed(3)})`);
}
function snapCarVis(p){
  p._v={total:p.total, off:spotOffOf(p.spot), blend:p.finished?1:0, spin:0, pfrom:null, _tgt:null, _top:0};
}
/* advance one car's visual state by dt seconds; returns true while still moving */
function stepCar(p,dt){
  if(!PTS.length || !document.getElementById("car-"+p.id)) return false;
  if(!p._v) snapCarVis(p);
  const v=p._v, baseScale=p.isBot? .92 : 1;
  const T=p.total, O=spotOffOf(p.spot);
  if(REDUCED_MOTION || Math.abs(T-v.total)>CAR_TELEPORT){       // restarts, grid resets
    v.total=T; v.off=O; v.spin=0; v.pfrom=null; if(p.finished) v.blend=1;
  }
  // lateral drift into the target lane
  v.off += (O-v.off)*Math.min(1, dt*7);
  if(Math.abs(O-v.off)<.15) v.off=O;
  // longitudinal chase: cruise pace scales with the length of the move,
  // with an ease-out so the car visibly brakes into its landing space
  if(v.total<T){
    if(v._tgt!==T) { v._tgt=T; v._top=Math.max(v._top||0, Math.min(15, 5+(T-v.total)*0.5)); }
    const rem=T-v.total;
    v.total=Math.min(T, v.total + Math.min(v._top, 2.4+rem*3.4)*dt);
    v.revving=false;
  }else if(v.total>T){
    if(!v.revving){ v.revving=true; v.spin=360; }               // knocked back — spin the car
    v.total=Math.max(T, v.total - CAR_REV*dt);
  }else{ v.revving=false; v._top=0; }
  if(v.spin>0) v.spin=Math.max(0, v.spin - dt*560);
  const arrived = v.total===T;
  let moving = !arrived || v.off!==O || v.spin>0;

  if(p.finished && arrived){
    // over the line: peel off and park in the Hall of Fame bay
    if(!v.pfrom) v.pfrom=carPose(v.total,v.off);
    v.blend=Math.min(1,(v.blend||0)+dt*1.1);
    const e=v.blend*v.blend*(3-2*v.blend);
    const k=Math.max(0,G.finishOrder.indexOf(p)), sl=podiumSlot(k);
    let d=((-90 - v.pfrom.ang)%360+540)%360-180;
    applyCarTransform(p, v.pfrom.x+(sl.x-v.pfrom.x)*e, v.pfrom.y+(sl.y-v.pfrom.y)*e,
                      v.pfrom.ang+d*e, baseScale*(1-0.15*e));
    moving = v.blend<1;
  }else{
    v.pfrom=null; v.blend=0;
    const ps=carPose(v.total, v.off);
    applyCarTransform(p, ps.x, ps.y, ps.ang + v.spin, baseScale);
  }
  if(!moving && p._settle){ const w=p._settle; p._settle=null; w.forEach(r=>r()); }
  return moving;
}
/* promise that resolves once a car has visually caught up with its true
   position — used to pace bot turns and corner checks with the animation */
function carSettle(p, maxMs=3000){
  const v=p._v;
  if(!v || (v.total===p.total && Math.abs(v.off-spotOffOf(p.spot))<.2 && (!p.finished || v.blend>=1)))
    return Promise.resolve();
  return new Promise(res=>{
    (p._settle||(p._settle=[])).push(res);
    setTimeout(res, maxMs);               // safety net: never stall the game
    animKick();
  });
}
function animKick(){
  if(REPLAY.active) return;               // the replay drives the cars itself
  if(!ANIM.raf){ ANIM.last=performance.now(); ANIM.raf=requestAnimationFrame(animStep); }
}
function animStep(now){
  ANIM.raf=0;
  if(REPLAY.active) return;
  const dt=Math.min(.06, Math.max(0,(now-ANIM.last)/1000)); ANIM.last=now;
  let busy=false;
  if(typeof G!=="undefined" && G.players) for(const p of G.players){ if(stepCar(p,dt)) busy=true; }
  if(busy) ANIM.raf=requestAnimationFrame(animStep);
}
function renderCars(opts){
  const NS="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  for(const p of G.players){
    let g=document.getElementById("car-"+p.id);
    if(!g){
      g=document.createElementNS(NS,"g"); g.setAttribute("id","car-"+p.id); g.setAttribute("class","car");
      p._rot = carSpriteInto(g,p,NS);
      carLayer.appendChild(g);
      p._ang=null;
      snapCarVis(p);
    }
    g.style.opacity="1";
    if(opts && opts.snap){ snapCarVis(p); p._ang=null; }
  }
  if(REPLAY.active) return;
  let busy=false;
  for(const p of G.players){ if(stepCar(p,0)) busy=true; }      // apply poses immediately
  if(busy) animKick();
}

/* ============================================================
   RACE REPLAY
   Every round the game stores a snapshot of where every car sits.
   After the chequered flag you can watch the whole race back as one
   continuous flow — every car rolling from lights-out to the flag
   with no card play, no pauses, just the racing.
   ============================================================ */
function pushReplaySnap(){
  if(!G.replay || !G.players.length) return;
  const snap=G.players.map(p=>({id:p.id, total:p.total, spot:p.spot, fin:!!p.finished}));
  const key=snap.map(s=>s.total+":"+s.spot).join("|");
  if(G.replay.length && G.replay._k===key) return;              // nobody moved — skip
  G.replay._k=key;
  G.replay.push(snap);
}
function replayBtnHTML(){
  return (G.replay && G.replay.length>1)
    ? `<button class="act secondary" id="watchReplay">🎬 Watch race replay</button>` : "";
}
function wireReplayBtn(){ const b=$("#watchReplay"); if(b) b.onclick=startReplay; }
function startReplay(){
  if(!G.replay || G.replay.length<2 || REPLAY.active) return;
  REPLAY.active=true; REPLAY.t=0; REPLAY.speed=1;
  if(ANIM.raf){ cancelAnimationFrame(ANIM.raf); ANIM.raf=0; }
  $("#setup").style.display="none";                             // results sheet waits behind the replay
  clearHighlights(); if(ptrEl) ptrEl.style.opacity="0";
  for(const p of G.players) p._ang=null;                        // headings snap cleanly to the grid
  buildReplayHUD();
  if(window.HeatAudio && window.HeatAudio.race) window.HeatAudio.race();   // race loop back on
  REPLAY.last=performance.now();
  REPLAY.raf=requestAnimationFrame(replayStep);
}
function replayStep(now){
  if(!REPLAY.active) return;
  const dt=Math.min(.06, Math.max(0,(now-REPLAY.last)/1000)); REPLAY.last=now;
  const R=G.replay, N=R.length-1;
  REPLAY.t=Math.min(N, REPLAY.t + dt*REPLAY.speed*REPLAY.rps);
  const i=Math.min(N-1, Math.floor(REPLAY.t)), f=Math.min(1, REPLAY.t - i);
  const A=R[i], B=R[i+1];
  for(let k=0;k<G.players.length;k++){
    const p=G.players[k];
    const a=(A[k]&&A[k].id===p.id)?A[k]:A.find(s=>s.id===p.id);
    const b=(B[k]&&B[k].id===p.id)?B[k]:B.find(s=>s.id===p.id);
    if(!a||!b) continue;
    const tot=a.total+(b.total-a.total)*f;
    const off=spotOffOf(a.spot)+(spotOffOf(b.spot)-spotOffOf(a.spot))*f;
    const ps=carPose(tot,off);
    applyCarTransform(p, ps.x, ps.y, ps.ang, p.isBot? .92 : 1);
  }
  const lbl=$("#rp_round"); if(lbl) lbl.textContent=`Round ${Math.min(N,Math.floor(REPLAY.t)+1)} / ${N}`;
  if(REPLAY.t>=N){ endReplay(); return; }
  REPLAY.raf=requestAnimationFrame(replayStep);
}
function endReplay(){
  if(!REPLAY.active) return;
  REPLAY.active=false;
  cancelAnimationFrame(REPLAY.raf); REPLAY.raf=0;
  const hud=$("#replayhud"); if(hud) hud.remove();
  // hand the cars back to the live engine — finishers cruise off to the podium
  const last=G.replay[G.replay.length-1];
  G.players.forEach((p,k)=>{
    const s=(last[k]&&last[k].id===p.id)?last[k]:last.find(x=>x.id===p.id);
    if(s) p._v={total:s.total, off:spotOffOf(s.spot), blend:0, spin:0, pfrom:null, _tgt:null, _top:0};
  });
  if(window.HeatAudio && window.HeatAudio.win){ try{ window.HeatAudio.win(); }catch(e){} }
  renderCars();
  $("#setup").style.display="flex";
}
function buildReplayHUD(){
  let hud=$("#replayhud"); if(hud) hud.remove();
  hud=document.createElement("div"); hud.id="replayhud";
  hud.innerHTML=`
    <span class="rp-title">🎬 RACE REPLAY</span>
    <span id="rp_round">Round 1 / ${G.replay.length-1}</span>
    <span class="rp-speeds">
      <button data-s="1" class="on">1×</button><button data-s="2">2×</button><button data-s="4">4×</button>
    </span>
    <button id="rp_exit">⏹ Skip</button>`;
  document.body.appendChild(hud);
  hud.querySelectorAll(".rp-speeds button").forEach(b=>{
    b.onclick=()=>{ REPLAY.speed=+b.dataset.s;
      hud.querySelectorAll(".rp-speeds button").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("on",x===b)); };
  });
  $("#rp_exit").onclick=endReplay;
}
function pointTurn(p){
  if(!ptrEl) return;
  if(p.finished){ ptrEl.style.opacity="0"; return; }
  const {x,y}=spotXY(p.total,p.spot);
  ptrEl.style.opacity="1";
  ptrEl.setAttribute("transform",`translate(${x},${y-24})`);
}

/* ============================================================
   WEATHER & ROAD CONDITIONS MODULE  (advanced rules)
   - Road-condition tokens: one drawn per corner. Corner-type tokens
     modify that corner; sector-type tokens modify the road AFTER it.
   - Weather deck uses the "refined weather effects" set: each weather
     applies a car-setup modifier before the race, may bar certain
     road-condition token types, and adds an effect on any sector that
     carries a "Weather" road-condition token.
   ============================================================ */

/* the 12 road-condition tokens (base module) */
const RC_POOL = ['lim+1','lim+1','lim-1','lim-1','overheat','overheat',
                 'slip','slip','heatctrl','heatctrl','weather','weather'];
const RC_DEF = {
  'lim+1':   {kind:'corner', name:'Speed Limit +1', short:'+1'},
  'lim-1':   {kind:'corner', name:'Speed Limit −1', short:'−1'},
  'overheat':{kind:'corner', name:'Overheat',       short:'🔥'},
  'slip':    {kind:'sector', name:'Slipstream boost',short:'≫'},
  'heatctrl':{kind:'sector', name:'Heat control',    short:'H'},
  'weather': {kind:'sector', name:'Weather',         short:'☁'}
};

/* refined weather deck.  setup: car-setup applied before the race.
     heatCap  = heat added(+)/removed(−) from the game (pool size)
     stress   = stress cards added(+)/removed(−) from the deck
     engineToDiscard / engineToDeck = reposition heat (pool unchanged)
   exclude: road-condition token types this weather bars.
   sector : effect applied on any sector carrying a Weather token. */
const WEATHER_TYPES = {
  extreme_heat:{ name:'Extreme Heat', icon:'🌡',
    setup:{engineToDiscard:3}, exclude:['heatctrl'], sector:{noCool:true},
    blurb:'3 Heat from Engine →discard. Heat-control tokens barred. No cooling on weather sectors.' },
  sunny:{ name:'Sunny', icon:'🌤',
    setup:{stress:-1}, sector:{slip:2},
    blurb:'Remove 1 Stress from your deck. Weather sectors give slipstream +2.' },
  cloudy:{ name:'Cloudy', icon:'☁',
    setup:{}, sector:{slip:1},
    blurb:'No card changes. Weather sectors give slipstream +1.' },
  cold:{ name:'Cold', icon:'❄',
    setup:{heatCap:-1}, sector:{cool:1},
    blurb:'Remove 1 Heat from the Engine (out of the game). Weather sectors: +1 Cooldown.' },
  mist:{ name:'Mist', icon:'🌫',
    setup:{heatCap:1}, exclude:['lim+1'], sector:{noSlip:true},
    blurb:'Add 1 Heat to the Engine. No +1-limit tokens. No slipstreaming FROM weather sectors.' },
  light_rain:{ name:'Light Rain', icon:'🌦',
    setup:{engineToDeck:3}, exclude:['lim+1','slip'], sector:{cool:1},
    blurb:'Move 3 Heat →draw deck. No +1-limit / slipstream tokens. Weather sectors: +1 Cooldown.' },
  heavy_rain:{ name:'Heavy Rain', icon:'🌧',
    setup:{stress:1}, exclude:['lim+1','slip'], sector:{aqua:true},
    blurb:'Add 1 Stress. No +1-limit / slipstream tokens. Aquaplaning on weather sectors: over speed 10 into a corner, flip — a Stress card spins you out.' },
  strong_wind:{ name:'Strong Wind', icon:'🎐',
    setup:{engineToDeck:3}, sector:{wind:true},
    blurb:'Move 3 Heat →draw deck. Weather sectors carry a head- or tail-wind (±1 corner speed, alters the slipstream) by direction.' },
  wind_gusts:{ name:'Wind Gusts', icon:'💨',
    setup:{stress:1}, sector:{gust:true},
    blurb:'Add 1 Stress. At corners bordering a weather sector, flip a card at the corner check — a Speed card adds to your corner speed.' },
  low_sun:{ name:'Low Sun', icon:'🌅',
    setup:{stress:1}, sector:{lowSun:true},
    blurb:'Add 1 Stress. On weather sectors you must play a Stress card if you hold one (blinded — no brake points).' },
  wet_asphalt:{ name:'Wet Asphalt', icon:'🌊',
    setup:{heatCap:1, stress:1}, sector:{wetShift:true},
    blurb:'Add 1 Heat to the engine + 1 Stess. On weather sectors, shifting DOWN costs +1 Heat per gear.' },
  thunderstorm:{ name:'Thunderstorm', icon:'🌩',
    setup:{stress:1}, exclude:['lim+1','slip'], sector:{aqua:true, gust:true, storm:true},
    blurb:'Add 1 Stress. No +1-limit / slipstream tokens. Weather sectors bring both aquaplaning and wind gusts. Three weather spin-offs red-flag the race.' },
  none:{ name:'Dry / No Weather', icon:'🏁', setup:{}, sector:{},
    blurb:'Plain race — no weather or road-condition tokens in play.' }
};

/* prepare weather state + car-setup pool modifiers (runs BEFORE the car is built) */
function setupWeather(key){
  const def = WEATHER_TYPES[key] || WEATHER_TYPES.none;
  G.weather = { key, def, cornerTok:[], sectorTok:[], sectorOfSpace:[],
                windClass:null, wind:null, weatherSpins:0, aborted:false };
  WEATHER_HEAT_MOD   = def.setup.heatCap || 0;
  WEATHER_STRESS_MOD = def.setup.stress  || 0;
}

/* car-setup that just repositions Heat cards (pool size already handled above) */
function applyWeatherCarSetup(you){
  const s = G.weather.def.setup;
  if(s.engineToDiscard){
    const k=Math.min(s.engineToDiscard, you.engine); you.engine-=k;
    for(let i=0;i<k;i++) you.discard.push(makeCard("heat"));
  }
  if(s.engineToDeck){
    const k=Math.min(s.engineToDeck, you.engine); you.engine-=k;
    for(let i=0;i<k;i++) you.deck.push(makeCard("heat"));
    shuffle(you.deck);
  }
  auditHeat(you,"weather setup");
}

/* Draw one road-condition token per corner — but a CHICANE counts as a single
   corner for this: it draws one token, and that token covers both of its Corner
   Lines. A corner-type token is stamped onto every line in the chicane; a
   sector-type token lands on the sector running on from the chicane's LAST line
   (the road after the chicane), never on the short stretch inside it. */
function rollRoadConditions(){
  const def=G.weather.def, n=TRACK.corners.length;
  G.weather.cornerTok=new Array(n).fill(null);
  G.weather.sectorTok=new Array(n).fill(null);
  if(G.weather.key!=='none'){
    let pool=RC_POOL.filter(t=>!(def.exclude||[]).includes(t));
    shuffle(pool);
    for(const grp of tokenGroups()){
      const tok = pool.length ? pool.pop() : null;
      if(!tok) continue;
      if(RC_DEF[tok].kind==='corner') for(const i of grp) G.weather.cornerTok[i]=tok;
      else                            G.weather.sectorTok[grp[grp.length-1]]=tok;
    }
  }
  buildSectorMap();       // maps every space to its sector; classifies wind if needed
  drawRoadConditions();   // paint the overlay
}

/* each corner "owns" the sector of road from itself up to the next corner */
function buildSectorMap(){
  const cs=TRACK.corners, n=cs.length;
  const map=new Array(S).fill(-1);
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=cs[i].at, b=cs[(i+1)%n].at;
    let sp=a;
    for(let g=0; g<S; g++){ map[sp]=i; sp=nextSpace(sp); if(sp<0 || sp===b) break; }
  }
  for(let sp=0;sp<S;sp++) if(map[sp]===-1) map[sp]=n-1;   // safety
  G.weather.sectorOfSpace=map;
  if(G.weather.def.sector.wind) classifyWind();
}

/* Strong Wind: a windsock direction (base "up", rotated 90° clockwise per pip
   on the first legend card's diamond) is compared with each sector's travel
   direction — aligned = tailwind, opposed = headwind, else sidewind.
   Digital adaptation of the physical windsock/board orientation. */
function classifyWind(){
  const top = G.legendDeck && G.legendDeck.length ? G.legendDeck[G.legendDeck.length-1] : null;
  const d = ((top?top.d:0)%4+4)%4;
  const dirs=[[0,-1],[1,0],[0,1],[-1,0]];   // up,right,down,left (image coords, y down)
  const w=dirs[d]; G.weather.wind=w;
  const n=TRACK.corners.length;
  const cls=new Array(n).fill('side');
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    let ax=0,ay=0,c=0;
    for(let sp=0;sp<S;sp++) if(G.weather.sectorOfSpace[sp]===i){ ax+=PTS[sp].tx; ay+=PTS[sp].ty; c++; }
    if(c){ const L=Math.hypot(ax,ay)||1; ax/=L; ay/=L;
      const dot=ax*w[0]+ay*w[1];
      cls[i]= dot>0.5 ? 'tail' : dot<-0.5 ? 'head' : 'side';
    }
  }
  G.weather.windClass=cls;
}

/* ---- sector lookups ---- */
function curSector(p){
  if(!G.weather || !G.weather.sectorOfSpace.length) return -1;
  return G.weather.sectorOfSpace[phys(p.total)];
}
function sectorTokOf(sec){
  return (sec>=0 && G.weather && G.weather.sectorTok) ? G.weather.sectorTok[sec] : null;
}
function isWeatherSector(sec){ return sectorTokOf(sec)==='weather'; }
function windClassAt(sec){
  return (G.weather && G.weather.windClass && sec>=0) ? (G.weather.windClass[sec]||'side') : 'side';
}
function sectorBefore(cornerTotal){
  const before=prevSpace(phys(cornerTotal));
  return G.weather.sectorOfSpace[before<0 ? 0 : before];   // run-up onto an open board
}
function approachIsWeatherSector(cornerTotal){ return isWeatherSector(sectorBefore(cornerTotal)); }
function cornerBordersWeatherSector(idx){
  const n=TRACK.corners.length;
  const after  = G.weather.sectorTok[idx]==='weather';           // sector after this corner
  const before = G.weather.sectorTok[(idx-1+n)%n]==='weather';   // sector before this corner
  return after || before;
}

/* ---- shift-gear cost (Wet Asphalt down-shift penalty folds with the 2-gear cost) ---- */
function weatherShiftPenaltyHere(p){
  return isWeatherSector(curSector(p)) && !!(G.weather && G.weather.def.sector.wetShift);
}
function shiftHeatCost(fromGear, toGear, wet){
  const two  = Math.abs(toGear-fromGear)===2 ? 1 : 0;   // normal 2-gear shift = 1 Heat
  const down = Math.max(0, fromGear-toGear);
  return (wet && down>0) ? Math.max(two, down) : two;   // down1 wet=1, down2 wet=2
}

/* ---- thunderstorm red flag: three weather-induced spin-offs stop the race ---- */
function registerWeatherSpin(induced){
  if(!induced || !G.weather || !G.weather.def.sector.storm) return;
  G.weather.weatherSpins++;
  if(G.weather.weatherSpins>=3 && !G.weather.aborted){
    G.weather.aborted=true;
    log("🚩 RED FLAG — three cars have spun off in the storm. The race is stopped.","warn");
    toast("🚩 RED FLAG",true);
  }
}

/* ---- bots: only aquaplaning & wind gusts touch Legends (they never cool / slipstream /
   pay Heat), and in a simplified probabilistic form noted for the player. ---- */
function botWeatherCheck(p, fromTotal, moveDist){
  if(!G.weather) return;
  const wDef=G.weather.def.sector;
  if(!(wDef.aqua||wDef.gust)) return;
  for(const ct of cornerTotals()){
    if(fromTotal < ct && p.total >= ct){
      const idx=cornerIdxOf(ct);
      if(wDef.aqua && moveDist>10 && approachIsWeatherSector(ct) && Math.random()<0.20){ botSpin(p,ct,"aquaplanes"); return; }
      if(wDef.gust && cornerBordersWeatherSector(idx) && Math.random()<0.15){ botSpin(p,ct,"is caught by a gust"); return; }
    }
  }
}
function botSpin(p, ct, tag){
  let t=ct-1; while(spaceFull(phys(t),p)) t--;
  p.total=t; takeSpot(p); renderCars();
  log(`${p.name} ${tag} and spins off before the corner!`,"warn");
  registerWeatherSpin(true);
}

/* ---- UI ---- */
/* Weather used to be a banner pasted over the top-right of the board, which ate a
   good chunk of the racing surface. It's now a small chip in the header beside the
   lap/round line; the full detail still lives on the board's weather panel and in
   the race log. */
function updateWeatherNote(){
  const el=$("#wchip"); if(!el) return;
  if(!G.weather || G.weather.key==='none'){ el.textContent=""; el.hidden=true; drawWeatherPanel(); return; }
  const w=G.weather.def;
  // a chicane's single token is stamped on both of its corner lines — count it once
  const n = tokenGroups().filter(g =>
    G.weather.cornerTok[g[0]] || G.weather.sectorTok[g[g.length-1]]).length;
  el.textContent = `${w.icon} ${w.name}${n?` · ${n} token${n===1?"":"s"}`:""}`;
  el.title = w.blurb;
  el.hidden = false;
  drawWeatherPanel();
}
function logWeatherSetup(){
  const w=G.weather;
  log(`Weather — ${w.def.name}. ${w.def.blurb}`,"me");
  if(w.key!=='none'){
    const parts=[];
    for(const grp of tokenGroups()){
      const head=grp[0], tail=grp[grp.length-1], lbl=groupLabel(grp);
      if(w.cornerTok[head])
        parts.push(`${lbl}: ${RC_DEF[w.cornerTok[head]].name}${grp.length>1?" (both lines)":""}`);
      if(w.sectorTok[tail]) parts.push(`Sector after ${lbl}: ${RC_DEF[w.sectorTok[tail]].name}`);
    }
    if(parts.length) log("Road conditions — "+parts.join(" · "));
    if(w.def.sector.wind && w.windClass){
      const names=w.windClass.map((c,i)=> w.sectorTok[i]==='weather'?`S${i+1}:${c}wind`:null).filter(Boolean);
      if(names.length) log("Wind on weather sectors — "+names.join(", "));
    }
  }
}

/* overlay: tinted bands for sector tokens, offset badges for corner tokens */
function drawRoadConditions(){
  if(!rcLayer) return;
  while(rcLayer.firstChild) rcLayer.removeChild(rcLayer.firstChild);
  drawCornerBadges();   // corner tokens live in the corner values now — repaint them first
  const w=G.weather; if(!w || w.key==='none') return;
  const NS="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  const mk=(nn,at)=>{ const e=document.createElementNS(NS,nn); for(const k in at) e.setAttribute(k,at[k]); return e; };
  const n=TRACK.corners.length;
  // a token badge with a bright halo so it stays legible on top of the busy
  // satellite board photo as well as the flat art board.
  const badge=(cx,cy,r,col,txt,fs)=>{
    rcLayer.appendChild(mk("circle",{cx, cy, r:r+2.5, fill:"#f5efe0", opacity:.95})); // halo
    rcLayer.appendChild(mk("circle",{cx, cy, r, fill:col, opacity:1, stroke:"#12100c", "stroke-width":2}));
    const t=mk("text",{x:cx, y:cy+fs*0.34, "text-anchor":"middle", "font-size":fs, "font-weight":"800", fill:"#12100c"});
    t.textContent=txt; rcLayer.appendChild(t);
  };
  // sector tokens (bands)
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const tok=w.sectorTok[i]; if(!tok) continue;
    // Gather this sector's spaces IN TRACK ORDER. A sector can wrap across the
    // start/finish line (e.g. corner 4 → corner 1), so collecting by ascending
    // index would split the run into two chunks and the path below would draw a
    // straight line across the infield joining them. Instead, find where the run
    // begins (a space whose previous space is outside the sector) and walk
    // forward around the loop, so the band follows the road from one corner to
    // the next and simply stops at each end.
    const set=new Set(); for(let sp=0;sp<S;sp++) if(w.sectorOfSpace[sp]===i) set.add(sp);
    if(!set.size) continue;
    let start=-1;
    for(const sp of set){ const pv=prevSpace(sp); if(pv<0 || !set.has(pv)){ start=sp; break; } }
    if(start<0) start=set.values().next().value;   // full-loop sector: any start works
    const ordered=[]; let cur=start;
    for(let g=0; g<S && cur>=0 && set.has(cur); g++){ ordered.push(cur); cur=nextSpace(cur); }
    const pts=ordered.map(sp=>PTS[sp]);
    if(!pts.length) continue;
    const col = tok==='weather' ? '#3fa7d6' : tok==='slip' ? '#6fce6f' : '#e3c34a';
    const d = "M "+pts.map(p=>p.x.toFixed(1)+" "+p.y.toFixed(1)).join(" L ");
    // dark under-stroke widens the band's edge for contrast, then the colour on top
    rcLayer.appendChild(mk("path",{d, fill:"none", stroke:"#12100c", "stroke-width":40,
      opacity:.28, "stroke-linecap":"round", "stroke-linejoin":"round"}));
    rcLayer.appendChild(mk("path",{d, fill:"none", stroke:col, "stroke-width":34,
      opacity:.42, "stroke-linecap":"round", "stroke-linejoin":"round"}));
    // the band is the token: mark it at the head of the sector (just after the
    // corner it belongs to) and again mid-run, so it reads at a glance
    const head=pts[Math.min(1, pts.length-1)];
    badge(head.x, head.y, 12, col, RC_DEF[tok].short, 13);
    if(pts.length>4){
      const mid=pts[Math.floor(pts.length/2)];
      badge(mid.x, mid.y, 9, col, RC_DEF[tok].short, 10);
    }
  }
  // corner tokens are NOT drawn as separate pieces any more — they're folded
  // straight into the corner's speed-limit badge (see drawCornerBadges), so the
  // number on the board is always the number you actually race to.
  drawWeatherPanel();   // panel reflects the weather now in play
}

/* ---------------- boot */
buildTrack();
renderCars({snap:true});
$("#menubtn").onclick=openMenu;
applyAudioPrefs();                 // push saved volume/mute into HeatAudio if it's ready…
setTimeout(applyAudioPrefs, 800);  // …and once more in case the host audio loads late
showModeSelect();
